# Inventory — everything the recipes modify and parameterize

Generated from the platform catalog (1734 items). Each item points to the **Latent Atlas** chapter that explains the concept behind it. The browsable version (search and filters) is at `index.html?lang=en#/catalogo`; the full research inventories, with file:line references and measured numbers, are in `docs/pesquisa/` (Portuguese).

## GLM-5.3-Flash EXL3 (vLLM)

### Modifications (patches, overlays, kernels, hotfixes, checkpoint transforms)

#### API / serving

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `patch_default_max_new_tokens.py` | overlay #22; DEFAULT_MAX_NEW_TOKENS | Pydantic before-validator that applies the max_tokens default only when the field is omitted. · Changes only the get_max_tokens() fallback when the request omits max_tokens, preserving the omission in the Pydantic before-validator. | Prevents unbounded generations that grow the KV and preempt other sessions. · Distinguishes omission from explicit values (16 or null). | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `patch_xgrammar_termination.py (backport vLLM #52805)` | always; commit 12f64b39 | accept_tokens() and validate_tokens() stop at the first terminal token, ignore advances after termination and clear the flag on reset. | Prevents Failed to advance FSM when combining structured outputs with spec decode. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `patch_xgrammar_termination.py (backport vLLM #53046)` | always; commit c6e19b3 | Validates drafts produced before an end of reasoning in the middle of the window before advancing the new grammar. | Prevents Failed to advance FSM when the end of reasoning falls inside a speculative block. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |

#### Abliteration

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ablit/fetch_transplant.py` | ~2.7 GiB via range requests · python3 ablit/fetch_transplant.py | Fetches only the o_proj tensors from the donor checkpoint. · Reads the donor's safetensors header and downloads, via HTTP range requests, only the bytes of …layers.{L}.self_attn.o_proj.weight (~2.7 GiB), checking SHA-256 against MANIFEST.json. | o_proj is native BF16 and RowParallelLinear only slices the input: the edit is done per rank, with no collectives. · Avoids downloading 164 GiB; the BF16 o_proj is byte-identical between NVFP4 and EXL3, so the transplant reproduces the published edit exactly. | 19 Abliteration, 24 Checkpoints |
| `ablit_runtime.py` | ABLIT=1 | At load time, applies the proj method (W' = W − α · r (rᵀW)/‖r‖²) or the transplant to the o_proj of layers 15–45; since o_proj is row-parallel, the edit is identical on every rank with no collectives; on TP3 the donor is padded along the input dimension. | Per-layer fidelity: mean rel_l2 ≈ 0.126 (L44 0.737 vs 0.74 published); KLD with ablit has not been measured. | 19 Abliteration, 38 Parallelism |
| `patch_ablit.py` | applied at start (last in the order) | Copies the runtime to models/glm5next/nvidia/glm53_ablit.py and injects _glm53_ablit_maybe_apply(self) at the end of Glm5NextModel.load_weights (layers 0–44) and of Glm5NextMTP.load_weights (block 45). | The edit happens before graph capture, so the served model already uses the edited weights. | 19 Abliteration, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `patch_ablit.py (+ ablit_runtime.py)` | overlay #25 at 94ae731 (#26 since #203); no-op if ABLIT≠1 | Hook at the end of Glm5NextModel.load_weights / Glm5NextMTP.load_weights that applies abliteration. | Edits the weights per rank at load time, without rewriting the checkpoint. | 19 Abliteration |

#### Attention / kernels

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `buffer_width = topk_tokens` | build patch | Removes the '+ (kpool-1)' from the indexer buffer width. | The SM120 kernel only instantiates top-k 2048. | 13 Sparse attention, 28 Kernels |
| `expandir select_k-1 pools` | build patch | Expands 511 pools (2044 tokens) + 3 tail tokens = 2047 candidates. | The indexer emitted up to 2051 candidates. 4 of 2048 (0.2%) are lost — the lowest-ranked ones — and the recent tail is kept. | 13 Sparse attention |
| `flashinfer_mla_sparse_sm120.py (bind-mount TP3)` | TP3 overlay | Pads Q from 22→32 heads in decode calls and slices back. | SM120 decode only instantiates local head counts {8,16,32,64,128}. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 28 Kernels |
| `flashinfer_mla_sparse_sm120.py vendorizado (TP3: pad Q 22 → 32)` | TP3 | Pads Q from 22 to 32 local heads at every call size and slices the output back. | SM120 decode only instantiates local heads {8,16,32,64,128} and prefill rejected 22 (Unsupported sparse-MLA prefill configuration … num_heads=22); softmax is per head and the extra heads have zero Q. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 28 Kernels |
| `NoPE → geometria GLM_NSA (pad 512→576)` | build patch | rope_pad=64 when qk_rope_head_dim==0; q = F.pad(q,(0,64)); kernel_qk_rope_head_dim=64; do_kv_cache_update writes a zeroed k_pe. | The stock image dies on the 1st forward pass ('pe_dim must be 64'). It is exact (a zero RoPE block does not change QK) and costs +24% of DSA KV. | 12 MLA & compact KV, 28 Kernels |
| `persistent_topk desligado` | always (if False and …) | Turns off decode persistent_topk and uses top_k_per_row_decode. | persistent_topk exceeds the GB10's shared memory (SMEM) on long sequences. | 13 Sparse attention, 28 Kernels |
| `rope_pad=64 (NoPE MLA na geometria GLM_NSA 576)` | inline patch in the Dockerfile; always active when qk_rope_head_dim == 0 | Pads q with 64 zeros (F.pad) and writes a zero k_pe to the cache, wrapping the NoPE MLA (d_qk 512) in FlashInfer SM120's 576 GLM_NSA geometry. | Without it the first forward pass dies with pe_dim must be 64 for fp8_ds_mla. It is exact (the zero RoPE block changes neither QKᵀ nor the softmax); it costs 656 B/token vs ~528 (~24% more KV on the 11 DSA layers), with no new kernel. | 12 MLA & compact KV, 28 Kernels |
| `supports_dense_mha_prefill = False` | declared in the SM120 backend | Routes prefill through the same sparse top-k MQA kernel instead of the dense forward_mha. | The inherited forward_mha raises NotImplementedError; this way prefill keeps the model's own sparse semantics. | 13 Sparse attention, 12 MLA & compact KV |
| `supports_dense_mha_prefill=False` | build patch | Makes prefill go through the same top-k MQA kernel instead of forward_mha. | The implementation inherited forward_mha, which raises NotImplementedError. | 13 Sparse attention, 28 Kernels |
| `top-k como capacidade (return_valid_counts)` | inline patch in the Dockerfile | Calls triton_convert_req_index_to_global_index(..., return_valid_counts=True), passes seq_lens = topk_lengths and the buffer width as max_seq_len; empty rows point to slot 0 with length 1 and have their output zeroed. | The impl used index_topk both as the table width and as the top-k; with index_kpool = 4 the indexer buffer becomes 2176. | 13 Sparse attention, 28 Kernels |
| `top-k como capacidade + comprimentos válidos` | build patch | triton_convert_req_index_to_global_index(..., return_valid_counts=True); sparse_mla_top_k = capacity; empty rows → slot 0 with length 1 and zeroed output. | index_kpool=4 widened the buffer to 2176: 'expects sparse block_tables shape (256,1,2048), got (256,1,2176)'. | 13 Sparse attention, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `top-k exatamente 2048: expandir select_k - 1 pools + cauda` | inline patch in the Dockerfile | Uses buffer_width = topk_tokens and expand_pools_and_append_tail(pool_ids[:, : select_k - 1], …): 511 pools (2044 tokens) + 3 tail tokens = 2047 ≤ 2048, in prefill and decode. | The SM120 kernel rejects topk ≠ 2048 for GLM_NSA; it gives up the 4/2048 lowest-ranked candidates (0.2%) and preserves the recent tail, which carries more weight in a causal model. | 13 Sparse attention |

| `scripts/logprob_capture.py` | /v1/completions echo, prompt_logprobs=20 | Captures the top-20 prompt logprobs and compares KL and argmax between two servers. | Basis of the quality panels (e.g., dense FP8: KL 0.002–0.013 nats/position, argmax 94–100%). | 25 Measuring quality |

| `scripts/spec-accept-gate.sh` | PASS if pos0 &lt; 0.999 with ≥100 drafts · post-boot check | Detects the vllm#53030 regression (a BatchDescriptor collision in PIECEWISE graphs pins acceptance at 1.00 at position 0). · Checks the drafts' per-position acceptance curve. | A 'perfect' acceptance is a sign of a bug; the gate requires monotonic decay. · The curve should decay; pos0 at exactly 1.00 is the signature of the vllm#53030 bug. | 35 Speculation: fundamentals, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `tests/bench_concurrency.py` | --levels 1,2,4,8,12,16 --modes code,data,chat --ctx 0,50000,100000 --reps 3 · concurrency ladder | Concurrency ladder per mode and context size. · Measures aggregate and per-stream throughput along a concurrency ladder. | 2026-08-31 ladder: code aggregate 41.9 (×1) → 127.2 (×16) tok/s; at ctx 50k, ×8 collapses (hit 0.25–0.875, TTFT p95 99–454 s). · 2026-08-31 ladder (MNBT 2048, 16 seqs): code aggregate 41.9 (×1) → 127.2 (×16). | 45 Benchmarking, 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `tests/bench_decode.py` | --phase structured --structured --runs 5 --max-tokens 400 (and prose) · --phase structured --structured / --phase prose | Measures decode per phase: median of 5×400 tokens. · Per-phase decode benchmark (structured vs prose). | 2026-08-30: structured 65.1 tok/s (acceptance 0.959; 6.71 tokens/step); prose 27.1 (0.341; 2.39). · Structured 65.1 tok/s (acceptance 0.959, 6.71 tokens/step) vs prose 27.1 (0.341, 2.39/step) on 2026-08-30. | 45 Benchmarking |
| `tests/bench_prefix_cache.py` | --runs 3 (with GLM53_EXPOSE_CACHE_RESET=1 on an isolated kit) · cold/warm pairs | Measures cold/warm prefix cache pairs. · Measures prefix cache hits and TTFT on follow-ups. | ~8k: 0/7995 → 7168/836 hits, TTFT 8.53 → 1.30 s; 814,571 tokens: 633.711 s cold vs 2.748 s repeat. · E.g., an ~8k follow-up with 7168 hits out of 8004 and TTFT 1.30 s vs 8.53 s cold. | 45 Benchmarking, 33 Prefix caching |

#### Distributed / network

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `parameter.py (_pad_then_narrow)` | TP3 | Pads before narrowing when the TP pad pushes the end of the shard beyond the dimension stored in the checkpoint. | Without it the loader would try to read past the stored tensor. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 24 Checkpoints |
| `parameter.py / weight_utils.py / vocab_parallel_embedding.py (bind-mount TP3)` | TP3 overlay; validate_loadclone_artifacts | Replace the image's read-only files to support the EP loader and vocab padding. | The launcher checks that the vendored weight_utils still has the loadclone. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 24 Checkpoints |
| `patch_tp3_glm.py` | TP3 overlay (FlyCockpit, MIT) | Head padding 64→66 (22 local), vocab padding_size=lcm(64,3)=192 → 154944, shared I → 2112 or disable_tp, A_log 64→66 at load time. · Head padding 64 → 66 (num_attention_heads, num_key_value_heads, linear_attn_config.num_heads, linear_num_heads), A_log 64 → 66, generic padding of  | 64 heads, vocab 154880, shared I 2048 and A_log 64 are not divisible by 3. Do not pad to 96 ('one rank ends up with only dummy KDA heads and the logits collapse'); do not pad moe_intermediate_size. · TP3 requires divisibility by 3; replicating the shared expert via disable_tp would triple the MLP; the routed experts' moe_intermediate_size | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `shard_exl3_col / shard_exl3_row (_load_exl3)` | always | TP sharding of the EXL3 experts: gate/up column-parallel (trellis dim 1, svh dim 0, suh replicated) and down row-parallel (trellis dim 0, suh dim 0, svh replicated), with global-to-local expert mapping. | One all-reduce per layer sums the halves; experts that are not local are skipped at load time. | 38 Parallelism, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `vocab_parallel_embedding.py / weight_utils.py (TP3)` | TP3 | Vendored versions of the vocab-parallel embedding and the v2 loader for vocabulary and tensor padding in TP3. | Needed so that the embedding and lm_head load with vocab 154,944 split by 3. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |

#### KV cache

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `_glm53_draft_block_size (compact)` | GLM53_DRAFT_KV_COMPACT=1 | Drafter block = largest multiple of 64 that divides the MLA block and fits in the page (896 at block 3584; 640 on TP3 with page 1,679,360 and block 2560), with a preflight check that all SWA layers belong to the DFlash drafter. | Reserved ids −32.78% (180 → 121); repeat TTFT for a 28,672-token prefix with a 64-token tail: 2.623 → 0.302 s; not a measured VRAM reduction. | 31 PagedAttention & KV, 33 Prefix caching |
| `guarda do worker (v1/worker/utils.py)` | always with a padded page | Prevents splitting a padded page into smaller kernel blocks. | The physical stride applies once per manager block, and splitting would cause out-of-bounds access; the backend must support the whole block (FLASH_ATTN declares multiples of 16). | 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `padded slot-share (page_size_padded = mla_page)` | when the MLA page is not an exact multiple of the drafter's | With block_size 64 and a padded page equal to the MLA page, drafter layer i co-owns MLA tensor i on disjoint ids (a view with page stride); with exact division it uses exact-fit. | 656 B and 4096 B do not fit together; the slot-shared drafter adds no bytes. Boot log: padded slot-share block=64 mla_page=2351104. | 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `patch_glm5_drafter_group.py` | overlay #7 · applied at start | Creates an extra SlidingWindowSpec group with 'padded slot-share' (block_size=64, page_size_padded=mla_page, draft layer i co-owns MLA tensor i), an optional compact block and a backend guard. · Accepts the drafter's 5 SlidingWindowSpec layers in the _get_kv_cache_groups_glm5_nex | DFlash2 did not fit in the 656 B MLA page, and inheriting the MLA block made 5 draft layers cost tens of MiB per block. Pool of 1,754,237 tokens at 1M; occupancy at 36k 44.6% → ~16%. · Without it each drafter layer would cost tens of MiB per block and the logged concurrency stayed at ~1×; pool of 1.75M tokens at 1M; occupancy of a ~36k re | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `patch_kpool_tail_seed_stride.py` | overlay #16; backport of vLLM #57477 | Uses TAIL_BLOCK_ELEMS and the real strides when seeding the tail in prefill. | The seed used a dense 2048 B stride, but the real page is 118,272 B at block 3584: it wrote into blocks 0–9 of another request. | 13 Sparse attention, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `patch_kpool_tail_seed_stride.py (backport vLLM #57477)` | applied at start | Addresses the tail as base = blk * TAIL_BLOCK_ELEMS + (t % KPOOL) * HEAD_DIM, passing tail.stride(0)/stride(1); detects an already-upstream fix and rejects a half-applied one. | The kernel treated the tail as dense (2048 B stride), but it is an as_strided view of the indexer page (118,272 B): each prefill left its own tail unseeded and wrote K/gate into another request's indexer block. | 13 Sparse attention, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `patch_kpool_tail_slotmap.py` | overlay #15 · applied at start | block_indices = tl.minimum(block_indices, block_table_stride - 1) in the KpoolTailSpec slot-map. · Clamps block_indices = min(block_indices, block_table_stride - 1) in the Triton slot mapping kernel. | The single circular block indexed beyond the row and corrupted the indexer in ~2k-token generations. · KpoolTail has 1 circular block; for pos ≥ block_size the kernel read garbage and, with ~2k generated tokens, there was a crash or silent corruption of another layer's indexer. | 13 Sparse attention, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `patch_kv_capacity_log.py` | overlay #12 (after drafter-group); log only · GLM53_KV_CAPACITY_LOG=1 | Logs each KV group and a capacity summary. · After the stock line, logs one line per group (type, block_size, page, blocks per request, whether it participates) and a summary: usable ids (num_blocks − 1), ids per segment (38) and capacity 642 // 38 × 3584 = 57,344 tokens. | The 'GPU KV cache size' line was misleading on a hybrid model. · GPU KV cache size: 1,553,140 tokens is max_concurrency × max_model_len; two reviewers read it as 1.5M of prefix cache when it was ~57K tokens of conversation. | 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `patch_mamba_align_state_free.py` | overlay #11; reserves 10 (was 9) · applied at start | Replaces the single last_state_block_idx slot with a list of superseded indices and reserves 1 + max_concurrent_batches + num_speculative_blocks. · Replaces the single last_state_block_idx slot with a per-request stale_state_block_idxs list and changes the reservation from 2 + nu | Under asynchronous scheduling the slot was overwritten and state blocks leaked until the end of the request. · Under asynchronous scheduling, with 7168-token chunks crossing 3584-token blocks, the slot was overwritten before the block became freeable, and the block stayed stuck until the end of the request. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids, 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `páginas de 64 no SM12x (platforms/cuda.py + get_supported_kernel_block_sizes)` | inline patch in the Dockerfile | With capability.major == 12 the indexer alignment becomes index_kpool × 64 = 256 (block 1792, 448 entries) and the backend declares only [64] instead of [64, 256]. | DeepGEMM paged-MQA only accepts block_kv 64 on sm_120; with alignment 128 the block fell to 1664 → 416 entries → page 32 → assert. | 31 PagedAttention & KV, 26 Inside the GPU |

| `exl3_fat_gemm.cu (exl3_fat_gemm / exl3_fat_gemm_scatter)` | E2; EXL3_FAT_KERNEL=1 | 256-thread CTA, 128×128 tile, B K4 dequantized in registers (dq_dispatch MCG), mma m16n8k16 fp16 → fp32; epilogue with a 128-point Hadamard via shuffles × svh; the scatter version multiplies by the routing weight and sums into out[token] without atomics. | +20.33% (8k), +21.36% (100k), +20.70% (300k) prefill (PR77) without reconstructing BF16; restricted to K4 MCG without mul1. | 28 Kernels, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |

| `patch_exl3_decode_pipeline.py (thin-decode FAST)` | GLM53_EXL3_MOE_FAST=1; derived from coolbho3k/GLM-5.3-Flash-NVFP4-DFlash2-2x-DGX-Spark | Creates glm53_exl3_moe_fast_kernel with a shared_input template (skips the redundant up Hadamard) and MOE_FRAG_STAGES 1 / MOE_SH_STAGES 8; dispatch only with K==4, N_off==1, the flag on and SM 12.1. | Stock spills (88 B frame, 39 LDL); FAST uses 127 regs and 0 LDL, layer +8–17%, serving +7.71/+8.88/+14.59%, parity 119/119; numerical study INCONCLUSIVE (0 flips). | 28 Kernels, 26 Inside the GPU |

| `patch_flashkda_tp3.py` | HAREM_KDA_FLASHKDA=1; vLLM PR #55737 · HAREM_KDA_FLASHKDA=1; adapted from NNNtrance (original integration in vLLM PR #55737) | Swaps the Triton-based KDA prefill for FlashKDA (14-argument ABI, FP32 state). · Resolves the KDA prefill backend, validates the 14-argument schema of torch.ops._flashkda_C.fwd, preallocates buffers in the workspace manager (graph-safe) and writes directly into the layer output;  | Part of the combined TP3 profile (+17.9%/+20.8% C1). · Fused chunked-prefill kernel (the same one as Kimi-K3's; the 12.0f cubin runs on sm_121); it only changes prefill; GPU validation of the upstream branch is pending. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `patch_indexer_warmup_range.py` | overlay #24 of 26; also applied at image build; #203 (b356b2c, after the snapshot) | Widens the warmup of the indexer's Triton BuildPrefillChunkMetadataKernel: query_slice_start=WarmupIntRange(0, 2) → (0, 3). The range's stop is exclusive, so (0, 2) only warmed {0, 1} and never Triton's "" integer bucket (neither ==1 nor %16==0) that sub-chunking offsets hit on l | Without it, the first prefill over ~75k tokens after a restart (512 MiB logits budget, index_kpool=4, MNBT=7168) compiled that variant mid-serve, a latency spike jit_monitor reported. After the patch the warmup covers 36 keys, a superset of every variant seen in the cache. It applies to any Triton stack: a specialization the warmup misses | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 13 Sparse attention |
| `patch_sparse_mla_slice.py` | TP4; VLLM_SM120_SPARSE_MLA_SLICE_TOKENS=64 · VLLM_SM120_SPARSE_MLA_SLICE_TOKENS=64 (TP4); from punkjazz-labs | Slices flashinfer_trtllm_batch_decode_with_kv_cache_mla into ≤64 query rows; preimage and result pinned by SHA-256; requires BF16 [T,16,576], pages [64,656], top-k 2048, ≥33,685,504 B of workspace. · Runs flashinfer_trtllm_batch_decode_with_kv_cache_mla in slices of up to 64 quer | All-rank stall (#128/#159) in an H16 call. Qualified on another 4×GB10 kit (153 min soak; max abs diff 0.00185609 vs FP32); it does not fix the race. · Mitigates the 4-rank stall (#128/#159/#223) without fixing the race; 153 min soak, max error 0.00185609 vs the FP32 reference. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 28 Kernels |

#### Launch / topology

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--entrypoint bash IMAGE /start.sh (script interno)` | head: start.sh:2093-2170; worker: 2173-2249 | The internal script assembles ARGS, applies the overlays in order and runs exec vllm serve. | Centralizes the same sequence of patches and flags on both ranks. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `agrupar kpool em 1 camada Docker` | grouped COPY/RUN | Groups the kpool patch steps into a single layer. | With 128 layers, overlay2 could not instantiate the image. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `asserts por inspect.getsource (verificação de build)` | fail-closed | After the build patches, checks the resulting source code with asserts. | A patch that did not take fails the build instead of producing a silently wrong image. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `bind mounts /opt/glm53/*.py:ro` | HF cache (or NFS volume :ro), CACHE_ROOT, Triton, TileLang, ~/.nv/ComputeCache, /start.sh, template, each overlay, ablit/ | Overlays, template and caches are mounted into the container instead of being copied into the image. | The overlays are applied at every start: changing a patch does not require an image rebuild. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `GLM53_* de política (paridade entre ranks)` | forwarded identically to all ranks | The launcher passes exactly the same GLM53_*/EXL3_* knobs to each container. | 'A rank that captures different CUDA graphs hangs NCCL after PIECEWISE'. Kernels, graphs and policies must be identical. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `GLM53_RECIPE_STAMP (LABEL glm53.recipe.stamp)` | SHA-256 of Dockerfile + overlay/ + files/ + tests/ + ablit/ (excludes transplant, nfs-server, nfs-share.sh) | Label on the image's last layer that identifies the recipe code that produced it. | Detects that the code has changed since the image was built. A GHCR pull is adopted only if the published stamp also matches; otherwise it restores the local tag. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `guard de artefatos de overlay` | identity string + exact last line + Python parse | Each mounted overlay must contain its identity string, end on the expected line and parse. | Detects truncated copies or wrong files before boot. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `image key (hash de RootFS.Layers)` | hash of the layers | Image identity compared between head and worker. | The .Id differs between overlay2 and containerd, and docker save\|load loses RepoDigests; the layer hash is stable. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `launcher TP3 (overrides forçados)` | ABLIT=0; EXL3_OVERLAY_HOST cleared; GLM53_KDA_BF16_LARGE_M=0; GLM53_EXL3_MOE_FAST/GLM53_KDA_FP8_FAT unset; removes --kv-cache-memory-bytes from the .env | start-tp3.sh neutralizes TP2 knobs before reading .env.tp3. | '14 GiB is a TP2 number'; the TP2 cooperative overlay would have the wrong ABI. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `overlays assados no build + testes CPU` | same order as at runtime; GLM53_REQUIRE_VLLM=1 in the tests (e.g., test_apc_no_store.py) | The Dockerfile runs the CPU tests and applies all overlays in the same order as at boot. | 'Keeps a fresh build identical to an image patched at boot'; TP3/TP4 inherit the internal defaults when the variable does not reach them. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `precedência do chamador sobre o .env` | KEY=value ./start.sh wins over the .env, including an explicit empty value | The launcher snapshots the exported variables before source .env and re-exports them afterward. Identity variables (HF_HOME MODEL MODEL_REVISION IMAGE PORT TP NNODES) warn when overridden. | Many knobs distinguish 'unset' (default) from 'set to empty' (error or stock) via ${VAR-default} vs ${VAR:-default}; ABLIT is forced to 0 after the .env. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `start.sh (launcher)` | start \| restart \| stop; optional TAIL | Orchestrates preflight → image → download → optional dense-h3 build → pack validation → drafter → sync/NFS → docker run worker (--headless) and head → /health → warmup + canary → READY banner. | ./start.sh restart dropped from 259 to 122 s (/health 230 → 99 s on 2026-09-19): 26 s init/NCCL, 39 s weights, 7 s KV profile, 11 s graphs+warmup, ~10 s API. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `start.sh / start-tp3.sh` | TP2 and TP3 launchers | Launchers that assemble the vllm serve arguments (--quantization, --speculative-config, capture sizes, retention env), reapply the overlays in order and bring up head and worker. | Same order and same environment on both ranks — a precondition for CUDA graphs and NCCL not to diverge. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `validação numérica pré-stop` | MAX_MODEL_LEN ≤1e6, MAX_NUM_SEQS ≤4096, MNBT ≤8,388,608 etc. | Validates the types and ranges of all knobs before stopping the containers. | Typos fail before taking down the healthy pair. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |

| `aviso InstantTensor KV (preflight)` | fires if LOAD_FORMAT=instanttensor, MAX_MODEL_LEN≥850000, GPU_MEM_UTIL≤0.85 and no --kv-cache-memory-bytes | Diagnostic that warns about the KV loss caused by InstantTensor. | It only warns and changes nothing; a reminder to pin the KV pool. | 31 PagedAttention & KV, 27 Unified memory |
| `instanttensor==0.2.0 --no-deps` | installed in the :exl3-instanttensor image | Installs the InstantTensor direct-I/O loader without dependencies. | A normal pip install pulls nvidia-nccl-cu13==2.29.7 on top of the image's NCCL 2.30.7. | 27 Unified memory, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `patch_cold_load_uma.py` | overlay #1; on unless GLM53_COLD_LOAD_UMA=0 · GLM53_COLD_LOAD_UMA=1 | Budgets InstantTensor by MemAvailable (4 GiB buffer + largest shard + 2 GiB) and clones the mmap into anonymous memory when the page size ≠ 4096. · Measures MemAvailable, detects UMA (difference between cuda_free and MemFree under 8 GiB), passes an explicit max_free_mem_usage and | With a full page cache, stock never reaches /health; with the patch, 164 GiB in 36 s @ 4.82 GB/s and /health in 230 s. · On GB10, mem_get_info returns MemFree and the page cache counts as used: after the 164 GiB rsync, InstantTensor aborted; with the patch, 164 GiB in 36 s and /health in 230 s (no gain with a clean cache). | 27 Unified memory |
| `patch_indexer_workspace.py` | overlay #23; GLM53_INDEXER_WORKSPACE · GLM53_INDEXER_WORKSPACE=rightsize | rightsize mode for the indexer workspace and an index_kpool == compress_ratio cross-check in the builder. · In rightsize, entries = min(max_num_seqs, MNBT) × cdiv(max_model_len + num_speculative_tokens, compress_ratio), capped at stock; the builder validates index_kpool == compre | Frees ~5 GiB locked at 1M: ~+26–28% KV. · Stock sized in tokens but indexed in pools: 5,036.40 MiB at 1M → 125.9 MiB (4 seqs) / 503.5 MiB (16 seqs), +4.79 / +4.43 GiB of KV (≈ +26–28%). | 13 Sparse attention, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `patch_loadclone.py (v2)` | overlay #21; GLM53_LOAD_CLONE / GLM53_LOAD_PREFETCH | Clone + bounded prefetch + POSIX_FADV_DONTNEED for mmap-based loading. · Clones auto/lazy mmap tensors into CPU memory before handing them over and reads ahead local shards (1 MiB buffer), applying POSIX_FADV_DONTNEED to the consumed ones. | mmap loading was slow and read-ahead was uncontrolled; not qualified on GPU. · Avoids the hang of H2D copies from mmap on 64 KiB pages and keeps the page cache under control; composes with PR #230 in either order. | 27 Unified memory |
| `patch_skip_cudagraph_profile.py` | overlay #2; active with CG_ESTIMATE=0 · CG_ESTIMATE=0 to skip | Skips the CUDA graph memory dry-capture when estimation is disabled. · Gates profile_cudagraph_memory() on the VLLM_MEMORY_PROFILER_ESTIMATE_CUDAGRAPHS flag. | The result was discarded anyway; the KV profile drops from 18 to 7 s. · The profile always ran but was only applied with the flag: 11 s of boot wasted with CG_ESTIMATE=0. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 31 PagedAttention & KV |

#### MoE

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `apply_exl3_fused_moe (tiers legacy → sorted → E1 → E2 → E3)` | tier via EXL3_FAT_* | Sorts (token, slot) pairs by expert (argsort), counts with scatter_add_ (not bincount, which may stage through the host), uses num_active = -1 and, if tokens ≤ cap, does a single exl3_moe launch; otherwise it dispatches the fat experts through the configured tier (legacy = Python | In a 1024-token chunk, 91–98.6% of the layers had experts above the 128 cap, falling into a Python loop with syncs (1.45 s of CPU in aten::nonzero over 3 steps). | 11 Mixture-of-Experts, 28 Kernels |
| `build_grouped_fat_tables / apply_exl3_grouped_fat` | E3 | Builds on the device, without syncs: fat_rows, exclusive prefix sums (row_off, tile_off), 64-row segments (seg_expert, seg_row0, seg_rows) and the row_token/row_weight/row_expert map via searchsorted. | On-device tables eliminate the synchronization and make E3 capturable; the kernels grid-stride over the actual number of segments (max 512 CTAs in y). | 28 Kernels, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `cooperative_moe (kernel de 2 estágios)` | via EXL3_OVERLAY_HOST; base Turboderp exllamav3@58d4d732 (headers 02aef45c) | In decode (1–32 rows), groups the slots that chose the same expert as rows of an m16 MMA (stage A gate/up, stage B down), with completion counters where the last block applies the rotations and sums the slots in a fixed order. | Reads the weights once per expert run: structured ×1 72.03 → 77.18 tok/s (+7%), ms/step 108.42 → 99.18 (2026-09-16); not bit-exact (18/18 in the 0.3% screen). | 28 Kernels, 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `Exl3MoEMethod` | always with exl3 | MoE method that creates the w13/w2 trellis/suh/svh/mcg (forbids a dense w13_weight), validates the MCG marker, creates one LinearEXL3 per expert without BF16 reconstruction and builds the exl3_moe pointer tables and buffers. | Expanding 288 experts × 43 MoE layers (42 decoder + MTP) to BF16 would not fit; it makes it possible to serve 164 GiB on 2 × 128 GB (Model loading took 79.65 GiB per rank). | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `extensions/cooperative_moe (TP2)` | enabled by EXL3_OVERLAY_HOST generated by prepare_profile.py; .so digest aa3fe5e9… | Specialization of turboderp's 2-stage MoE kernel (58d4d732) for H=4096, local I 1024, top-k 8, K4 MCG, 1–32 rows. | Structured ×1 72.03 → 77.29 tok/s (+7.3%); ×2 113.91 → 124.46 (+9.3%); 32-row kernel 1.183 → 0.997 ms; 18/18 cases within 0.3% of the reference. | 28 Kernels, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `extensions/cooperative_moe/tp3 (ABI 2)` | TP3/EP | EP variant: local I 2048 with no intra-expert sharding, up to 96 local experts, 256/512 slots, 9-bit key, 32- or 64-row TUs. | Historical (combined bundle, not isolatable): C1 +17.90% on code, C8 aggregate +61%, but C8 per stream −17.53% and 7.6K prefill −8.92%. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 28 Kernels |
| `extensions/cooperative_moe/tp3 (ABI2)` | local I 2048 / EP96; 32/64 rows; verified manifest | Cooperative MoE variant for TP3's expert-parallel geometry. | Part of the combined TP3 profile (+17.9%/+20.8% C1). | 28 Kernels, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `patch_exl3_ep_shard.py` | TP3 overlay (after patch_dense_fp8.py) · TP3/EP | Uses moe_parallel_config.tp_size (=1 under pure EP) when slicing EXL3 experts. · Under EP, uses moe_parallel_config.tp_size (1 in pure EP) instead of the world size when slicing experts. | Under EP the loader was still slicing each expert by TP=3: 'dim 0 size 2048 is not divisible by tp=3'. · The rank already owns whole experts; avoids the 2048 not divisible by 3 error. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 38 Parallelism |
| `patch_exl3_expert_map.py` | TP3 overlay · TP3/EP | Caches expert_map in a private attribute. · Caches the expert_map in _exl3_pinned_expert_map. | expert_map is a read-only property under EP → AttributeError. · Under EP the expert_map is a read-only property. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `patch_exl3_fat_kernel.py` | build patch · applied at build time | Adds the E2 (exl3_fat_gemm/_scatter) and E3 (exl3_fat_moe_*) kernels for fat experts. · Copies the fat-kernel .cu/.cuh files into quant/ of exllamav3_ext and adds the m.def(...) entries to bindings.cpp. | The basis for the +20–21% (E2) and +37–45% (E3) in cold prefill. · Additive kernels compiled into the same extension, without touching the stock ones. | 28 Kernels, 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `pin_exl3_expert_map / map_topk_to_local` | always | Pins the expert_map on the device and maps invalid or non-local top-k ids to the n_local sentinel. | A CPU → GPU copy is forbidden inside CUDA graph capture. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 38 Parallelism |
| `resolve_exl3_fat_tier / grouped_fat_eligibility` | at load time | Resolves the effective tier: without a shared SUH it is capped at sorted; E3 requested without the symbols fails at load; ineligible (not K4, not MCG, mul1, hidden % 256, intermediate % 128, capability below 9.0) falls back to E2 with the reason logged. | Explicit, fail-closed degradation; the exl3 e2 diag … effective_tier=grouped line is the boot check. | 28 Kernels, 41 Recipe engineering |

Since 98cf18f it downloads Mia-AiLab/GLM-5.3-Flash-EXL3-4bpw-TensorFold@76c0b517 (83 shards, ~164 GiB); the DFlash2 pin is still dc77ff1c…. | The pins keep changes on the Hub from changing the model; the worker receives it via NFS or rsync. | 24 Checkpoints |

| `logs/cluster.lock` | flock; stop waits 30 s | Lock that serializes start, restart and stop. | Keeps two launchers from destroying each other. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |

#### Prefix caching

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `[glm53-dflash-boundary-lookup-v1]` | with GLM53_DRAFT_KV_COMPACT=1 | Looks up the drafter group ending exactly at the boundary (drop_eagle_block=False) and leaves the manager as non-EAGLE. | DFlash's context KV at position p depends only on the hidden state at p, so it does not need the lookahead block: 1 fewer id per retained window. | 33 Prefix caching, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `[glm53-dflash-eagle-verify-v3]` | stage of patch_hybrid_prefix_hit.py | An EAGLE lookup that failed does not count as verified on a subsequent pass. | Avoids reusing drafter blocks that were not verified. | 33 Prefix caching |
| `[glm53-dflash-swa-replay-v1/v2]` | stage of patch_hybrid_prefix_hit.py | After an APC swap the drafter needs a full fresh window (2048 tokens); if any DFlash group did not return a full, verified window, it applies the replay clamp (backs off the hit by alignment pages) and discards the drafter's blocks. | Ensures the drafter's KV is rebuilt correctly without sacrificing the MLA/Mamba hit. | 33 Prefix caching, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `[glm53-kpool-replay-floor-v1]` | stage of patch_hybrid_prefix_hit.py | The hit candidate leaves at least 4 fresh tokens (the KpoolTail's block_size). | Needed to rebuild the indexer's tail ring. | 33 Prefix caching, 13 Sparse attention |
| `[glm53-partial-replay-fallback-v1]` | stage of patch_hybrid_prefix_hit.py | A thin Mamba tail without a drafter window first tries the previous shared checkpoint. | Preserves a correct recurrent-state hit when the drafter window is missing. | 33 Prefix caching, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `[glm53-participating-fine-hits-v1]` | stage of patch_hybrid_prefix_hit.py | Only groups that participate in prefix caching can veto fine-grained hits. | The KpoolTail, which is scratch, no longer vetoes hits. | 33 Prefix caching |
| `eagle_group_ids (só SlidingWindowSpec exatos)` | part of patch_hybrid_prefix_hit.py | Marks only exact SlidingWindowSpec groups as EAGLE; the drafter does not reduce curr_hit_length (MTP without SWA keeps the all-groups fallback). | GLM does not annotate is_eagle_group: the coordinator marked every group and MLA dropped its last 3584-token page. | 33 Prefix caching, 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |

| `patch_apc_per_group_retention.py` | overlay #9 (after hybrid-prefix) · applied at start; default unset | Per-group retention_interval via VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL_SWA; 0 = boundaries only + low eviction priority. · Creates a per-group retention_interval; the EAGLE-exempt drafter group gets VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL_SWA (integer ≥ 0, multiple of the scheduler b | The drafter consumed 33 of 38 IDs per segment and its blocks went to the LRU tail, protected ahead of the MLA/Mamba ones (priority inversion). · The drafter's blocks (33 of 38 ids per segment) went to the end of the LRU while the MLA/Mamba blocks that produce the hit were freed first: priority inversion (87% of 642 ids). | 33 Prefix caching |
| `patch_cache_reset.py` | overlay #24 at 94ae731 (#25 since #203); GLM53_EXPOSE_CACHE_RESET=1 | Mounts only the cache reset router (elif GLM53_EXPOSE_CACHE_RESET=1). · An elif GLM53_EXPOSE_CACHE_RESET=1 in build_app mounts only /reset_prefix_cache, /reset_mm_cache and /reset_encoder_cache. | Previously, prefix cache reset only existed with full dev mode. · Does not expose /sleep, /rlhf, /rpc and other dev routes, because root routes do not go through the bearer guard. | 33 Prefix caching, 45 Benchmarking |
| `patch_hybrid_prefix_hit.py` | overlay #8; marker [glm53-dflash-boundary-lookup-v1] · applied at start; versioned stages | Only an exact SlidingWindowSpec counts as EAGLE; the draft does not shrink the MLA+Mamba hit; boundary lookup with compact; 4-fresh-token floor for kpool; migrates the legacy form. · Fixes the hybrid APC coordinator with the EAGLE-like drafter in versioned stages, with migration  | Since dflash is use_eagle(), the coordinator treated every group as EAGLE and MLA lost its last 3584-token page. Prefix of 28,672 + tail of 64: repeat TTFT −90.02% (0.302 vs 2.6–3.4 s). · Issue #7: follow-ups looked like 0% APC; with the patch, a ~8k follow-up reuses 7168/8004 tokens and TTFT drops from 8.53 s to 1.30 s; 4 concurrent ~7.5 | 33 Prefix caching, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |

#### Quantization

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `_exl3_shared_w13_suh (SUH compartilhado)` | detected at load time | Detects when gate and up share the same suh and applies a single input Hadamard. | Saves one rotation and is a prerequisite for tiers E1 onward and for the FAST path (shared_input). | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 28 Kernels |
| `Exl3Config.get_quant_method` | always with --quantization exl3 | Dispatches RoutedExperts → Exl3MoEMethod; ParallelLMHead → EXL3 only if declared; LinearBase → Exl3LinearMethod, Glm53DenseFp8Method or UnquantizedLinearMethod. | It is the extension point that decides the precision of each module in the model. | 30 vLLM architecture, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `Exl3LinearMethod (EXL3 denso)` | GLM53_DENSE_EXL3=1; port of Alexbob0/glm53-flash-dense-exl3-tp2 (MIT) ← vcruz305/vllm-exl3 | Loads EXL3 dense linears per output shard (QKV/merged column), with BF16 shards as a contiguous tail, replicated layers (disable_tp) and the lm_head if the vocab is already a multiple of 64. | Mutually exclusive with FP8 on the same module; ABLIT rejects an EXL3 o_proj; not measured in an A/B against the default. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `Glm53DenseFp8Method` | GLM53_DENSE_FP8 | In process_weights_after_loading: scales = amax(\|W\|, dim=1)/448, fp8 = clamp(W/scales, ±448) in e4m3, prepare_fp8_layer_for_marlin; apply uses apply_fp8_marlin_linear (W8A16, the activation stays BF16). | ~11 ms less per step; prose +12–19% on its own, +37% on hard prose with adaptive-k; KL proxy 0.002–0.013 nats/position, argmax 94–100% (dense,kda). | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 28 Kernels |
| `offset_draft_layer_prefixes` | with an EXL3 drafter | Shifts the prefixes declared by the pack (model.layers.0…) to the drafter's runtime numbering (45..49). | Without it, the drafter's quantization config would not match the layer names. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 24 Checkpoints |
| `overlay/exl3.py` | installed at …/quantization/exl3.py | Implements Exl3Config, packed loading, TP sharding (gate/up by column, down by row, all-reduce per layer), fused exl3_moe, E2/E3, dense-FP8, KDA BF16 and dense-EXL3. | It is the quantization plugin that lets vLLM serve the EXL3 checkpoint. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `patch_dense_fp8.py` | overlay #19; GLM53_DENSE_FP8 · applied at start | Reinstalls exl3.py, keeps the quant config in KDA/MLA when it is exl3 and rejects a non_routed_exl3 pack with the flag off. · Installs exl3.py and makes the KDA (kda.py) and MLA (model.py) constructors keep the quant_config when it is exl3 (stock reset it to BF16). | The BF16 dense projections were slow in decode: ~−11 ms/step. · Without it, the dense projections would never receive the FP8 method. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 28 Kernels |
| `patch_model_overrides.py` | baked in at build time only · applied at build time | Puts 'exl3' in the list of quantizations that can override the config's detection. · Inserts exl3 at the top of ModelConfig's ordered list of overrides. | Without it, vLLM rejects --quantization exl3 against the checkpoint's quantization_config. · So that exl3's override_quantization_method wins over auto_gptq and other methods during resolution. | 30 vLLM architecture, 24 Checkpoints |
| `registro do método exl3 (quantization/__init__.py)` | build patch | Adds exl3 to the Literal of methods and lazily imports Exl3Config. | vLLM does not know EXL3. | 30 vLLM architecture, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `registro exl3 (QuantizationMethods + import lazy)` | inline patch in the Dockerfile | Adds exl3 to the QuantizationMethods Literal and lazily imports Exl3Config in get_quantization_config. | Without the registration, vLLM does not recognize quant_method = exl3. | 30 vLLM architecture, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `tools/dense_overlay.py` | --jobs 16 · GLM53_MODEL_PRESET=dense-h3 | Range-reads ~5.3 GB of dense EXL3 tensors from turboderp to assemble the dense-h3 target. · Creates an overlay pack with symlinks to every file of the TR3 pack plus an extra safetensors with the non-expert linears in EXL3 (range-read from turboderp/GLM-5.3-Flash-exl3 branch 4.05b | 66 s with --jobs 16 vs 1261 s serial; not measured in an A/B. · Assembles the dense EXL3 H3 target without downloading another full checkpoint (~2 min of download). | 24 Checkpoints, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `tools/dflash2_exl3_convert.py / dflash2_exl3_quant.sh` | 6 bpw; fork MiaAI-Lab/exllamav3@63b32f0 | Quantizes the DFlash2 drafter to 6 bpw without calibration (synthetic Hessian); k_proj/v_proj stay BF16 (sentinel 16) and candidate_selector.hidden_projection stays BF16; kernel_projection and fc count toward the budget. | The fused context KV needs a flat tensor and the selector is precision-sensitive; it takes ~25 min on the GB10 (including compiling the extension). | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `tools/dflash2_exl3_package.py` | final conversion step | Packages the EXL3 drafter by copying the BF16 tensors byte-identical from the original snapshot and writing quantization_config with relative model.layers.N prefixes. | The relative prefixes are later shifted by offset_draft_layer_prefixes at runtime. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `tools/dflash2_exl3_quant.sh / dflash2_exl3_convert.py / dflash2_exl3_package.py / stage_dense_h3.py` | MiaAI-Lab/exllamav3 63b32f0; 6 bpw | Quantizes the DFlash2 drafter to 6 bpw EXL3, converts, packages and stages the dense-h3 pair. | ~25 min on the GB10 with a synthetic Hessian, no calibration; not measured in an A/B. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `TP3: KDA f_b_proj/g_b_proj mantidos em BF16` | automatic at TP=3 with FP8 enabled | At TP3 the KDA f_b_proj and g_b_proj projections stay BF16 even with FP8 enabled (log: TP=3 keeps KDA f_b_proj/g_b_proj in BF16). | The local pitch of 8,726 is not a multiple of 8 and the offset is not 16 B-aligned, both Marlin requirements. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 28 Kernels |

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |

| `patch_mamba_align_chunking.py` | overlay #6 (after decode-floor v5; v7 since b94fdfd) | Uses the scheduler's LCM (3584), respects the private Mamba boundaries and applies the EAGLE back-off only to the participating non-SWA group. | The state checkpoints were aligned to the wrong block and sub-block progress stalled. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids, 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `patch_mamba_align_chunking.py (v2)` | applied at start | Shared checkpoints use the scheduler's LCM (self.block_size); smaller private Mamba pages force a stop at their next boundary; the EAGLE back-off only applies if a participating non-SWA group is EAGLE; only grants of at least one block are rounded. | The alignment used the smallest page (the drafter's) and small grants rounded down to zero, causing starvation with the mixed-prefill caps; rejects any decode-floor older than v5; since b94fdfd (#283) it accepts v5 and v7. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `patch_scheduler_decode_floor.py (fair v5)` | GLM53_MIXED_PREFILL_CHUNK=fair; marker [glm53-decode-floor:v5] (migrates v1–v4) · superseded by v7 in b94fdfd (#283) | Decodes are allocated first; only prefill that competes with a decoder generates debt; credit accumulates SHARE of the engine time; fit dt = a + b·n (window 24, p75, clamp [1; 1.5]); target = the largest ladder rung that fits in MAX_STEP_MS; a newcomer gets an immediate probe; ro | Resolves issue #6 and the 20 min freeze: a 2k newcomer gets its first token at 11.6 s (skip ~163 s); incumbent 20.6–20.9 tok/s vs 24.7 solo; v4 priced 1024 tokens at ~2.7 s (actual ~0.95 s). | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `patch_scheduler_decode_floor.py (v5)` | overlay #5; marker # [glm53-decode-floor:v5] · v7 since b94fdfd (#283) | Implements the fair policy: share-based credit, a+b·n fit, largest rung that fits, newcomer probing, decodes first. | Newcomers waited for the entire decode (skip) or killed the incumbent (0). TP2: a 2k newcomer in 11.6 s and a 30k one in 163.6 s during the rehearsal; the incumbent keeps ~80–90%. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `patch_scheduler_decode_floor.py (v7)` | overlay #5; marker [glm53-decode-floor:v7]; b94fdfd (#283), after the snapshot | Combines three directions in one patcher, keeping v5's rules (ladder, a + b·n fit, decodes first): a waiting request that vLLM refuses KV allocation no longer stalls a runnable prefill, which keeps being retried and recovers once memory frees (#246); explicit priorities rank fair | Qualified live on a TP2 pair with a pre-registered baseline → candidate → baseline run (2026-09-30): PASS on KV-blocked admission progress, the #180 contention canary (5/5 per arm) and the CPU migration matrix; decode, concurrency and temperature-0 identity were inconclusive because the two baselines differed by more than the tolerance. K | 32 Scheduler & batching, 42 GLM recipe |

| `CandidateSelector` | part of the drafter | For each block position, takes top-k candidates from the lm_head and scores edges unary_logits + einsum(predecessor ⊙ hidden_proj, successor) with low-rank codebooks [vocab, rank]. | A hidden-conditioned bigram model picks a coherent sequence across adjacent positions; hidden_projection stays BF16 because the selector's precision drives acceptance. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `dflash2_speculator.py` | overlay | Candidate-selector walk with a vendored Gumbel-max. | Picks the continuation of the draft block while preserving sampling. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `dflash2_speculator.py (DFlash2Speculator)` | method dflash + DFlash2DraftModel | Speculator that runs the drafter and the selector and, via _cache_draft_logits_kernel, writes the realized scores into draft_logits (−inf outside the candidates). | The draft_logits are required for probabilistic rejection sampling. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |
| `pad-tp3-config.py` | TP3-only copy of the draft config: GQA 32/8 → 36/9 | Generates a padded drafter config (keeping the 4:1 ratio) with model.safetensors as a hardlink to the resolved blob. · Idempotent padding of config.json (which the MTP SpeculativeConfig reads from the file) and of the GQA 32/8 → 36/9 drafter. | qwen3_dflash.py asserts total_num_heads % tp_size with world TP 3 even at draft TP 1; 'FlashInfer requires qo % kv == 0'. · Keeps the ratio of 4 that FlashInfer requires (qo % kv == 0). | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `patch_adaptive_k.py` | overlay #18; GLM53_ADAPTIVE_K | Per-request acceptance EMA, minimum across the batch, and extra FULL graphs for each k+1. · _Glm53AdaptiveK observes the per-request acceptance, chooses k by EMA, uses the minimum across the batch's decodes (the AsyncScheduler sizes the placeholders), trims spec_token_ids and app | A fixed k=7 wastes verification on prose; prose +13–21%. · Prose acceptance decays by position (0.75/0.58/0.41/0.28/0.16/0.09/0.06) and verifying 7 costs ~8 rows; the sparse-MLA and KDA backends only support FULL graphs with a uniform batch. Silk Road +21%, sky/sunset +13%. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `patch_dflash2.py` | baked in at build time only · applied at build time | Registers DFlash2 (decoder_layer_cls, checkpoint is_causal:false → bidirectional attention within the block, V2 speculator). · Installs the drafter and the speculator, honors the checkpoint's is_causal, registers DFlash2DraftModel → (qwen3_dflash2, DFlash2Qwen3ForCausalLM), force | The base image did not know the DFlash2 drafter. · It is the registration that makes this image's vLLM recognize and run DFlash2. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `patch_dflash2_exl3.py` | overlay #20 · with an EXL3 drafter | Installs qwen3_dflash2.py with support for EXL3 drafts (6 bpw): prefix offsets and a shape guard for the fused KV. · Calls offset_draft_layer_prefixes before building the layers and, in the fused context KV buffer, uses the whole tensor when qkv_proj only has K/V (q in EXL3, k/v  | Enables the quantized draft of the dense-h3 preset; it is inert for BF16 drafts. · The DFlash context KV (RMSNorm → fused KV GEMM → K-norm → RoPE) needs a flat weight. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `patch_glm_eagle3.py` | baked in at build time only; taps (6, 15, 25, 34, 43) · applied at build time | Gives Glm5NextModel the EagleModelMixin/SupportsEagle3 interfaces and captures auxiliary states in the mHC (hc_post → hc_contract). · Gives EagleModelMixin and SupportsEagle3 to Glm5NextModel and, after the aux_hidden_state_layers layers, materializes hc_post and contracts the 4  | DFlash2 conditions on the hidden state of target layers; without the contraction to 4096 the states are unusable. · The drafter needs a 4096 hidden, but the mHC layer returns 4 streams (hidden, residual, post, comb). | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 15 Hyper-connections |
| `qwen3_dflash2.py` | overlay (port from vLLM main) | DFlash2 drafter model ('grouped dynamic conv + candidate selector'), with top-k via torch.topk. | The image's LogitsProcessor has no get_top_k_tokens. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `qwen3_dflash2.py (DFlash2Qwen3ForCausalLM)` | SPEC_METHOD=dflash | Port of the DFlash2 drafter model from vLLM main: Qwen3 layers with non-causal SWA attention within the block; uses logits + torch.topk because the image's LogitsProcessor has no get_top_k_tokens. | Without it, this image's vLLM cannot run the DFlash2 drafter. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |

#### API / serving

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--chat-template (CHAT_TEMPLATE / CHAT_TEMPLATE_HOST)` | /opt/glm53/chat_template.jinja ← files/chat_template.jinja | Multimodal template mounted in the container. Builds [gMASK]&lt;sop&gt;&lt;\|system\|&gt;…&lt;\|assistant\|&gt;&lt;think&gt; and the XML tool-call format. | 'The checkpoint's jinja is language-only'. The template is validated with Jinja2 (loopcontrols) before the restart. | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--enable-prompt-tokens-details` | 94ae731: only in examples/tp2-long-coding.env · now in every launcher (start.sh, start-tp3.sh, start-tp4.sh; ecd1af6, PR #206) | Includes in the response the prompt token breakdown (e.g., cached tokens). The client reads usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens (when streaming, with stream_options.include_usage). | Lets the client see prefix-cache hits in long coding sessions. It is response metadata only: it does not enable or retune prefix caching. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 33 Prefix caching |
| `--served-model-name` | GLM-5.3-Flash-EXL3 | Model name exposed in /v1/models. | It is the name clients send in the model field. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--served-model-name (SERVED_MODEL_NAME)` | GLM-5.3-Flash-EXL3 | Id for the model field in the OpenAI API. | It is the name clients need to send. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `/tokenize` | at the root; prompt or messages field; /v1/tokenize is 404 | Endpoint that returns the token IDs. | The warmup uses /tokenize to build prompts with exact token counts. | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DEFAULT_MAX_NEW_TOKENS` | 65536 (1..1,000,000); empty = stock; TP2 only · 65536 (1..1000000); empty = stock | max_tokens default used only when the client omits the field. · max_tokens default applied only to requests that omit the field. | Without it, an omission can consume the entire remaining context and grow the KV until other sessions are preempted (#43). It is not an admission gate ('admission is chunk-based'); an explicit max_tokens: null keeps the native 16. · Avoids answers cut short by a small default when the client omits max_tokens; an explicit null keeps 16. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `examples/pi (perfil de cliente)` | openai-completions, baseUrl http://127.0.0.1:8888/v1, contextWindow 500000, maxTokens 8192, enable_thinking false; settings: httpIdleTimeoutMs 600000, reserveTokens 65536, keepRece | Example configuration of a client agent ('pi') for the API. | (interpretation) It is consistent with a small MAX_NUM_SEQS: 2 active subagents, long timeouts and context compaction. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `extra_body` | do not send it nested over raw HTTP | extra_body is an option of the OpenAI Python SDK, not a JSON field. | Over raw HTTP, the extra fields (chat_template_kwargs etc.) go at the top level. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `PORT` | 8888 | HTTP port of the OpenAI API on the head (--host 0.0.0.0 --port $PORT). | The preflight checks whether it is free; change it if it is taken. The API is open by default. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `VLLM_API_KEY` | empty = no auth | Bearer required on /v1, read natively by vLLM; passed only to the head via -e with no value on the command line. | The key never goes into argv or to the worker. /health, /metrics and /tokenize are left unguarded. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `VLLM_SERVER_DEV_MODE` | 1 mounts all dev routes | vLLM server dev mode. | Exposes more than necessary; GLM53_EXPOSE_CACHE_RESET is the minimal alternative. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `vllm_xargs` | extra per-request field | vLLM extra request field used to pass custom parameters, such as skip_writing_prefix_cache. | Lets you extend the OpenAI API without breaking clients. | 34 API, tools & JSON |

#### Abliteration

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ABLIT` | 0; 1 only via caller export (TP2) or .env.tp3 | Edits self_attn.o_proj (KDA [4096, 8192], DSA [4096, 16384], BF16) at load time; nothing is rewritten to disk. · Turns on load-time editing of the o_proj weights (abliteration). | Off by default and forced to 0 after the .env: ABLIT=1 in the .env has no effect on TP2. · No-op when off; KLD with ablit has not been measured. | 19 Abliteration |
| `ablit/LAYER_MAP.json` | 45 layers + MTP; safety-anchor 0–14, edit 15–44, mtp-edit 45 | Map of layer roles and o_proj shapes (4096×8192 in the linear layers, 4096×16384 in the DSA ones). | Documents which layers are edited and which remain as safety anchors. | 19 Abliteration, 18 The three models |
| `ABLIT_ALPHA` | 3.0 (proj); 1.0 = plain projection | W' = (I − α r rᵀ/‖r‖²) W. · Factor α of the projection W' = (I − α r rᵀ/‖r‖²) W. | α&gt;1 'over-projects: stronger, less coherent'. · α = 1 removes the component; α above 1 over-projects (flips the direction). | 19 Abliteration, 03 Tensors & algebra |
| `ABLIT_DIR` | /opt/glm53/ablit | Directory of abliteration artifacts. | Auto mode picks the transplant if ablit/transplant/ exists in this directory. | 19 Abliteration |
| `ABLIT_DIRECTION` | dealign; bf_oproj; .pt path · dealign; bf_oproj; path to a .pt | Refusal-direction vector used by the proj method (refusal_direction_*.pt files). · Selects the refusal direction (4096-element fp32 vector) used by the proj method. | The chosen direction determines what is removed. · The directions come from different sources (dealign-oproj-svd, blackfrost-oproj-svd) and have different fidelities. | 19 Abliteration |
| `ABLIT_DONOR` | dealignai/GLM-5.3-Flash-UNCENSORED-NVFP4 | Donor checkpoint for the transplanted o_proj tensors. | With HTTP range requests it downloads ~2.7 GiB instead of 164 GiB; the o_proj is byte-identical BF16 between the NVFP4 and the EXL3. | 19 Abliteration, 24 Checkpoints |
| `ABLIT_INCLUDE_MTP` | 1 | Also edits the o_proj of the MTP block. · Also edits the o_proj of the MTP block (layer 45). | 'A stock MTP head keeps proposing refusals' as a draft. · A stock MTP kept proposing refusals via spec decode. | 19 Abliteration, 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `ABLIT_LAYERS` | 15-45 (45 = MTP); 0–14 stay as 'safety anchors' | Range of layers whose o_proj are edited. · Inclusive range of edited layers. | The early layers stay intact to preserve coherence. · Layers 0–14 stay stock as safety anchors. | 19 Abliteration |
| `ABLIT_METHOD` | auto (transplant if ablit/transplant/ is populated, otherwise proj); transplant; proj · auto (transplant if ablit/transplant/ exists, otherwise proj); transplant; proj | transplant copies the bytes of the L15–45 o_proj from a donor checkpoint; proj does orthogonalization. · Chooses between transplanting donor weights and orthogonalizing along a refusal direction. | The transplant reproduces 32/32 bypass; proj with the published vectors is statistically random (max \|rᵀW\| 0.084, same as random vectors). · The published directions are statistically random against the stock o_proj (max \|rᵀW\| 0.084); the transplant reproduces the edit: 32/32 on the donor's gate vs 9/32 with projection. | 19 Abliteration, 25 Measuring quality |
| `preset abliterated` | bullerwins/GLM-5.3-Flash-exl3-4bpw-ablit@14858211ed81d7fa773f8a0db02f38f36d230252, 120 shards (175,642,157,752 bytes) | Serves a pre-edited checkpoint; forces ABLIT=0; the fallback is the same repo; fail-closed. | Applying the runtime edit again 'would produce a different model'. | 19 Abliteration, 24 Checkpoints |
| `refusal_direction_glm53_bf_oproj.pt` | source blackfrost-oproj-svd; alpha_ref 3.0; n_layers 43; skip_early_ref 2 | Alternative refusal direction used with ABLIT_DIRECTION=bf_oproj. | Another direction source, with its own reference parameters. | 19 Abliteration |
| `refusal_direction_glm53_dealign_late.pt` | source dealign-oproj-svd-L15-35-39-43-45; 4096-element fp32 vector | dealign refusal direction used with ABLIT_DIRECTION=dealign. | Direction derived via SVD of the edited o_proj; as a projection direction it reaches 9/32 on the donor's gate. | 19 Abliteration |

#### Attention / kernels

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `additional_config[kda_prefill_backend]` | auto / triton / flashkda | Alternative way, via vLLM's additional_config, to choose the KDA prefill backend. | Same resolution as HAREM_KDA_FLASHKDA without depending on an environment variable. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `backend de atenção do draft (FLASH_ATTN)` | do not pin; SM121 picks FLASH_ATTN for non-causal SWA | Attention backend for the DFlash2 drafter layers. | Pinning TRITON_ATTN (causal mask within the block) dropped structured to ~29 tok/s and 0.31 acceptance: the block needs bidirectional attention. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 28 Kernels |
| `backend de atenção do drafter (FLASH_ATTN vs TRITON_ATTN)` | do not pin; SM121 picks FLASH_ATTN (non-causal) | The drafter needs bidirectional attention within the speculative block. | Pinning TRITON_ATTN (causal within the block) drops acceptance to 0.31 and decode to ~29 tok/s (pos0 is fine, the rest collapse). | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 28 Kernels |
| `DFlash2 GQA (32 Q / 8 KV)` | 32/8; TP3: 36/9 | Query and KV heads of the DFlash2 drafter. | FlashInfer requires qo % kv == 0; on TP3, padding to 36/9 keeps the ratio at 4. | 12 MLA & compact KV, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `FLASHINFER_MLA_SPARSE_SM120` | attention backend for the target's MLA layers · the only sparse-MLA backend on SM12x | FlashInfer's sparse-MLA backend for SM12x, used with NoPE and padding to 576. · FlashInfer's sparse-MLA attention backend for SM12x, with DSV3_2/GLM_NSA geometries (d_qk 576, 656 B, topk 128/512/1024/2048) and DSV4. | It is the only sparse-MLA path on the GB10 and only supports uniform FULL CUDA graphs, which restricts adaptive-k. · No geometry covers d_qk = 512 with topk 2048, which motivated the inline Dockerfile patches. | 28 Kernels, 12 MLA & compact KV |
| `is_causal (config do drafter)` | false (IncoAI DFlash2) | Field in the drafter checkpoint that sets bidirectional attention within the block. | Honoring it is what requires a non-causal backend (FLASH_ATTN) for the drafter. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 06 Attention |

|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--distributed-executor-backend mp` | mp (not Ray) | Uses vLLM's multiprocessing executor, with one process per node. · vLLM's multiprocessing executor (no Ray). | It is simpler than Ray for 2 nodes with 1 GPU each. · Keeps the recipe simple across two nodes, with the worker in headless mode. | 30 vLLM architecture, 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `--headless` | worker only (rank 1) | The worker runs the model without bringing up the API. · Brings up the worker without an API server; only rank 0 serves HTTP. | Only rank 0 hosts the HTTP server and the scheduler. · Avoids a second server and keeps a single entry point. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `--master-addr` | HEAD_IP | Rendezvous address of rank 0 (head). | Every rank connects to it to form the group. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `--nnodes / --node-rank` | 2; 0 (head) / 1 (worker) | Number of nodes and this node's position in the distributed group. | Rank 0 on the head with the API; headless ranks on the workers. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `--tensor-parallel-size` | 2 (TP2); 3; 4 | Degree of tensor parallelism across the nodes. | TP2 is the default; TP3/TP4 require their own pads, EP and profiles. | 38 Parallelism |
| `directed ring (cabeamento TP3)` | Port0 of each node → Port1 of the next; one /24 per cable; MTU 9000; ipv4.never-default yes | Physical topology required for the ring of 3 nodes directly connected via CX7. | 'A mirrored ring leaves one pair that never connects'; reordering NCCL_IB_HCA does not fix it. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `ENABLE_EXPERT_PARALLEL / --enable-expert-parallel` | 1 (TP3) | Turns on expert parallel: 96 of the 288 experts per rank. | The packed trellis with intermediate size 2048 is not divisible by 3. | 38 Parallelism, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `HEAD_CX7_IB / WORKER_CX7_IB` | rocep1s0f1 / rocep1s0f0; comma-separated list for dual-rail · RDMA HCA name per node | Each rank's RDMA device (HCA); becomes NCCL_IB_HCA. · Names of each node's RDMA HCAs. | Defines which physical port carries the TP all-reduce. · NCCL needs the right HCA to use RoCEv2 instead of TCP. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `HEAD_CX7_IF / WORKER_CX7_IF` | enp1s0f1np1 / enp1s0f0np0 · ConnectX-7 interface name per node | Each rank's CX7 network interface; becomes NCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME and GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME per rank. · Names of each node's ConnectX-7 network interfaces. | NCCL cannot use the 10.0.0.x aliases: without these pins ncclCommInitRank hangs. Exporting a generic NCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME does not override them. · NCCL does not use the 10.0.0.x aliases; it needs each node's real interface names. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `HEAD_GID / WORKER_GID` | default = NCCL_IB_GID_INDEX; kit: head=4, worker=3 · GID per node | Per-rank GID index, passed as each container's NCCL_IB_GID_INDEX. · Per-node alternative to NCCL_IB_GID_INDEX, with each NIC's GID. | The GID is per NIC: 'this kit needs head=4, worker=3'. A single global index does not work for every kit. · The nodes may have different GID indices. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `HEAD_HOST_IP / WORKER_HOST_IP / WORKER2_HOST_IP` | empty → the rank's 10.0.0.x IP | Address each rank advertises to the process group on TP3. | It must be on the chosen socket interface; otherwise the rendezvous does not complete. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `HEAD_IP` | 10.0.0.1 | Rank 0's IP: becomes --master-addr and the head's VLLM_HOST_IP; the preflight requires it to be assigned locally. | Every rank does the rendezvous here (port MASTER_PORT). In the kit, 10.0.0.x are loopback/management aliases, not the data path, which goes over RoCE. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `MASTER_PORT` | 29521 | Becomes --master-port, the port of the torch.distributed rendezvous on the head. | The preflight checks that it is free. A busy port prevents the ranks from finding each other. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_BUFFSIZE / NCCL_LL128_BUFFSIZE` | 1048576 / 262144 (TP3) | Size of NCCL's per-connection buffers (Simple and LL128). | Keeps the host's pinned memory sane on a GB10 (otherwise 512 connections × 9 MiB). | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 27 Unified memory |
| `NCCL_CROSS_NIC` | 0 (TP2); 1 (TP3/TP4) · 1 (TP3) | Allows NCCL to pair NICs with different indices across nodes. · Allows NCCL to use different NICs on the two sides of a channel. | Rings of 3 or more nodes with two ports per node need cross-NIC to close. · In the TP3 directed ring NCCL pairs NIC indices per channel; the Port0 → Port1 link to the next node needs this. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `NCCL_IB_DISABLE` | 0 | Keeps the IB/RoCE verbs transport enabled. | Without it NCCL would fall back to TCP sockets, which are much slower for the per-step all-reduces. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_GID_INDEX` | 3 (template); alternatives per kit · correct RoCEv2 GID of the NIC | Index into the RoCE GID table that NCCL uses. The preflight validates the index on each listed HCA and prints tables 0–7 with the type (RoCE v1/v2). · Index of the RoCEv2 GID used by NCCL. | An all-zero entry kills the rank about 60 s later (ibv_modify_qp errno 61); the preflight catches this before it brings down the healthy pair. · A wrong or zeroed GID brings down the rank in ~60 s (ibv_modify_qp errno 61). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_HCA` | data HCAs (TP3) | HCAs used by NCCL for data traffic. | In TP3 the data goes over the directed-ring HCAs, and the bootstrap over the management LAN. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `NCCL_IB_MERGE_NICS` | 0 | Does not merge the two ports into one logical NIC. | Each port is pinned explicitly per rank via NCCL_IB_HCA. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_ROCE_VERSION_NUM` | 2 | Uses RoCEv2 (RDMA over UDP/IP, routable). | The chosen GIDs are of type RoCE v2 (::ffff:ip); the version must match. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_SUBNET_AWARE_ROUTING` | 1 (TP3) | Pairs NICs by subnet, not by index. · Subnet-aware routing for NCCL over IB/RoCE. | A 3-cycle with two ports per node has no consistent labeling: without it you get 'ibv_modify_qp … 110 Connection timed out'. · Part of the TP3 RoCE directed-ring configuration with direct links between nodes. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `NCCL_IGNORE_CPU_AFFINITY` | 1 | NCCL ignores the process's CPU affinity. | Part of the containers' fixed NCCL environment; the inventory does not detail the reason. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_NCHANNELS` | empty → NCCL decides; positive integer; TP3 empty → NCCL_MIN_NCHANNELS=NCCL_MAX_NCHANNELS=8 | Sets NCCL_MIN_NCHANNELS = NCCL_MAX_NCHANNELS on both ranks. | Pins the number of parallel rings (reference 'Entrpi 2× Spark: 8'). In TP3, pinning it at 8 limits the host's pinned memory. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_NET` | IB | Forces NCCL's IB network transport. | Prevents NCCL from silently picking sockets. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_NET_PLUGIN` | none | Does not load an external NCCL network plugin. | Uses only the built-in IB transport, which gives predictable behavior. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_NVLS_ENABLE` | 0 | Disables NVLink SHARP. | The GB10 has no NVLink/NVSwitch, so the feature does not exist on this machine. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_P2P_DISABLE / NCCL_SHM_DISABLE` | 1 / 1 (TP3) | Disables NCCL's local P2P and shared-memory transports. | Keeps NCCL on IB instead of guessing a local transport (copied from the DS4.1 recipe). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_PROTO` | ^LL128 (TP3) | Excludes NCCL's LL128 protocol. | Part of the set that limits pinned memory in TP3; the LL and Simple protocols remain. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NNODES` | 2 | Becomes --nnodes; each container receives --node-rank 0 (head) or 1 (worker). | Multi-node vLLM without Ray needs to know how many nodes there are and the rank of each process. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 38 Parallelism |
| `SOCKET_IFNAME` | TP3: enP7s7 | Interface used by the bootstrap on the management LAN. | Separates the bootstrap from the RoCE data traffic, which uses the ConnectX-7 HCAs. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `SOCKET_IFNAME (+ HEAD_/WORKER_/WORKER2_SOCKET_IFNAME)` | empty → uses the CX7 pins; TP3 kit: management LAN enP7s7 | Control interface (gloo + NCCL bootstrap) in TP3. | A 3-node ring cannot use the CX7 interface: each pair has its own /24 and gloo accepts exactly ONE interface name. The data stays on RoCE via NCCL_IB_HCA. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `TP` | 2 (do not change; TP3/TP4 use start-tp3.sh/start-tp4.sh) | Becomes --tensor-parallel-size: each node runs one GPU process with half of each matrix. | 'do not change for this recipe': TP=3 is the start-tp3.sh launcher (with padding and EP), not TP=3 in the .env. | 38 Parallelism, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `TP3_HEAD_OVERRIDE / --hf-overrides` | 66 (3 × 22) | Passes --hf-overrides with 66 attention heads for TP3. | 64 is not divisible by 3; 96 (32 local) would leave one rank with only dummy KDA heads and the logits collapse. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 24 Checkpoints |
| `USE_HOST_NCCL / NCCL_HOST_DIR / WORKER_NCCL_HOST_DIR / NCCL_SO_NAME` | 0 / ~/nccl-2.30.7 / libnccl.so.2.30.7 | With 1, does an LD_PRELOAD of a host NCCL instead of the image's NCCL. | An LD_PRELOAD of a second library makes DeepEP flag a duplicate NCCL. Keep 0. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `VLLM_HOST_IP` | HEAD_IP on the head; WORKER_IP on the worker | Address each rank advertises to distributed vLLM, set per rank together with NCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME/NCCL_IB_HCA/GID. | A wrong address makes the rendezvous pick the wrong interface. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `WORKER2_*` | WORKER2_IP 10.0.0.3; USER/HOME/SSH/CX7_IF/CX7_IB/GID inherit from rank 1 | Configuration of rank 2 (third node) in TP3. | The third node needs its own IP, interface, HCA and GID, with defaults inherited from worker 1. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `WORKER3_*` | WORKER3_IP 10.0.0.4 etc. | Configuration of rank 3 in TP4 (measured kit: 4 Sparks, 2× 200G + 2× 100G on a CRS812 switch). | TP4 is experimental and was not tested by the maintainer. With a switch there is no direct ring. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `WORKER_IP` | 10.0.0.2 | IP of rank 1: target of the launcher's SSH and the worker's VLLM_HOST_IP. | Defines the second node of TP=2; the launcher uses it for SSH, rsync/NFS and the remote docker run. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 41 Recipe engineering |

| `--kv-cache-dtype / KV_CACHE_DTYPE` | fp8 → fp8_ds_mla (packed layout) | Packed layout of 656 B per token per MLA layer: 512 fp8 NoPE + 16 B of scales + 128 B of RoPE (zeros here). | The only sparse-MLA kernel on SM12x accepts only fp8_ds_mla; BF16 and NVFP4 have no sparse kernel. | 23 Quantized KV cache, 12 MLA & compact KV |

TP3/TP4 strip this argument from the shared .env. · With | 31 PagedAttention & KV, 27 Unified memory |

The 560 MiB E3 scratch keeps 1M from fitting at util 0.87. Reducing to 256k to 'free up slots' does not help: the hybrid block-id overhead shrinks the pool. | 31 PagedAttention & KV, 10 Prefill, decode & KV |
| `block_size` | 64 (MLA kernel); MLA manager 3584; scheduler block = LCM = 3584; KpoolTail 4 | KV allocation granularity in each group of the hybrid cache. | APC hits are aligned to 3584 tokens (7168/10752/14336 = 2/3/4 pages). | 31 PagedAttention & KV, 33 Prefix caching |
| `blocos por request de 1M (414)` | 414 = 280 MLA + 1 tail + 4×9 Mamba + 97 drafter | Denominator of vLLM's GPU KV cache size line. | 643 / 414 = 1.553 → 1,553,140 tokens is not the cache size; the actual cached conversation = 642 // 38 × 3584 = 57,344 tokens. | 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `drafter SWA (grupo 6)` | window 2048; block 64 (or 896 compact); 5 layers; 2048 B/token/layer on TP2 | Geometry of the DFlash2 drafter's KV group (sliding window). | 8 KV heads × 128 × 2 (K,V) × 2 B = 4096 B/token, half per rank on TP2; an EAGLE hit needs 33 contiguous blocks. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `fp8_ds_mla` | 656 B/token/layer = 512 NoPE (fp8) + 16 B scales + 128 B RoPE (64 bf16) | Packed MLA KV layout: FP8 latent with scales plus a BF16 RoPE block. | GLM is NoPE, so the overlay writes a zeroed RoPE block (pad 512→576): 'a zero RoPE block adds nothing to QK'. It costs ~24% more DSA KV (656 vs ~528 B). | 23 Quantized KV cache, 12 MLA & compact KV |
| `GLM53_DRAFT_KV_COMPACT` | 1 when SPEC_METHOD=dflash and unset; 0 otherwise; explicit empty = error · 1 when SPEC_METHOD=dflash (unset); 0 otherwise; explicit empty is rejected | Derives the draft block as the largest multiple of 64 that divides the MLA block and fits in the physical page (TP2: page 3584 × 656 = 2,351,104 B, draft 2048 B/token → block 896; TP3: 2560 → 640). · Drafter block = largest multiple of 64 that divides the MLA block and fits in th | Reduces the block IDs reserved per maximum-length request from 180 to 121 (−32.78%), without reducing VRAM. 14 GiB: 876,958 → 1,572,073 tokens (1.85×). The drafter's APC lookup now ends exactly at the boundary. · Drafter id demand drops from 65 to 6 per request and repeat TTFT drops 90% in the 64-token-tail case; no universal decode gain  | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `GLM53_KV_CAPACITY_LOG` | 1; 0 | Logs each KV group and a capacity summary at boot. · Enables the per-KV-group log and the summary of ids per aligned segment. | The 'GPU KV cache size' in the log is max_concurrency × max_model_len, not the prefix cache size. E.g.: 643 block IDs, 38 per 3584 segment → ~57K tokens of cached conversation. · Log only; makes the reading of the real cached-conversation capacity honest. | 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `KpoolTail (block_size 4)` | block_size 4 (= index_kpool), 1 block per request, circular | KV group that holds, as circular scratch, the indexer's tail of not-yet-compressed tokens. | It is a special group: it must not veto prefix cache hits nor be indexed beyond the row size. | 13 Sparse attention, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `mamba_block_size` | 16 | Granularity of the Mamba/KDA state group; it enters the LCM that yields the scheduler's 3584 block. | Linear attention state can only be checkpointed at aligned boundaries. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `mamba_cache_mode=align` | align | Mode in which the KDA state (MambaSpec) is stored in block-aligned checkpoints, in 4 Mamba groups. | The recurrent state can only be reused at aligned positions; the origin of the state-free and aligned-chunking patches. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids, 33 Prefix caching |
| `mla_page (bloco do manager 3584)` | 3584 tokens × 656 B = 2,351,104 B per layer | Mamba-aligned block of the KV manager: hybrid vLLM picks an attention block_size whose page accommodates the KDA state. | That is why APC hits are aligned to 3584 tokens; the MLA kernel uses a 64-token page. | 31 PagedAttention & KV, 33 Prefix caching |
| `speculative_config.kv_cache_dtype (KV do drafter)` | auto/bf16, forced when the target uses fp8_ds_mla, fp8* or nvfp4 | Forces the drafter's KV to auto/BF16 even when the target is in FP8 MLA. | The dense drafter cannot use the target's MLA layout and SM121 has no FA3/FA4 for pure FP8; it costs 2048 B/token/layer on TP2. | 23 Quantized KV cache, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `stride da cauda do indexer` | 118,272 B (stride(0)=59136 bf16) at block 3584; 38,016 B at block 1152 (upstream) | The tail is an as_strided view of the indexer page, not a dense array. | Addressing it as dense corrupted another request's indexer block. | 13 Sparse attention, 31 PagedAttention & KV |

|---|---|---|---|---|

| `cooperative_moe constantes TP2 (HIDDEN, INTERMEDIATE_LOCAL, TOPK, ROWS_MAX, SLOTS_MAX, EXPERTS_MAX, BITS, CTR_LEN, PARAMS_SIZE)` | 4096, 1024, 8, 32, 256, 288, 4, 3587, 344 | Compile-time specialization of the cooperative kernel for the GLM TP2 geometry. | The sort key (expert shifted by 8 bits, or slot) limits it to 256 slots; any other shape stays on the stock path. | 28 Kernels |
| `DENSE_EXL3_PREFILL_BF16_MIN_M` | 144 | M boundary for using the BF16 copy of dense EXL3; equal to LinearEXL3's AUTO_RECONSTRUCT_THRESHOLD. | Deliberately separate from Marlin's 512: each library has its own crossover. | 28 Kernels |
| `dispatch_policy.json (cooperative_moe/tp3)` | geo 2 at 2,3,4,5,10,18,24 rows; geo 1 at 1,6–9,12,15,16,20,25,30,32,35,40,48,56; stock for the rest; 64 rows = stock | Per-row-count policy that chooses a cooperative geometry or the stock kernel in the TP3/EP variant. | Chosen by the lowest worst-case ratio over 3 EP ranges × 2 routing patterns, requiring ≤ 0.97 (gain ≥ 3%). | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 28 Kernels |

TP3 does not inherit it from the .env (the ABI would be wrong). · It is the only real way to activate the cooperative kernel; GLM53_COOPERATIVE_MOE=1 alone does nothing. | 28 Kernels, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `EXL3_SELFCHECK_GPU` | 0 at build; 1 at launch | Turns on the EXL3 overlay tests that need a GPU. | There is no GPU at build time; at launch it validates the real kernels before serving. | 28 Kernels, 25 Measuring quality |
