Must be identical on all ranks before graph capture. · Geo 2 launches rows*topk*(H/32) = 8192 down blocks with 8 rows (vs 2048 in geo 1) and lost decode throughput on this kit; geo1: structured 80.0 vs 78.0 tok/s. | 28 Kernels |
| `GLM53_DENSE_EXL3_PREFILL_BF16` | off; all; list kda_in,kda_o,mla_qkv_a,mla_q_b,mla_o,shared_gate_up,shared_down,dense_gate_up,dense_down; H3 profile: kda_in,shared_down,mla_qkv_a,shared_gate_up,kda_o,mla_q_b · off | Keeps the reconstructed BF16 of the listed EXL3 projections for M&gt;144. · Keeps a reconstructed BF16 copy (get_weight_tensor(), Hadamards already applied) of the dense EXL3 linears for use with M above 144. | In prefill (large M), a BF16 GEMM beats on-the-fly trellis dequantization. · The bottleneck of dense EXL3 prefill is the Hadamard/cast passes around the reconstruct, not the reconstruct itself. | 28 Kernels, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |

Requires an image built with the patch. · +7.71% / +8.88% / +14.59% decode (TheGrill A/B/A2); fail-closed if the image lacks the patch. | 28 Kernels, 25 Measuring quality |

Prefill TTFT −12%, agentic −4..−10%, at the cost of +3.3 GiB/rank (12576×4096×2 B × 34 layers). Requires kda in GLM53_DENSE_FP8. · Cold TTFT −11.4% (16k), −12.3% (100k), mixed −14.1%; costs +3.29 GiB per rank (98.25 MiB × 34 layers). | 28 Kernels, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |

| `KDA_BF16_LARGE_M_MIN_M` | 512 (not configurable) | Boundary of the BF16 vs Marlin dispatch, in number of scheduled rows (M). | Decode never exceeded M=220 and prefill stays ≥ 768; the 221–511 band was empty in the measured steps. | 28 Kernels, 10 Prefill, decode & KV |

| `TORCH_CUDA_ARCH_LIST / FLASHINFER_CUDA_ARCH_LIST` | 12.1a | Compiles and JITs the kernels for sm_121a (GB10), at build time and at runtime. | Kernels written for datacenter GPUs (sm_90/sm_100) do not work on the GB10; wrong cubins fail or run slowly. | 26 Inside the GPU, 28 Kernels |

#### Launch / topology

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--device /dev/infiniband / --cap-add IPC_LOCK / --ulimit memlock=-1` | always | Exposes the verbs devices and allows pinning memory without limit. | RDMA needs to register and pin buffers and to access /dev/infiniband inside the container. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `--gpus all / --network host` | always | The container sees the GB10 GPU and uses the host's network stack. | NCCL, gloo, RoCE and the API work without NAT or port mapping. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--ipc=host / --shm-size 32g` | always | Large shared memory between the vLLM processes. | vLLM's shm_broadcast (API ↔ EngineCore ↔ workers) uses shared memory. | 30 vLLM architecture, 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `--ulimit stack=67108864` | 64 MiB | Large stack for the container processes. | Part of the recipe's standard docker run flags; the inventory does not detail the reason. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `.env.example` | recipe defaults | The recipe's defaults file; it differs from the code on some defaults (GLM53_MIXED_PREFILL_CHUNK, EXL3_FAT_GROUPED, GLM53_INDEXER_WORKSPACE). | What applies in production is what the launcher exports, not the code default. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `.env.example (perfil TP2)` | MAX_MODEL_LEN 850000, MAX_NUM_SEQS 4, MNBT 7168, util 0.85, KV 11 GiB, DFLASH 7 / draft TP 2, DENSE_FP8 all, KDA_BF16_LARGE_M 1, LOAD_FORMAT instanttensor, fair | The current coherent TP2 profile. | Fits 850k with 1.45× of KV headroom, dense FP8 and E3 prefill. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `.env.tp3.example (start-tp3.sh)` | 1000000, util 0.80, draft TP 1, DENSE_FP8 dense,kda, KDA_BF16 0, SWA retention 0, TP3_HEAD_OVERRIDE=66, ENABLE_EXPERT_PARALLEL=1, MM_ENCODER_TP_MODE=data, HAREM_KDA_FLASHKDA 0; KV  | 3-node profile, tested on 2026-09-14. | TP3 lab: structured 87.8 / code 54.9 / prose 39.6 tok/s (TP2: 73.4/45.0/32.9). 40 GiB cap → 3,230,656 tokens (3.23× of 1M). | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 42 GLM recipe |
| `.env.tp4.example (start-tp4.sh)` | util 0.75, MAX_NUM_SEQS 8, MNBT 2048, DFLASH 3, draft TP 4, SLICE_TOKENS 0; inherits 850000 · temp rows: 94ae731 128 (E2), now 32 with E3 (e5dc885); GLM53_ADAPTIVE_K=off (opt-in, 1 | Experimental 4-node profile. At snapshot 94ae731 it did not forward E3, adaptive-k, dense FP8, MM_IMAGE_TOKENS, LONG_PREFILL or DEFAULT_MAX_NEW_TOKENS. Since 2026-09-30 start-tp4.sh forwards EXL3_FAT_GROUPED with default 1 (e5dc885), takes adaptive-k only as an explicit .env.tp4  | Decode 1.45× vs 2 nodes, cold prefill only +29% (fabric-bound), 8 streams 135 tok/s; a 150-min soak hung 3/3 times (31–78 min). E3 on TP4 (4 Sparks, 1M ctx, 2026-09-27): cold prefill 1,316 / 1,761 / 1,940 → 1,772 / 2,422 / 2,733 tok/s at 8K / 32K / 100K (+35 / +38 / +41%), decode unchanged. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `ARG BASE` | vllm/vllm-openai:glm53-flash-arm64-cu130@sha256:905c0293… (vLLM fork 0.1.dev20051+g487ecf187, CUDA 13.0, arm64) | Base image of the vLLM fork with GLM-5.3-Flash support. | It is pinned by digest, not by tag, so that the build is reproducible. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `BUILD` | 0; 1 forces a rebuild | Runs docker build of this Dockerfile. | Applies the build patches locally when the published image does not work. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `CUDA_HOME / CPATH` | /usr/local/cuda; headers in nvidia/cu13/include | CUDA toolchain used to compile exllamav3_ext. | The image keeps the CUDA headers under nvidia/cu13; without CPATH the compilation cannot find them. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `examples/tp2-long-coding.env` | 262144, seqs 2, MNBT 1024, util 0.865, KV auto (≈12.7–12.9 GiB; 4.15–4.21×), DENSE_FP8 dense,kda, KDA_BF16 1, EXL3_MOE_FAST 1, SPINWAIT 16, retention 14336/0, --enable-prompt-token | Profile for long coding sessions with few users. | Trades maximum context for headroom and history retention; the 14336/0 retention was not qualified. | 42 GLM recipe, 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `examples/tp3-throughput.env` | 1000000, seqs 8, MNBT 4096, LONG_PREFILL 2048, KV 32 GiB, draft TP 1, ADAPTIVE_K ema (2,4,7), DENSE_FP8 dense,kda,shared,mla, TEMP_ROWS 64, HAREM_KDA_FLASHKDA 1, 25 capture sizes,  | Combined TP3 throughput profile (with the ABI2 cooperative MoE). | C1 coding 41.46 → 48.88 (+17.90%), prose +20.83%; C8 aggregate 76.41 → 123.06; TTFT C8 10.63 → 0.52 s; per stream C8 −17.53%; prefill 7.6K −8.92%. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 45 Benchmarking |
| `EXTRA_ARGS` | --kv-cache-memory-bytes 11811160064 + --cudagraph-capture-sizes … (auto) · extra vllm serve arguments (e.g., --kv-cache-memory-bytes) | Extra vllm serve flags coming from the .env, space-separated. · Additional arguments forwarded to vllm serve from the .env. | This is how the fixed KV pool and explicit capture sizes get in. TP3/TP4 remove --kv-cache-memory-bytes from the shared .env. · An explicit value there always wins over what the launcher generates (e.g., capture sizes). | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `GLM53_MODEL_PRESET` | empty; abliterated; dense-h3 · empty; dense-h3 | Selects pinned checkpoint presets. · Builds/selects the dense EXL3 H3 target + EXL3 6 bpw drafter pair. | Each preset carries its own repository, revision, shard count and SHA-256, fail-closed. · Opt-in, TP2 only, not measured in an A/B against the default. | 24 Checkpoints, 42 GLM recipe |
| `GLM53_OVERLAY_ORDER` | 94ae731: 25 overlays: cold_load_uma → skip_cudagraph_profile → … → cache_reset → ablit · now 26 (patch_indexer_warmup_range.py as #24, #203) | Ordered list of overlays applied as python3 /opt/glm53/&lt;patch&gt; on each rank. · Order in which the overlays are reapplied at start, idempotently and fail-closed, on both ranks. | Order matters: hybrid-prefix before per-group-retention (same insertion point); no-store after it; kv-capacity-log after drafter-group; mamba-chunking after decode-floor v5 (v7 since #283); on TP3, EP-shard/expert-map after dense-fp8. · Several patches share files and anchors (coordinator, kv_cache_utils.py, scheduler.py); ranks with diff | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `IMAGE` | ghcr.io/miaai-lab/glm-5.3-flash-2x-dgx-sparks:exl3-instanttensor; alt :exl3 (without InstantTensor), glm53-flash-sm121:local | Serving image used on both ranks. | git pull does not swap the running image: old kits need to pull the new tag. Do not use glm53-flash-sm121:v8 (old NVFP4/Ray). | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `IMAGE_PLATFORM` | empty → inspect → linux/arm64 | Platform passed to docker save. | Multi-arch OCI indexes break docker load without --platform. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `imagem base vllm/vllm-openai:glm53-flash-arm64-cu130@sha256:905c0293…` | vLLM 0.1.dev20051+g487ecf187 | vLLM base image, pinned by digest. | All patches are anchored byte for byte to this pin; on its own the image does not serve the checkpoint on SM121 (pe_dim, exl3, DFlash2, hybrid KV…). | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `MAX_JOBS` | 8 (main build); 2 (Dockerfile.e3-layer) | Compilation parallelism for the extensions. | 'keep the compile light on a UMA host': a heavy compile competes for the same RAM as the GPU. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `SKIP_BUILD` | 0; 1 keeps the GHCR image even with a diverging stamp | Prevents the automatic rebuild when the recipe stamp diverges. | Without it, any change in Dockerfile/overlay/files/tests/ablit triggers a local rebuild. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `SKIP_OVERLAY_VERIFY` | 0 | With 0, runs test_exl3_overlay.py with EXL3_SELFCHECK_GPU=1 on the GPU before launching. | Performs a real self-check of the sm_121a cubins and the fused GEMM before serving. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `SKIP_PULL / PULL` | 0 / 0 | Skips or forces the docker pull of the image. | Controls whether the kit fetches the published tag or uses the local image. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `SKIP_SHIP` | 0 | With 1, does not copy the image to the worker. Normally the worker runs docker pull; if that fails, docker save --platform linux/arm64 \| ssh docker load. | The worker does not need access to GHCR (issue #8). | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |

#### Memory / weight loading

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--gpu-memory-utilization (GPU_MEM_UTIL)` | 0.85 (TP2; was 0.87); .env.tp3 0.80; .env.tp4 0.75; long-coding 0.865; TP3/TP4 scripts default 0.87 | Fraction of 'GPU' memory (on UMA, the system RAM) that vLLM may use. | Each 0.01 equals 1.2 GiB of host headroom. A 256k prefill at 0.87 with zero MemAvailable took down a head (2026-09-06). 0.87 requires ≥105.9 GiB free after the ~9 GiB init. | 27 Unified memory, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `CG_ESTIMATE / VLLM_MEMORY_PROFILER_ESTIMATE_CUDAGRAPHS` | 1 (upstream); 0 removes the deduction | Controls whether the memory profiler subtracts an estimate of CUDA graph memory from the KV. | The estimate is 2.43 GiB, but the captured graphs consume −0.19 GiB: ~2.6 GiB of KV stay reserved and never used. 0 keeps the CUDA graphs and only removes the deduction; with 0, patch_skip_cudagraph_profile skips the dry capture (KV profile 18 → 7 s). | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `entrada do workspace do indexer (132 B)` | 128 fp8 + 4 for the scale; stock = max_model_len × 40 entries | Size of each entry in the indexer's gather workspace. | In stock at 1M, 5,036.40 MiB is subtracted from the KV during profiling. | 13 Sparse attention, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `GLM53_COLD_LOAD_STAGE_MMAP` | 1 | Clone()s file-backed mmap tensors when the kernel page size is not 4 KiB. | Required on kernel 6.17.13-rocket64k: cuMemcpyHtoDAsync hangs with an mmap source on 64 KiB pages. | 27 Unified memory |
| `GLM53_COLD_LOAD_UMA` | 1 (kill switch 0) | Sizes the InstantTensor buffer against MemAvailable, not MemFree. | Makes it possible to boot with a full page cache: 164 GiB in 36 s at 4.82 GB/s. | 27 Unified memory |
| `GLM53_COLD_LOAD_UMA / GLM53_COLD_LOAD_STAGE_MMAP` | unset (enabled); 0 = kill switch | Enables the MemAvailable-based InstantTensor budget and the mmap clone on kernels with 64 KiB pages (patch_cold_load_uma). | cuMemcpyHtoDAsync 'hangs on 64 KiB file-backed mappings'. They are forwarded only if defined (an exported empty value would trip the kill switch). | 27 Unified memory |
| `GLM53_HOST_MEM_HYGIENE` | 1 | Before docker run: sync; echo 1 &gt; /proc/sys/vm/drop_caches on both nodes (via sudo -n), then waits for MemAvailable ≥ GPU_MEM_UTIL × MemTotal + 1.5 GiB (up to 45 attempts, 2 s apart). · Drops the host page cache before docker run and waits for MemAvailable. | After a 164 GiB rsync, MemFree sits at ~2 GiB and InstantTensor aborted ('buffer_size … exceeds device memory budget') or cut io_depth down to a few dozen. · The container cannot run drop_caches; with UMA the page cache occupies memory that would otherwise belong to the GPU. | 27 Unified memory, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `GLM53_INDEXER_WORKSPACE` | rightsize (TP2 since 2026-09-07); stock; TP3/TP4 scripts default to stock (they inherit rightsize from .env) · .env.example:333 = rightsize; unset = stock | Size of the sparse indexer's gather workspace in prefill. · Indexer gather workspace = min(max_num_seqs, MNBT) × cdiv(max_model_len + k, compress_ratio) entries of 132 B. | stock = max_model_len × 40 entries × 132 B = 5,036.40 MB locked at 1M. rightsize = min(MAX_NUM_SEQS, MNBT) × cdiv(MAX_MODEL_LEN + k, index_kpool) = 126 MB at seqs=4 (504 MB at 16), roughly +26–28% KV. · 5,036.40 MiB (stock, 1M) → 125.9 MiB (4 seqs), ≈ +26–28% KV. | 13 Sparse attention, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `GLM53_LOAD_CLONE` | 1; exactly 0/1 | Copies mmap tensors (auto/lazy) into independent CPU storage. · Stages auto/lazy mmap tensors in CPU memory before handing them to the loader. | Moves the tensors off the mmap. 0 does not disable the non-4KiB page safeguard. · Avoids direct H2D copies from mmap; does not disable the 64 KiB protection. | 27 Unified memory |
| `GLM53_LOAD_PREFETCH` | 0; 0..16 · 0 (0..16) | Read-ahead of local shards (including the current one), with POSIX_FADV_DONTNEED after each shard is consumed. · Threaded read-ahead of local shards + POSIX_FADV_DONTNEED on the consumed shards. | 'The window does not bound host RAM or page-cache residency'. NFS/Lustre keep the stock prefetch. · Speeds up reads and gives page cache back; it is not a RAM limit; NFS/Lustre keep the stock prefetch. | 27 Unified memory |
| `GLM53_PREFLIGHT_MEMORY_HEADROOM_KIB` | 2097152 (2 GiB) | Headroom required beyond GPU_MEM_UTIL × total in the memory preflight (MemAvailable ≥ util×total + 2 GiB). | Triggers PREFLIGHT FAIL before the healthy containers are stopped. | 27 Unified memory, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `GPU_MEM_UTIL / --gpu-memory-utilization` | 0.85 (TP2); 0.80 (TP3); 0.75 (TP4) | Fraction of unified memory handed to vLLM. | Each 0.01 = 1.2 GiB of headroom for the host; with UMA the operating system competes for the same RAM. | 27 Unified memory, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `INSTANTTENSOR_BUFFER_SIZE` | unset (4 GiB buffer); tp3-throughput 536870912 | Size of the InstantTensor staging buffer. | A smaller buffer gives memory back to the KV and the OS under UMA, at the cost of load throughput. | 27 Unified memory |
| `INSTANTTENSOR_CHUNK_SIZE / INSTANTTENSOR_IO_DEPTH` | unset; best from the sweep: chunk 8 MiB × io_depth 256 | Size of each read and number of I/Os in flight. | In the measurement, reads reached 4.88 GB/s, close to the NVMe's 4.9 GB/s O_DIRECT ceiling. | 27 Unified memory |
| `INSTANTTENSOR_CONCURRENCY / INSTANTTENSOR_BACKEND / INSTANTTENSOR_CACHE_BUFFER` | unset; tp3-throughput CACHE_BUFFER=0 | InstantTensor concurrency, I/O backend and cache buffer (CACHE_BUFFER only on TP3). | Loader fine-tuning. In the combined TP3 profile, weight loading dropped from 324–371 s to ~55.5 s. | 27 Unified memory |
| `INSTANTTENSOR_MAX_FREE_MEM_USAGE` | unset | Cap on the free memory that InstantTensor may use. | A value set on its own that does not cover the 4 GiB buffer is raised with a warning (#273). | 27 Unified memory |
| `INSTANTTENSOR_MAX_FREE_MEM_USAGE / INSTANTTENSOR_BUFFER_SIZE` | unset (the patch uses a 4 GiB pinned buffer) | InstantTensor overrides for the free-memory fraction and the buffer size. | An insufficient MAX_FREE_MEM_USAGE set on its own is raised (#273), never lowered; the explicit pair is used as given. | 27 Unified memory |
| `KDA_BF16_LARGE_M_CHUNK_ROWS` | 512 | Builds the BF16 copy in blocks of 512 rows (~8 MiB of fp32 scratch). | Controls peak memory at load without changing the result: the e4m3 × bf16 product is exact in fp32. | 04 Numbers in the computer, 27 Unified memory |
| `LOAD_FORMAT (--load-format)` | instanttensor when IMAGE contains 'instanttensor'; empty = vLLM auto | Chooses the weight loader: InstantTensor (direct-I/O O_DIRECT) or vLLM's auto (mmap/safetensors). | Measured (#204): 164 GiB in ~65–70 s cold (&lt;10 s with page cache) vs ~290–300 s with auto. Costs ~4.4–5.6 GiB of KV (12.4–12.5 vs 17–18 GiB at 850k/0.85), hence the fixed pool in EXTRA_ARGS. | 27 Unified memory, 24 Checkpoints |
| `LOAD_FORMAT / --load-format` | instanttensor (:exl3-instanttensor image); empty = vLLM auto | Selects the direct-I/O weight loader (O_DIRECT, pinned ring buffer). | ~65–70 s cold load vs ~290–300 s with auto; leaves ~4.4–5.6 GiB less for KV. | 27 Unified memory, 24 Checkpoints |
| `NCCL_CUMEM_ENABLE` | 0 | Disables NCCL's cuMem (VMM) allocation. | (interpretation) Avoids problematic VMM allocations in the GB10's unified memory. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 27 Unified memory |
| `NFS_SHARE` | 0 (template); kit 1 · 1 = NFSv4 over the ConnectX; rsync as the alternative | With 1, the worker node mounts the head node's HF cache over NFSv4 read-only instead of copying it. · Shares the head node's HF cache with the worker node over NFS in read-only mode. | Avoids a second 164 GiB copy; on TP3 it avoids about 330 GiB of duplicates. · Avoids duplicating 164 GiB; never over the management link. | 27 Unified memory, 24 Checkpoints |
| `PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF` | expandable_segments:True when unset; an explicit empty value disables it | Configures PyTorch's caching allocator with expandable segments. · Configures PyTorch's CUDA allocator with expandable segments. | Fragments memory less. Caveat: the hidden-states KV connector (training windows) refuses expandable_segments. · Reduces memory fragmentation, which matters with a tight budget on UMA. | 27 Unified memory, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `vm.swappiness=0 (swap e livelock UVM)` | swappiness 0; keep swap and recycle it before every start | OS setting that keeps vLLM pages from going to swap. | If that happens, the driver may start spinning: GPU at 96% drawing 12–20 W and /health still returning 200, a silent livelock. | 27 Unified memory |

#### MoE

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--enable-expert-parallel (ENABLE_EXPERT_PARALLEL)` | 1 (TP3) | Each rank owns whole experts (288/3 = 96 per rank) instead of slices. | Avoids slicing the 2048-wide EXL3 trellis by 3; requires loader patches. | 38 Parallelism, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `EXL3_FAT_BATCHED` | 0 | Tier E1: persistent buffers and a GPU→CPU copy of the counts on a side stream, overlapped with the launch of the thin experts. | Hides part of the synchronization wait; requires SUH shared between gate and up. | 28 Kernels, 26 Inside the GPU |
| `EXL3_FAT_EXPERT_LOG` | code default · 1 if the tier is not E3; 0 with E3 | Logs fat-expert statistics. · Emits a per-step routing histogram (exl3 fat-expert P0 line). | Shows the load imbalance across experts in the prefill chunks. · Shows hot experts, but costs 1 host-device synchronization per MoE layer in tier E3. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts, 28 Kernels |
| `EXL3_FAT_GROUPED` | 1 (E3, default since 2026-09-07); 0 = E2 (1M fits again) · 1 (recipe default since 2026-09-07); code default 0 · TP4: not forwarded at 94ae731; now default 1 (e5dc885) | 3 launches per layer (gather, gate/up+SwiGLU, down+scatter) from on-device tables, with no host sync. · Tier E3: 3 launches per layer (gather, gate/up+SwiGLU, down+scatter) driven by on-device segment tables, with no synchronization and capturable in a CUDA graph. | +37–45% cold prefill (isolated MoE layer at 7168 tokens: 77–91 → 31 ms); costs a 560 MiB scratch deducted from the KV. Fail-closed without the kernels; at snapshot 94ae731 TP4 did not forward it (it silently ran E2); since e5dc885 start-tp4.sh forwards it with default 1 (4 Sparks, 1M ctx: cold prefill +35 / +38 / +41% at 8K / 32K / 100K,  | 28 Kernels, 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `EXL3_FAT_GROUPED_TOPK` | 8 | Routing top-k assumed by the E3 path. · Top-k used to size the E3 scratch (MNBT × topk rows). | Must match the model's top-8. · Avoids reallocation in steady-state prefill; growing the scratch inside a graph capture is an error (exl3.py:1119-1123). | 11 Mixture-of-Experts, 28 Kernels |
| `EXL3_FAT_KERNEL` | 1 (E2, PR77) · 1 (.env.example:120) | Direct/scatter kernels for fat experts. · Tier E2 (PR77): direct GEMM over the trellis (exl3_fat_gemm) and GEMM with scatter (exl3_fat_gemm_scatter) for the fat experts. | +20–21% cold prefill: ~8K 941.04 → 1,132.32; ~100K 1,023.20 → 1,241.71; ~300K 995.05 → 1,201.02 tok/s. · +20–21% cold prefill (PR77, 2026-09-01) without reconstructing the weights in BF16. | 28 Kernels, 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `EXL3_FAT_SCRATCH_ROWS` | empty → uses MAX_NUM_BATCHED_TOKENS | Row capacity of the scratch for tiers E1/E2/E3. | Preallocates the scratch; E2's is rounded up to a power of 2 (exl3.py:914-915). | 28 Kernels |
| `EXL3_FAT_SORTED` | 0 | Sorted tier: runs the fat experts from contiguous slices of the sort, with a single counts.tolist() instead of N synchronizations. | Intermediate tier, implied by the higher tiers; checkpoints without shared SUH are limited to it. | 28 Kernels, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `EXL3_FAT_SORTED / EXL3_FAT_BATCHED` | 0 / 0 (implied by the higher tiers) | Older sorted-routing and E1 batched tiers for fat experts. | Subsumed by E2/E3. | 28 Kernels |
| `EXL3_FUSED_MOE` | 1; 0 = per-expert LinearEXL3 loop · 1 (.env.example:108); 0 = Python loop with LinearEXL3 | One exllamav3_ext.exl3_moe launch per layer instead of one per expert. · Uses the fused exllamav3_ext.exl3_moe kernel, with one launch per MoE layer. | The fused kernel is graph-safe and lets the whole decode run in a CUDA graph. · 0 is only for debugging; GLM53_EXL3_MOE_FAST=1 requires 1. | 28 Kernels, 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `EXL3_MOE_ROW_TILE` | 0 · 0; 1 = 128-row slices on the GPU | 128-row tiles for fat experts. · Launches exl3_moe once per 128-row slice of the expert-sorted buffer. | 'Tile (P2a) and TEMP_ROWS=1024 (P2b) lost to MNBT=1024'. · Measured (P2a): −61% to −18% prefill tok/s; kept off. | 28 Kernels |
| `EXL3_TEMP_ROWS_FUSED` | 32 with E3; 256 with E2; TP4 128 at 94ae731 (now 32 with E3 / 256 with E2, e5dc885); tp3-throughput 64 · code 128; launcher 32 with E3, 256 with E2 (start.sh:361-363) | Temporary rows per expert in the fused kernel; experts with more rows become 'fat'. · Number of rows (tokens) per expert that fit in the exl3_moe temporary buffers; experts with more rows are treated as fat. | 'Keep ≥ MAX_NUM_SEQS × (DFLASH_TOKENS+1)' (4×8=32) so decode stays in a single graph-safe launch. 1024 was slower (P2b). The 'keep 128' comment in the .env is outdated. · README rule: keep ≥ MAX_NUM_SEQS × (DFLASH_TOKENS+1) = 4×8 = 32 so decode fits in a single graph-safe launch. Raising it to 1024 made prefill 13–24% slower (P2b). | 28 Kernels, 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `GLM53_COOPERATIVE_MOE` | 0 | Enables install() of the cooperative MoE in direct integrations. | In the standard launcher it does not activate anything on its own; use EXL3_OVERLAY_HOST. | 28 Kernels |
| `MoE: 288 experts roteados, top-8, +1 shared` | 288 / 8 / 1 (from layer 3 onward) | MoE routing configuration of GLM-5.3-Flash. | In a 1024-token chunk the hottest expert shows up in almost every token (max_rows 1024, mean ≈ 920), which motivates the fat tiers E1–E3. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts |

#### Model architecture

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--hf-overrides (TP3_HEAD_OVERRIDE)` | 66 → {"num_attention_heads":66,"num_key_value_heads":66,"linear_num_heads":66,"text_config":{…same…}} | Overrides config.json at runtime to declare 66 heads on TP3. | 64 is not divisible by 3; padding 64→66 gives 22 heads per rank. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 24 Checkpoints |
| `A_log` | 64 (TP3: 64→66 at load) | KDA decay-gate parameter, one per head. | Has to follow the head padding on TP3. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `A_log / dt_bias / lower_bound / beta (KDA)` | gate = lower_bound · sigmoid(exp(A_log) · (g + dt_bias)); FP32 state [heads, 128, 128] | Per-channel gate parameters and delta-rule rate parameters in the KDA layers. | FlashKDA receives the raw gate and beta and applies the bounded gate internally; on TP3, A_log is padded from 64 to 66. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `compress_ratio` | = index_kpool (cross-check) | Indexer compression ratio read by the builder. | The patch refuses to run if index_kpool ≠ compress_ratio, because the rightsize workspace depends on it. | 13 Sparse attention |
| `f_b_proj / g_b_proj` | BF16 on TP3 | KDA projections tied to the gates. | On TP3, Marlin does not accept these projections (pitch not aligned to 8 with 22 heads), so they stay BF16. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids, 28 Kernels |
| `hc_post / hc_contract` | mHC; contracts to hidden 4096 | Hyper-connection outputs: hc_post has to be materialized and contracted (hc_contract) to 4096 before capturing auxiliary hidden states. | The DFlash2/EAGLE3 drafter expects 4096-dim hidden states, not the expanded residual streams. | 15 Hyper-connections, 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `hidden=4096` | 4096 | Width of the residual stream (per mHC stream). | The cooperative MoE and E3 require H = 4096 (hidden % 256); the drafter receives the hidden contracted to 4096. | 07 Transformer block, 15 Hyper-connections |
| `index_kpool` | 4 (pools of 4 compressed tokens) | Indexer compression factor: it scores blocks of 4 tokens instead of individual tokens. · Number of tokens per compressed pool of the sparse indexer. | Widens the buffer to 2176 without a patch; the index_kpool×64 = 256 alignment changes the DeepGEMM block; it is checked against compress_ratio. · Must equal the KV spec's compress_ratio (the builder raises an error if they differ); it also sets the KpoolTail block. | 13 Sparse attention |
| `index_topk` | 2048 | Number of tokens the sparse indexer picks per query. · Top-k width of candidate tokens for sparse attention. | The SM120 kernel only instantiates top-k 2048, which motivates the buffer_width and select_k patches. · It is a template parameter in the SM120 kernels (rejects topk ≠ 2048 for GLM_NSA); the buffer is rounded up to a multiple of 128 (2176 with the tail). | 13 Sparse attention, 28 Kernels |
| `intermediate_size` | 12288 (dense layers 0–2) · 12288 (dense MLP of layers 0–2) | MLP width of the first 3 dense layers. · Width of the dense MLP in the first three layers. | It is a target of GLM53_DENSE_FP8 (dense group) and of the dense_gate_up/dense_down lists. · These layers form the dense group of GLM53_DENSE_FP8. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts, 07 Transformer block |
| `is_causal (config do draft)` | false | DFlash2 checkpoint field that declares bidirectional attention within the block. | The draft block is predicted in parallel; a causal mask drags down acceptance for the later positions. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `kv_lora_rank` | 512 | Dimension of the MLA latent stored in the KV cache. · Dimension of the compressed K/V latent in MLA. | With NoPE this gives d_qk=512; the SM120 kernel requires kv_lora_rank=512 with a 576 query, hence the 64 padding. · rope_pad requires kv_lora_rank == 512 to build the GLM_NSA 576 (512 + 64) geometry. | 12 MLA & compact KV |
| `linear_attention / deepseek_sparse_attention (tipos de camada)` | 34 KDA (0,1,2,4,5,6,8,…) + 11 sparse MLA (3,7,11,…,43 and MTP 45) | 3:1 hybrid scheme of per-layer token mixers. | Creates a KV cache with heterogeneous groups (Mamba, MLA, indexer) and requires aligning state checkpoints. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids, 18 The three models |
| `moe_intermediate_size` | 2048 (288 routed experts, top-8, 1 shared) | Intermediate width of each expert. · Width of each routed expert and of the shared expert. | Not padded on TP3 (the EXL3 trellis is packed at 2048), hence the use of expert parallelism. · At TP2 it becomes 1024 locally; on TP3 it is not divisible by 3, so the routed experts use EP and the shared expert is padded 2048 → 2112. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `num_attention_heads / num_key_value_heads / linear_num_heads` | 64 / 64 / 64 (TP3 override 66) | MLA and KDA attention heads. | 64 is divisible by 2 and 4 but not by 3: the basis for the 64→66 padding on TP3. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 06 Attention |
| `num_hidden_layers` | 45 (+ MTP block at layer 45; 46 entries in LAYER_MAP.json); 34 KDA + 11 NoPE sparse MLA (3,7,11,…,43) · 45 (+ MTP block at index 45) | Number of body layers and hybrid pattern (3 KDA : 1 MLA). · Number of decoder layers in GLM-5.3-Flash. | Defines the KV groups (MLA 11 layers, Mamba/KDA 34) and the EAGLE3 taps. The README says 'MLA 12 layers', counting the MTP. · The MTP block at index 45 is used with SPEC_METHOD=mtp and is also edited by ABLIT. | 18 The three models, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `qk_rope_head_dim` | 0 (NoPE) | RoPE dimensions of the MLA head; GLM uses 0 (no positional encoding in the MLA layers). · RoPE dimension of the MLA heads; 0 means the MLA layers do not use RoPE. | Breaks the kernel's assumption: the stock image dies on the first forward pass with 'pe_dim must be 64 for fp8_ds_mla'. · Breaks kernel assumptions (pe_dim must be 64 for fp8_ds_mla), worked around with a pad of 64 zeros. | 12 MLA & compact KV, 08 Position & RoPE |
| `select_k` | 512 pools (+ tail ≤3) → up to 2051 candidates · 512 pools (the overlay uses 511 in the expansion) | Number of pools selected by the indexer. · Number of pools the indexer picks for each query. | 512×4 + tail exceeds 2048; the overlay expands 511 pools (2044) + a tail of 3 = 2047. · 512 × 4 + a tail of 3 = 2051 exceeds the 2048 template; the overlay expands 511 pools but keeps select_k = 512 for the fused top-k fast path. | 13 Sparse attention |
| `SWIGLU_LIMIT_DEFAULT` | 10.0 | SwiGLU clamp applied in the EXL3 MoE (the E3 kernel fuses gate/up + SwiGLU). | Must match the reference to preserve numerical parity. | 07 Transformer block |
| `SWIGLU_LIMIT_DEFAULT / swiglu_limit` | 10.0 | SwiGLU clamp: silu(min(g,limit)) * clamp(u,-limit,limit), applied inside the MoE kernels. | It is part of the model's semantics; the TP3 cooperative kernel only accepts a limit of 10 (0.5 failed the screen). | 07 Transformer block, 28 Kernels |
| `vocab (154.880)` | 154,880; TP3 padded to 154,944 | Vocabulary size of GLM-5.3-Flash. | It is a multiple of 64 (allows an EXL3 lm_head); on TP3, padding_size = lcm(64, 3) = 192 takes it to 154,944. | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |

#### Multimodal

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--language-model-only (LANGUAGE_MODEL_ONLY)` | 0 (vision on); 1 turns the tower off | Loads only the language model, without the vision tower. | Without vision, more memory is left over in UMA. | 17 Multimodal |
| `--limit-mm-per-prompt (LIMIT_MM)` | {"image":48,"video":1} (history 4 → 100 → 48); TP4 script {"image":100,"video":1} | Per-prompt media ceiling; it is validation only, nothing is reserved. | Worst case 48×2048 = 98k tokens; above that the response is HTTP 500 'At most N image(s)…' instead of a dead engine. Stateless clients resend every image, so the session gets 'poisoned' once it passes the ceiling. | 17 Multimodal |
| `--media-io-kwargs video.num_frames (VIDEO_NUM_FRAMES)` | empty (vLLM 32) | Number of frames sampled per video. | Each frame becomes thousands of tokens; limiting frames limits the encode cost. | 17 Multimodal |
| `--mm-encoder-tp-mode (MM_ENCODER_TP_MODE)` | TP3 data; empty = weights | TP mode of the vision tower: data replicates the encoder and splits the images. | The encoder dimensions are not divisible by 3. | 38 Parallelism, 17 Multimodal |
| `--mm-processor-cache-gb (MM_PROCESSOR_CACHE_GB)` | 1 (vLLM 4); 0 disables it | RAM cache of already processed media. | On UMA, the default's 4 GiB would be 4 GiB less for the model. | 17 Multimodal, 27 Unified memory |
| `--mm-processor-kwargs max_image_tokens (MM_IMAGE_TOKENS)` | 2048; empty = the checkpoint's 8000 | Per-image token ceiling. | 1080p frame: 2691 tokens uncapped, 2040 at 2048, 1008 at 1024. 8000 exceeds MNBT 7168, which is the size of the encoder cache. Incident: an 11.9 MB video → 236,544 encode tokens and an OOM kill of the worker. | 17 Multimodal |
| `LIMIT_MM / --limit-mm-per-prompt` | {image: 48, video: 1} | Limit on images and videos per prompt. | An 11.9 MB video turned into 236,544 tokens and killed the worker (2026-09-14); nothing is reserved for the vision tower. | 17 Multimodal |
| `MM_ENCODER_TP_MODE / --mm-encoder-tp-mode` | data (TP3) | Runs the vision tower in data-parallel mode. | The vision tower does not split across 3 ranks. | 38 Parallelism, 17 Multimodal |
| `MM_IMAGE_TOKENS / --mm-processor-kwargs max_image_tokens` | 2048 | Per-image token ceiling in the multimodal processor. | A 1080p frame yields 2691 tokens uncapped, 2040 with 2048 and 1008 with 1024; 33 frames = 67,320 tokens. | 17 Multimodal |
| `MM_PROCESSOR_CACHE_GB / --mm-processor-cache-gb` | 1 | Size of the multimodal processor cache. | Keeps the multimodal memory cost on UMA small. | 17 Multimodal, 27 Unified memory |
| `SKIP_MM_PROFILING (--skip-mm-profiling)` | 1 | Skips the dummy profiling run with a maximum-size image+video. | Worst-case profiling OOMs on this UMA. Consequence: nothing is reserved for the vision tower, so the MM ceilings must be tight. | 17 Multimodal, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `SKIP_MM_PROFILING / --skip-mm-profiling` | 1 | Skips multimodal profiling at startup. | Nothing is reserved for the vision tower, which leaves memory for KV; that is why the per-prompt limits are the safeguard. | 17 Multimodal, 31 PagedAttention & KV |

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `_FAT_BUCKET_EDGES` | (16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048) | Bucket edges of the histogram of max rows per expert. | Diagnoses how load is distributed across experts during prefill. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `CACHE_ROOT / VLLM_CACHE_ROOT` | ~/.cache/vllm-glm53-flash → /root/.cache/vllm | Host vLLM cache mounted into the container (compilation, adaptive-k file). | Persisting the cache across boots shortens startup time. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `CONTAINER_HEAD / CONTAINER_WORKER` | glm53-exl3-head / glm53-exl3-worker; TP3/TP4: glm53-exl3-tp3-* / -tp4-* | Container names for each rank. | TP3 and TP4 use their own names so they neither reuse nor destroy the TP2 stack. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `cooperative_moe .so SHA256` | aa3fe5e9… | Pinned hash of the cooperative MoE native binary; the runtime refuses an unvalidated .so. | A rebuild with another toolchain changes the hash and requires numerical requalification. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `EXL3_FAT_DIAG_SCHEMA` | 2 | Contract of the exl3 e2 diag diagnostic line (configured and effective tier, symbols, capability, counters). | Fixed keys used by the boot check; any change requires a schema bump. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `EXPECTED_SHARDS` | 94ae731: 120 · now 83 (98cf18f) | Number of shards for the download to count as complete; pinned presets impose their own value. | Detects a partial download before trying to load. The TensorFold pack has 83 shards (~164 GiB); TR3 had 120. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `FLASHINFER_DISABLE_VERSION_CHECK` | 1 | Disables FlashInfer's version check. | Part of the containers' fixed environment; the inventory does not detail the reason. | 28 Kernels |
| `GHCR_TOKEN / GHCR_USER` | unset / MiaAI-Lab | Optional login to the GitHub Container Registry. | Only needed because of the anonymous pull rate limit. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `GLM53_COOP_QUALIFICATION / GLM53_COOP_MAINTENANCE_TEST / GLM53_COOP_BUNDLE / GLM53_COOP_EP_RANK / GLM53_COOP_TEST_HELPERS` | empty | Maintenance and qualification modes of the TP3 cooperative runtime. | Only in a reserved window, never alongside production. | 25 Measuring quality, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `GLM53_EXTRA_ENV` | empty; 'NAME=VALUE …' (e.g., VLLM_DEBUG_WORKSPACE=1, VLLM_LOGGING_LEVEL=DEBUG) | Extra diagnostic env applied on both ranks. | Rejects the launcher's own names (NCCL_*, HF_*, GLM53_*, EXL3_*, FLASHINFER_*, PATH…) and never logs the values. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `GLM53_VALIDATE_PYTHON` | unset → tries python3, python3.12, python3.11, /usr/bin/python3 | Interpreter with jinja2 used to validate the template. | If set, it is the only candidate (fail-closed). | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `GLM53_WARMUP_MAX_CONCURRENCY / _DFLASH_K / _TRITON_CACHE_DIR / _BEARER / WARMUP_CURL` | MAX_NUM_SEQS / DFLASH_TOKENS / host Triton / VLLM_API_KEY / test seam | Warmup script parameters. Formula: BLOCK_SIZE = min(256, next_pow2(scheduled_tokens + 1 + k)). | 'BLOCK 8 is unreachable': the ladder must hit exactly the BLOCKs that serving will use. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `GLM53_WARMUP_REQ_TIMEOUT` | 240 s | Per-request warmup timeout. | Bounds hung boots without aborting legitimate compilations. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `HF_DOWNLOAD_EXCLUDE` | runtime-results/**,src/**,runtime/src/**,scripts/**,docs/**,results/**,.materialization/**,runtime/scripts/** | Patterns excluded from the download. | Skips the extra files in the HF repository that are not weights. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `HF_HOME / HF_TOKEN / HF_BIN` | ~/.cache/huggingface (container /root/.cache/huggingface) / optional / optional | Host HF cache, access token and download CLI (fallback python3 -m huggingface_hub.commands.huggingface_cli). | The cache is mounted into the container (or exported via NFS); the token is only needed for gated repositories. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `HF_HUB_OFFLINE / TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE` | 1 / 1 | The container never downloads anything from the Hub. | Downloads stay in the launcher (pinned). This makes it possible to mount the HF cache read-only via NFS on the worker. | 24 Checkpoints, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `IMG / DRAFT_SNAP / BUILD / OUT` | see the script | Environment variables for the drafter conversion (image, snapshot, build and output directories). | They isolate the conversion in a reproducible environment. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `KDA_LARGE_M_STATS_PATH` | empty | Path for a JSON dump of the bf16/marlin counters every 512 calls. | Dispatch observability; does not count CUDA graph replays. | 28 Kernels |
| `MODEL_CACHE_NAME / MODEL_FALLBACK_CACHE_NAME` | models--&lt;org&gt;--&lt;repo&gt; | Folder name in the HF cache for the target and the fallback. | Avoids downloading 164 GiB again when the kit already has the upstream. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `MODEL_FALLBACK` | 94ae731: brandonmusic/GLM-5.3-Flash-tr3-4bpw · now the same repository as MODEL (98cf18f) | At the snapshot, the repository used if the mirror returned 404 or had fewer than 120 shards. Since 98cf18f it is Mia-AiLab/GLM-5.3-Flash-EXL3-4bpw-TensorFold itself: point it at another id only to adopt a complete cache that is already on disk. | It kept the download possible if the mirror disappeared; with Mia's own pack as the default, the fallback to the TR3 upload is gone. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `MODEL_REVISION` | 94ae731: 25a44fdbf16862a46b7cc9921142c6c81350af2f · now 76c0b5173166d2795dd48860f45d8224817f894c (98cf18f) | Pinned commit of the weights: at the snapshot, of the TR3 mirror; now, of the EXL3 4 bpw TensorFold pack. | Pinning the revision makes the served model immutable. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `NCCL_DEBUG` | WARN; INFO for diagnostics | Verbosity of the NCCL logs. | INFO shows the chosen transport, HCA and GID, which helps diagnose fallback to TCP and GID errors. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NFS_CLIENTS` | 10.0.0.2,10.0.0.3,10.0.22.0/24,10.0.23.0/24,10.0.33.0/24 | NFS export ACL. | It must include the CX7 fabric subnets so the mount goes over the fast cable. | 27 Unified memory |
| `NFS_DEVICE` | :/ | The export root is $HF_CACHE_DIR. | The worker sees hub/&lt;repo&gt;/snapshots/&lt;rev&gt; at the same path as the head. | 27 Unified memory |
| `NFS_OPTS` | ro,sync,no_subtree_check,no_root_squash,insecure,fsid=0 | Export options of the NFS server. | Read-only export of the HF cache root. | 27 Unified memory |
| `NFS_OPTS_CLIENT` | nfsvers=4.2,ro,nconnect=8,rsize=1048576,wsize=1048576,hard,timeo=600 | NFS client mount options. | 'nconnect=8 is what makes a cold load use the cable': several parallel TCP connections saturate the CX7. | 27 Unified memory |
| `NFS_SERVER_IP_{rank} / WORKER_FABRIC_IP` | autodetected via ip route get; TP3 kit 10.0.22.1/10.0.23.1 | The head's CX7 address that each rank mounts. | 10.0.0.* and 127.* are refused: mounting over the management network 'turns a 164 GiB load into an overnight job'. | 27 Unified memory, 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NFS_VOLUME / NFS_CONTAINER / NFS_IMAGE` | glm53-weights / glm53-nfs / glm53-nfs:local | Docker volume and NFS exporter container. | Reuses a live exporter (vllm-fn-nfs, glm53fp8-nfs, dsv41-nfs) and merges the client lists. | 27 Unified memory |
| `PIN (conversor exllamav3)` | MiaAI-Lab/exllamav3@63b32f0 (v1.4.2) | Pinned fork of the EXL3 converter used to quantize the drafter. | Conversion reproducibility; the fork supports DFlash2 with uncalibrated_quantize. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `PYTHONFAULTHANDLER` | 1 (TP3) | Makes Python dump tracebacks on fatal errors (extra TP3 variable). | (interpretation) Helps diagnose hangs and native crashes in the 3-node topology. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `READY_TIMEOUT / VLLM_ENGINE_READY_TIMEOUT_S` | 3600 s | Timeout for the /health wait and for engine readiness. | Loading and warming up a '320B' MoE is slow; short timeouts would kill valid boots. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `SKIP_DOWNLOAD / REFRESH_WEIGHTS` | 0 / 0 | Skips the download check or forces a fresh fetch of the weights. | Controls when the launcher trusts the local cache. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `SKIP_SYNC / FORCE_SYNC` | 0 / 0 | Skips the rsync to the worker or ignores the per-revision .glm53-exl3-synced marker. The worker is verified shard by shard by dereferenced size. | The sync marker is 'claim, not proof': the size check catches truncated copies. | 24 Checkpoints, 27 Unified memory |
| `TAIL` | 0; 1 follows the logs after READY | Makes the launcher follow the container logs. | It is an operational convenience. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `TRITON_CACHE_DIR / TILELANG_CACHE_DIR` | /root/.triton/cache / /root/.tilelang/cache | JIT cache directories inside the container, mounted from the host. | Avoids recompiling the Triton/TileLang kernels on every boot. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 28 Kernels |
| `TRITON_HOST_CACHE / TILELANG_HOST_CACHE / NV_HOST_CACHE` | $CACHE_ROOT/{triton,tilelang,nv} (includes ~/.nv/ComputeCache) | Host-side persistence of the JIT caches and NVIDIA's ComputeCache. | Without it, DFlash2 re-JITed a 31 MiB cubin on every boot (30 s → 0 s). | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 26 Inside the GPU |
| `VLLM_DEBUG_WORKSPACE` | diagnostics (via GLM53_EXTRA_ENV) · 1 (measurement) | Turns on debug logs for vLLM's workspaces. · vLLM variable used to measure the size of the reserved workspace. | Helps find workspaces that eat into the KV budget (e.g., indexer). · This is how the repo measured the 5,036.40 MiB of the indexer's stock workspace at 1M. | 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `VLLM_NO_USAGE_STATS / DO_NOT_TRACK` | 1 | Turns off vLLM's usage telemetry. | On Grace/aarch64, py-cpuinfo JSON-parses an empty output and crashes. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `vm.swappiness=0 (+ ciclar swap)` | swappiness 0; swap kept and cycled before start | Configures the system to avoid swapping, keeping swap enabled and cycling it before starting. | Paging of vLLM memory can cause a UVM livelock (96% util at 12–20 W with /health still returning 200). | 27 Unified memory |
| `WORKER_USER / WORKER_HOME / WORKER_SSH` | unset → $USER; $HOME or /home/$WORKER_USER; ${WORKER_USER}@${WORKER_IP} | Account, home (where the HF cache lives) and SSH target used on the worker. | Kits with different accounts per node (kit: zurih); the launcher relies on passwordless SSH in BatchMode. | 42 GLM recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |

#### Prefix caching

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--enable-prefix-caching` | always · on | Turns on Automatic Prefix Caching (hashing of the prompt's full blocks and reuse). · Turns on vLLM's Automatic Prefix Caching. | Stateless conversations resend the history: a ~8k follow-up reuses 7168/8004 ≈ 90% (TTFT 8.53 → 1.30 s). · Hits aligned to 3584 tokens (MLA/Mamba manager block); ~8k follow-up: TTFT 1.30 s vs 8.53 s cold. | 33 Prefix caching |
| `cache_salt` | per-request string · stock vLLM; unique salt in the cold-prefill benchmarks | Cache namespace: requests with different salts do not share prefixes, but still store them. · Per-request salt that isolates the prefix hash. | Isolates tenants and benchmarks without turning off APC. · It isolates but still writes blocks, so it does not protect the resident conversation; in the benchmarks, a unique salt keeps APC from masking the cold prefill. | 33 Prefix caching, 45 Benchmarking |
| `GLM53_APC_NO_STORE` | 1 (honors the client flag); 0 ignores it · 1; 0 ignores the client flag | Allows a per-request opt-out of writing to the prefix cache. · Kill switch for the per-request no-store. | Batches and evaluations do not reorder the LRU against the owner's conversation. Accepts 1, "1", true; other values return HTTP 400. · Only accepts 0/1; other values fail at boot (fail-closed). | 33 Prefix caching |
| `GLM53_APC_RETENTION_INTERVAL` | unset (dense grid); multiple of 3584 ≤ 1,000,000; long-coding 14336 | Becomes the global VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL: stores a Mamba/KDA state every interval (full attention stays dense). | More history fits in the same pool, but edits and branches recompute back to the previous checkpoint. | 33 Prefix caching, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `GLM53_APC_RETENTION_INTERVAL → VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL` | empty (dense grid) | Global retention interval for hashed blocks in the prefix cache. | More capacity, but a coarser hit granularity; it coarsened the Mamba/MLA grid (subagent hits dropped to 45%). | 33 Prefix caching |
| `GLM53_APC_RETENTION_INTERVAL_SWA` | empty (inherits); 0 (TP3/TP4 templates, long-coding); multiple of 3584; requires SPEC_METHOD=dflash | Becomes VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL_SWA: retention for the drafter's SWA group; 0 = reachable boundaries only + low eviction priority for draft-only blocks. | Keeps 33 of each segment's 38 IDs (drafter) from pushing MLA/Mamba into eviction. 0/0: 4 histories of 210K revisited in ~2.5 s; however, an edit at 90% of 128K takes 112.49 s vs 14.70 s. | 33 Prefix caching, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `GLM53_APC_RETENTION_INTERVAL_SWA → VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL_SWA` | empty = inherits the global; 0 = reachable boundaries only + low eviction priority for the drafter; N multiple of 3584 ≤ 1,000,000 | Per-group retention applied only to the drafter's SWA group. | 0/0: 4 histories of 210k reused in ~2.5 s, but an edit at 90% of 128k went from 14.70 s to 112.49 s. | 33 Prefix caching |
| `GLM53_EXPOSE_CACHE_RESET` | 0; 1 (TP2 only) | Mounts only /reset_prefix_cache, /reset_mm_cache and /reset_encoder_cache. · Mounts only the cache reset router (/reset_prefix_cache, /reset_mm_cache, /reset_encoder_cache). | Meant for cold benchmarks on an isolated kit. Root routes sit outside the Bearer guard (GUARDED_PREFIX=/v1,/v2,/inference,/cohere). · Root routes sit outside the bearer guard; that is why it is opt-in and does not expose /sleep, /rlhf or /rpc. | 33 Prefix caching, 45 Benchmarking |
| `ids por segmento de 3584 (38 = 1 MLA + 4 Mamba + 33 drafter)` | 38 | Cost in block ids of retaining one aligned segment with dense retention. | 87% of the ids go to the drafter, which does not contribute to the hit; this motivates per-group retention. | 33 Prefix caching, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `PREFIX_MATCH_UNIT` | empty (vLLM resolves it); 64 supported; 512 invalid | Granularity of the prefix hash. | A finer granularity does not create Mamba checkpoints that don't exist: hits remain limited to the aligned state points. | 33 Prefix caching, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `skip_reading_prefix_cache` | stock vLLM opt-out (read only) | Existing vLLM opt-out that only avoids reading the prefix cache. | It does not prevent writing, which is why the repo created skip_writing_prefix_cache. | 33 Prefix caching |
| `skip_writing_prefix_cache` | per request via vllm_xargs: 1, "1" or true | The request reads the prefix cache but does not insert hashes; its blocks go back to the front of the free queue (LIFO). | Batch/eval jobs do not displace the server owner's resident conversation. | 33 Prefix caching, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `skip_writing_prefix_cache (vllm_xargs)` | {"skip_writing_prefix_cache": 1} per request | Request field that tells the server not to write its blocks' hashes to the prefix cache; the blocks go to the front of the free queue. | Recommended for batches and evals, so they do not evict the conversations' cache. | 33 Prefix caching |

#### Quantization

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--quantization exl3 (QUANTIZATION)` | exl3; never marlin | Selects the EXL3 quantization method registered by the overlay. | Do not pass --moe-backend marlin (that is for NVFP4): the experts are EXL3 trellis. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `--quantization exl3 / QUANTIZATION` | exl3 (.env.example:103); never marlin | Selects the exl3 quantization method, registered in vLLM by the overlay, to load the routed experts in packed trellis format. | Without the registration, vLLM would expand or reject the checkpoint; --moe-backend marlin is the NVFP4 workaround and is wrong here. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `bits` | 4 (accepts 3,4,5,6 — exl3.py:1726) | Bits per weight of the trellis (K); defines k_words = bits*16 int16 words per 16×16 tile. | K4 = 64 words per tile; the E2, E3 and cooperative kernels only support K4 with the MCG codebook. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 24 Checkpoints |
| `BITS (dflash2_exl3_quant.sh)` | 6.0 | bpw of the DFlash2 drafter quantized to EXL3. | Defines the drafter of the H3 pair (dense-exl3-h3-dflash2-6bpw). | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `codebook` | mcg only for experts (exl3.py:1722); mul1 accepted only in EXL3 dense layers | MCG procedural codebook (multiplier 0xCBAC1FED) that turns the trellis's 16-bit states into fp16 values. | The overlay rejects other codebooks for experts; mul1 (0x83DCD12D) only applies to turboderp's EXL3 dense layers. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `codebooks EXL3 cb0 / cb1 / cb2` | cb0 = 3INST (×89226354 + 64248484); cb1 = MCG (×0xCBAC1FED); cb2 = mul1 (×0x83DCD12D, byte sum via dp4a) | Three families of procedural decoding from 16-bit states to fp16. | Experts use MCG, turboderp's dense layers use mul1; the specialized kernels only support K4 MCG. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `dense_overlay.py flags (--branch, --prefix-rewrite, --lm-head, --draft-layers)` | --branch 4.05bpw; --prefix-rewrite model.language_model.:language_model.model.; --lm-head optional; --draft-layers 45 (MTP) | Choose the donor branch, rewrite tensor-name prefixes, and include the lm_head and the MTP layer in the dense pack. | The donor's and the loader's names differ; the EXL3 lm_head is only possible because 154,880 is a multiple of 64. | 24 Checkpoints, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `DRAFT_QUANT_FC` | 1 | Quantizes the drafter's fc (input projection of the target's hidden states). | Counts toward the bit budget; can be turned off. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `DRAFT_QUANT_KERNEL_PROJ` | 1 | Quantizes the kernel_projection of the drafter's dynamic convolution. | Counts toward the bit budget; can be turned off to keep this projection in BF16. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `GLM53_DENSE_EXL3` | 0; 1 requires TP=2, GLM53_DENSE_FP8=off, ABLIT=0 · 0; 1 = serves a pack with non_routed_exl3 | Runs the dense projections in EXL3 (pack marked non_routed_exl3). · Enables Exl3LinearMethod for the dense linears declared by the pack. | A pack with non_routed_exl3 and the flag at 0 is rejected, so that weights are not served with the wrong method. · A pack with non_routed_exl3 and the flag at 0 is rejected, because the replaced BF16 weights do not exist in the index. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 24 Checkpoints |
| `GLM53_DENSE_FP8` | .env.example all; unset in start.sh off; TP3 dense,kda; tp3-throughput dense,kda,shared,mla; long-coding dense,kda; groups shared,dense,kda,mla · .env.example:158 = all; absent = o | At load time, quantizes the non-routed dense projections to weight-only FP8 per output channel and runs them via Marlin. · Quantizes the target's dense projections at load time (per output channel, e4m3, amax/448 scale) and runs them with Marlin W8A16. | dense,kda: ~−11 ms/step (+10% across the board; prose +12–19%). all vs dense,kda (2026-09-26): ms/cycle −4.7/−4.9%, tok/s +4–5%, NOT-WORSE on kl_panel; KL proxy vs stock 0.002–0.013 nats/position. TP3: Marlin does not accept KDA's f_b_proj/g_b_proj, which stay BF16. · ~11 ms less per step; all vs dense,kda: −4.67%/−4.91% ms/cycle and +4–5 | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 28 Kernels |
| `GLM53_DENSE_FP8 grupos (shared, dense, kda, mla)` | shared: mlp.shared_experts.{gate_up_proj,down_proj}; dense: mlp.{gate_up_proj,down_proj} of layers 0–2; kda: self_attn.{in_proj_qkvbfg_a,f_b_proj,g_b_proj,o_proj}; mla: self_attn.{ | Defines which dense modules become FP8 in each GLM53_DENSE_FP8 group. | kv_b_proj stays BF16 because MLA weight absorption reads the weight directly; the drafter, MTP and vision are never converted. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 12 MLA & compact KV |
| `glm53_profile (tools/pack_profile.py)` | name=dense-exl3-h3-dflash2-6bpw, draft.model, draft.revision (40-hex), draft.config_sha256 | Pack metadata that on its own selects GLM53_DENSE_EXL3=1, GLM53_DENSE_FP8=off, …PREFILL_BF16, GLM53_KDA_BF16_LARGE_M=0, ABLIT=0, SPEC_METHOD=dflash, DFLASH_DRAFT_TP=2. | 5-layer draft, 4096 wide, 6 bpw EXL3 on q/o/MLP/conv/fc, BF16 K/V (bf16_shards=[1,2]). Not measured in an A/B against the defaults. | 24 Checkpoints, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `Hadamard 128 (escala 1/sqrt(128))` | blocks of 128; 0.088388347648 | Incoherence rotation applied at the input (x ⊙ suh) and at the output (⊙ svh) of the EXL3 GEMMs. | Spreads outliers before quantization; in the kernels it is done via butterflies and shuffles across lanes. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 28 Kernels |
| `MCG_MULTIPLIER / MCG_MARKER_SIGNED_INT32` | 0xCBAC1FED / -877912083 | MCG codebook constants; the marker is checked on each expert during loading. | If the checkpoint is not MCG, loading fails with packed ABI mismatch instead of producing numerical garbage. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `Mia-AiLab/GLM-5.3-Flash-EXL3-TR3-4bpw` | byte-identical mirror of brandonmusic/GLM-5.3-Flash-tr3-4bpw; snapshot 5ab363a8…; ~164 GiB; 120 shards · default only up to 94ae731; since 98cf18f the vLLM lane serves Mia-AiLab/GL | Target checkpoint at snapshot 94ae731 (no longer the default in either recipe), with the routed experts in EXL3 4 bpw and the rest in BF16. | KLD 0.024555 ≈ official FP8 (0.024629) with 54% of the bytes; TR3 K6 0.013723 (254 GB); NVFP4 0.060535. These KLDs are TR3 measurements; they do not carry over to the new pack automatically. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 24 Checkpoints |
| `MODEL` | 94ae731: Mia-AiLab/GLM-5.3-Flash-EXL3-TR3-4bpw · now Mia-AiLab/GLM-5.3-Flash-EXL3-4bpw-TensorFold@76c0b517 (98cf18f) | At snapshot 94ae731, the target repository: a byte-identical mirror of brandonmusic/GLM-5.3-Flash-tr3-4bpw (snapshot 5ab363a8…), uniform EXL3/TR3 K4 on the routed experts only, ~164 GiB in 120 shards. Since 2026-10-04 (98cf18f) the default is Mia's own EXL3 4 bpw pack, Mia-AiLab/ | A durable mirror prevents changes on the Hub from altering the served model. The rest (attention, shared experts, dense layers, embeddings, lm_head) stays BF16. On the TensorFold lane (revision 078455ff, CHANGELOG v1.3.3) the new pack measured 2–18% lower KL than TR3 against Z.AI's official FP8 on all six test sets; the vLLM lane pins ano | 24 Checkpoints, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `MUL1_MARKER_SIGNED_INT32` | -2082680531 (0x83DCD12D) | Marker of the mul1 codebook used by the EXL3 dense layers (turboderp). | Requires exactly one marker per shard, protecting the dense pack against mixed codebooks. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `preset dense-h3` | TR3 config SHA-256 4f5341e0…, index 2f64d21c…; turboderp/GLM-5.3-Flash-exl3 branch 4.05bpw rev 2a30229e…; overlay 1a1b0793…; draft incoai/…DFlash2@dc77ff1c… → local/GLM-5.3-Flash-D | Builds, on the head node, the dense-EXL3 target + 6 bpw draft pair. | The builds are byte-reproducible; a divergent build is rejected. | 24 Checkpoints, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `quant_method` | exl3 | quantization_config field that vLLM uses to resolve which quantization method loads the checkpoint. | It is what binds the EXL3 checkpoint to Exl3Config; without the registered method, vLLM does not recognize the format. | 24 Checkpoints, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `QUANTIZATION exl3 – trellis / suh / svh / mcg` | trellis int16 + suh/svh fp16 + mcg int32 (marker); MCG codebook (0xCBAC1FED) or mul1 | Tensors of each EXL3 expert: trellis paths, suh/svh vectors and the codebook marker; one fused exllamav3_ext.exl3_moe launch per layer. | (interpretation) suh/svh are the sign vectors of the input/output Hadamard rotations, and mcg is the computed codebook. 4 bpw gives KLD 0.024555 ≈ official FP8 (0.024629) with 54% of the bytes. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 25 Measuring quality |
| `quantization_config.non_routed_exl3.layers` | {prefix: {bits: K, bf16_shards: [...]}} | Declares which dense modules are EXL3 and which output shards stay BF16 (as a contiguous tail). | Enables per-shard mixed precision; kv_b_proj is forbidden because of MLA absorption. | 24 Checkpoints, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `scope` | glm53_routed_experts_only | quantization_config field documenting that only the routed experts are quantized in EXL3. | Everything else (attention, KDA, shared, dense MLP, embeddings, lm_head) stays in native BF16, which makes it possible to apply FP8 or abliteration at load time. | 20 Quantization fundamentals, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |
| `tensor_storage (ledger)` | ~37 MiB; discarded by the overlay | Tensor-storage ledger present in the checkpoint's quantization_config, removed from the config object by the overlay. | Avoids carrying 37 MiB of useless metadata in vLLM's configuration object. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `trellis / suh / svh / mcg` | trellis int16 [K/16, N/16, 16*bits]; suh fp16 (input); svh fp16 (output); mcg int32 0xCBAC1FED = -877912083 | The 4 tensors of each EXL3 expert matrix: the trellis bitstream, the input and output sign/scale vectors and the codebook marker. | They explain k_words = 64 and why the kernels dequantize in registers without reconstructing BF16; suh/svh are the scales of the Hadamard rotations. | 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3, 24 Checkpoints |
| `uncalibrated_quantize (caps)` | True for DFlash2 | Converter capability that quantizes against a synthetic Hessian, without calibration data. | Makes it possible to quantize the drafter without assembling a calibration set. | 20 Quantization fundamentals, 22 Hadamard, trellises & EXL3 |

#### Reasoning / tools

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--chat-template` | /opt/glm53/chat_template.jinja | GLM chat template included in the image. | Controls reasoning and the reasoning effort (a word at character 39 of the prompt). | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--default-chat-template-kwargs (GLM53_DEFAULT_REASONING_EFFORT)` | omitted; low/high/max (medium rejected) | Sets the default reasoning_effort by passing {"reasoning_effort":"…"} to the template. | The template maps any value outside low/high to max. | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--enable-auto-tool-choice` | always · on | Lets the model decide when to call tools (tool_choice auto). · Allows the model to decide to call tools (tool_choice auto). | Agentic clients depend on it. · Required for agentic clients that send tools. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--exclude-tools-when-tool-choice-none` | not used (rejected) | vLLM flag that removes the tools from the prompt when tool_choice is none. | Rejected because it changes the prompt and destroys the shared prefix of the prefix cache. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 33 Prefix caching |
| `--reasoning-parser glm45` | glm45 | Splits &lt;think&gt;…&lt;/think&gt; into the reasoning field. · Parser that separates the reasoning (&lt;think&gt;) from the final content. | Also keeps the tool_choice:none hook alive. · Delivers the reasoning in a separate field and is the basis for the stop and grammar patches. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `--tool-call-parser glm47` | glm47 | Converts tool calls in GLM's XML format (&lt;tool_call&gt;name&lt;arg_key&gt;…) into OpenAI tool_calls. · Tool-call parser for the GLM format. | Required for agents and tools. · Its adjust_request is the hook used to mask the tool-call start with tool_choice none. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `chat_template_kwargs.clear_thinking` | true for multi-turn agents | Replaces the reasoning of old turns with &lt;think&gt;&lt;/think&gt;. | Trims context in long sessions. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `chat_template_kwargs.enable_thinking (ou thinking)` | on by default; false closes &lt;think&gt;&lt;/think&gt; | Turns reasoning on or off per request. | 'Prefer low-effort thinking over thinking off for long structured output': off corrupted 11/12 answers with tables (#257); true + low → 0/6 corrupted, 791/792 numbers correct. | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `GLM53_DEFAULT_REASONING_EFFORT / --default-chat-template-kwargs` | empty (template → Max); low / high / max; medium rejected | Sets the default reasoning effort through the chat template kwargs. | The effort word sits at character 39 of the prompt: changing it per request breaks the prefix cache. | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `GLM53_SUPPRESS_STOPS_IN_REASONING` | 1; 0 | The client's stop strings stay dormant until &lt;/think&gt;. | Avoids ending mid-reasoning with empty content (DSpark #42). | 34 API, tools & JSON, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `GLM53_SUPPRESS_STOPS_IN_REASONING / VLLM_SUPPRESS_STOPS_IN_REASONING` | 1 | Keeps the client's stop strings dormant until the end of reasoning (&lt;/think&gt;). | The CoT repeats harness stops (e.g., Question:) and ended the request with empty content (DSpark #42). | 34 API, tools & JSON, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `reasoning (campo de resposta)` | reasoning, not reasoning_content | Field in which the glm45 parser returns the reasoning. | Clients that read reasoning_content 'lose' the reasoning. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `reasoning_effort` | low / high / max (per request) | Per-request reasoning effort, injected into the chat template. | Thinking off on long tables corrupted 11/12 answers; reasoning_effort low with thinking on: 0/6 corrupted and 791/792 numbers correct (#257). | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `reasoning_effort (cliente)` | low/high; absent → Max | Inserts the line &lt;\|system\|&gt;Reasoning Effort: … (only with thinking). | Do not vary it per request within a conversation: the line falls at character 39 of the prompt and causes a total prefix-cache miss. | 09 Generation & sampling, 33 Prefix caching |
| `tool_choice: none` | with declared tools | Enforces 'no tool call' by adding &lt;tool_call&gt; to bad_words. | Keeps the tools in the prompt (prefix shared with the APC) without generating a tool call; the masking also acts on the draft positions. | 34 API, tools & JSON |

#### Sampling / generation

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `generation_config (top_p=0.95)` | top_p=0.95 injected | The model's generation_config injects top_p = 0.95 into requests. | It changes which sampler kernels are used; that is why the warmup burns in top_k-only and top_p-only variants. | 09 Generation & sampling, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `generation_config.json (temperature, top_p)` | temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95 | Sampling defaults from the Hub, applied when the client omits them. | 'Sending an explicit top_p=1.0 overrides this and is worse'. | 09 Generation & sampling |
| `max_tokens (cliente)` | ≥ 32768 with thinking | Output budget recommended to the client. | Max effort goes beyond 8k output tokens; short budgets cut the answer off inside the reasoning. | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |

#### Scheduler / batching

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--long-prefill-token-threshold (LONG_PREFILL_TOKEN_THRESHOLD)` | empty (omits the flag); positive ≤ MNBT; TP3 script 3584; tp3-throughput 2048 | Limits how many tokens a long prefill consumes per step. | A/B/A at MNBT 7168 with 3584: frozen warm session 131/108 s → 15 s; contended prefill +6%; solo +3..+11%. (interpretation) The explicit empty value in the .env suppresses the TP3 default of 3584. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `--max-num-batched-tokens (MAX_NUM_BATCHED_TOKENS)` | 7168 (default); 2048 (PR77, TP4, seqs=16); 1024 (long-coding); 4096 (tp3-throughput); never 8192 | Scheduler token budget per step (prefill chunk size). | 8192 with a long history overflows the GB10's persistent_topk smem (crash at 300k). MNBT is also the encoder cache size, hence MM_IMAGE_TOKENS ≤ MNBT. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 17 Multimodal |
| `--max-num-seqs (MAX_NUM_SEQS)` | 4 (TP2/TP3); TP4 8; long-coding 2; tp3-throughput 8; ladder 16 | Maximum number of in-flight requests in the continuous batch. | They are 'four generations in flight, not four parked sessions'. TP4: with 4 → 84 tok/s aggregate at 8 streams and a 27 s worst TTFT; with 8 → 135 tok/s and 1.0 s. Capture sizes and EXL3_TEMP_ROWS_FUSED need to follow. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `COLD_TOK_S` | 1300.0 | Initial prefill throughput estimate before any samples exist. | Seed of the cost model dt = a + b·n. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `FIT_WINDOW` | 24 | Sample window used in the linear fit dt = a + b·n. | In this kit, a step with prefill costs ~0.3 s fixed + ~0.68 ms/token; the window tracks that cost. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `GLM53_FAIR_PREFILL_CHUNK` | 256 (≤ MNBT) | Probe chunk used until step-time samples exist. · Probe chunk used until timing samples exist. | Every step with prefill costs ~0.3 s fixed in this kit; probing measures without putting the incumbent at risk. · Afterwards, fair v5 picks the largest ladder rung that fits in the step budget. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `GLM53_FAIR_PREFILL_MAX_CHUNKS` | 1 (1..16) · 1 (max 16) | How many distinct prefills enter per turn. · Number of distinct prefills served per mixed turn. | Raising it does not multiply a request's chunk; it only splits the budget among more newcomers. · Does not multiply the chunk of a single request. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `GLM53_FAIR_PREFILL_MAX_INTERVAL_MS` | 2000 (1..600000) | Age after which an already-served prefill may borrow a chunk. · Age after which an already-served prefill can borrow 1 chunk (after paying off its debt). | Aging prevents a prefill from stalling forever. · Aging against starvation; the repo recommends not shortening this one first. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `GLM53_FAIR_PREFILL_MAX_STEP_MS` | 2000 (was 1000) | Maximum estimated duration of a mixed step. · Estimated duration limit of a mixed step (prefill + decode). | '2000 ms lets the ladder reach 2048 when a mixed step of 256 tokens costs ~1 s (1000 ms locks that tuning)'. · 1000 ms locked the fitter at 1024; 2000 allows 2048 chunks (0.3 + 0.00068 × 2048 ≈ 1.69 s). | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `GLM53_FAIR_PREFILL_SHARE` | 0.30 (was 0.20); 0..1 · 0.30 (code and .env); suggested range 0.20–0.30 | Fraction of engine time credited to the contended prefill. · Fraction of engine time that becomes credit for prefill. | 0.30 vs 0.20: a 30k newcomer waits ~145 s instead of ~220 s; the incumbent becomes 1.3–1.4× slower (vs 1.16×). · 0.30 vs 0.20: a 30k newcomer waits ~145 s vs ~220 s; the incumbent becomes 1.3–1.4× vs ~1.16× slower. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `GLM53_MIXED_PREFILL_CHUNK` | fair (default TP2/3/4 since 2026-09-15); skip/-1; N&gt;0 (ladder 512); 0/off/no · code: skip; launchers/.env: fair (TP2/TP3/TP4 since 2026-09-15); positive N = cap; 0/off · fair v5 | Policy for mixing a new request's prefill into the steps in which another request is decoding. · Policy for prefill while decodes are in progress. | skip 'starves prefills until the decode finishes'; 0/off 'drops the incumbent 10–36× at TP=2'. fair v5 puts decodes first, charges only the prefill that contends, fits dt = a + b·n and picks the largest rung (128..2048) that fits in MAX_STEP_MS. · skip = starvation (221 s; 20 min freeze); 0 = the incumbent drops 10–36×; fair balances the  | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `LADDER` | (128,256,512,768,1024,1536,2048) | Chunk-size rungs that fair v5 can choose from (v7, since b94fdfd, keeps the same ladder). | v5 saves credit for the largest rung that fits instead of spending it on small chunks (v4 got stuck at 128, ~70 effective tok/s). | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `LONG_PREFILL_TOKEN_THRESHOLD` | empty (TP2); TP3 profile: 2048 | Global cap on prefill tokens per request per step, even when the request is alone. | It is not the fairness fix: it also limits a solo prefill. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `max_concurrent_batches` | 1 synchronous; 2 asynchronous | Number of in-flight batches under async scheduling. | It factors into the Mamba block reservation and the drafter's id demand (2 × 2048 in-flight tokens = 97 blocks per 1M request). | 32 Scheduler & batching, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `MAX_NUM_BATCHED_TOKENS / --max-num-batched-tokens (MNBT)` | 7168 (default with E3); historical 2048 (winner of the pre-E2 P1 ladder); 8192 forbidden (indexer smem) | Token budget per step; it also sizes the scratch of the EXL3 fat tiers. | Larger chunks amortize the fixed cost but worsen the interleave; 7168 with E3 gives the best prefill (1492–1587 tok/s). | 32 Scheduler & batching, 28 Kernels |
| `MAX_NUM_SEQS / --max-num-seqs` | 4 (TP2); 8 (TP4) | Maximum number of generations in flight. | Sizes EXL3_TEMP_ROWS_FUSED (4 × 8 = 32 rows) and the indexer's rightsize workspace. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 31 PagedAttention & KV |

DFlash2 reaches 65.1 tok/s on structured. | 35 Speculation: fundamentals, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `aux_hidden_state_layers (taps EAGLE3)` | (6, 15, 25, 34, 43) | Target layers whose hidden state, contracted from the mHC, feeds the DFlash2 drafter. | Features from several depths give the drafter context; they require hc_post + hc_contract because of the 4 residual streams. | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 15 Hyper-connections |

| `dflash_config: selector_top_k / selector_rank` | 16 / 256 (DFlash2 config.json, DFcfg:14, :13) | Number of candidates per position and rank of the candidate selector's codebooks. | They control the cost and quality of the selector, which directly drives acceptance. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `DFLASH_DRAFT_TP (draft_tensor_parallel_size)` | 2 (TP2); 1 (TP3: rank 0 only); 4 (TP4); empty = inherits TP | The drafter's own TP. | C4 keep (TP2): prefill 8k 938 / 16k 972 / 100k 997 tok/s; decode 65.1/27.1. Draft TP1 avoids network traffic per draft step but concentrates the work on rank 0. TP3: 32 heads/8 KV heads do not divide by 3 → pad to 36/9 and draft TP=1 ('the divisibility tax'). | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 38 Parallelism |
| `DFLASH_DRAFT_TP / draft_tensor_parallel_size` | 2 (TP2); 1 (TP3) | Tensor parallelism degree of the drafter. | TP=1 on TP3 is the divisibility tax (the drafter's heads do not divide by 3). | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
