| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--additional-config` | {"gdn_prefill_backend":…} (not used) | Chooses the prefill backend of the GDN layers, among other extras. | GDN prefill FlashInfer SM120 is on the list of non-adopted experiments. | 28 Kernels, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `--linear-backend` | present, not used | Chooses the implementation of the linear GEMMs. | Dense BF16 GEMMs go to cuBLAS; the efficiency of skinny GEMMs (M=1..4) determines tok/s. | 28 Kernels |
| `_select_sm120_config` | table by base_programs × bf16/fp8 (RTX PRO 6000, sm_120) | Launch table of the QSA kernels tuned for sm_120 in the v0.30 backport. | ⚠ _is_sm120() compares == (12, 0); the GB10 is sm_121 (12,1), so it uses the default GB300 table (inferred from the code). | 26 Inside the GPU, 28 Kernels |
| `block_n / target_splits / num_warps (tabela stock sparse GQA)` | small (16,64,4), lt32 (16,32,4), le256 (64,8,2), le512 (64,4,2), large (64,1,2) | Stock table of the GQA split-K sparse attention kernel, by base_programs bucket = num_rows × num_kv_heads. | On the GB10, the stock decode launches ~5× more CTAs than the chip runs at once and then pays for a merge of 64 partials. | 28 Kernels, 26 Inside the GPU |
| `FUSED_GDN_STATE_DTYPES` | includes bf16 | List of state dtypes accepted by the fused Gated DeltaNet kernel. | It is what makes MAMBA_SSM_CACHE_DTYPE=bfloat16 possible without falling back to another kernel. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids, 28 Kernels |
| `GB10_PROFILES['mqa']` | tiles_small=2 (stock 1), tiles_large=8 (stock 8), num_warps=2 | Overrides tiles_per_program and num_warps of the QSA indexer's MQA scorer. | Fits the block-selection scorer to the GB10's SM count. Not measured. | 28 Kernels, 13 Sparse attention |
| `GB10_PROFILES['sparse']` | small (32,16,4), lt32 (32,8,4), le256 (64,4,2), le512 (64,2,2), large (64,1,2) | GB10 profile of the GQA sparse kernel with fewer split-K partitions for 48 SMs. | '≈1.3 waves instead of ≈5, one merge over 16 partials instead of 64' (fable §8.2). Not measured. | 28 Kernels, 26 Inside the GPU |
| `IS_FP8` | bool tl.constexpr (v0.30 lane) | In the v0.30 backport, upcasts K to Q's dtype and V to fp16 in the PV dot. | 'fp8 QK measured slower here and less accurate'; fp16 has a larger mantissa than bf16 and P≤1 fits within its range. | 23 Quantized KV cache, 04 Numbers in the computer |
| `KV_QUANT_MODE` | 0 (bf16) / 1 (fp8), tl.constexpr | Triton constexpr that turns on the FP8 cast and scale branches in the QSA kernels. | With 0, the compiled BF16 code is identical to the original ('inert unless fp8'). | 28 Kernels, 23 Quantized KV cache |
| `QSA_PROFILE` | stock / gb10 / JSON path · stock (default) / gb10 / path to a JSON file | Chooses the launch table (block_n, splits, warps; MQA tiles) of the QSA Triton kernels. · Chooses the launch profile of the QSA kernels and mounts the qsa_gb10 overlay. | The stock tables were tuned on GB300 (160 SMs); on the GB10 (48 SMs) the decode (16, 64 splits, 4 warps) launches ~5× more CTAs than fit and pays for a merge of 64 partials. Not benchmarked; incompatible with FP8 KV and VLLM_QSA_DET_TOPK (they all overwrite ops/qsa.py). · The stock table was tuned on the GB300 (160 SMs); the GB10 has 48.  | 28 Kernels, 26 Inside the GPU |
| `softmax_scale / output_scale` | head_dim^-0.5 × k_scale; v_scale (host floats) | Premultiplies the FP8 scales on the host: softmax_scale absorbs k_scale; output_scale (v_scale) is folded into the per-row inverse normalizer (inv_normalizer = output_scale / max(normalizer, 1e-20)). | The kernel does not need on-device scale buffers and the split-K LSE stays unscaled for the merge. softmax_scale is converted to base 2 (×1.4427) so exp2 can be used. | 28 Kernels, 23 Quantized KV cache |
| `VLLM_ENABLE_FLA_PACKED_RECURRENT_DECODE` | present, unused | Enables a packed recurrent decode path from FLA for GDN. | A kernel alternative the recipe does not explore. | 28 Kernels, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `VLLM_FLASHINFER_AUTOTUNE_CACHE_DIR` | /root/.cache/vllm/flashinfer_autotune_cache_unfused (determinism) / /tmp/fi_autotune (lane v0.30) · /root/.cache/vllm/flashinfer_autotune_cache_unfused (when VLLM_MOE_DET_FINALIZE= | Directory of the FlashInfer autotune cache. · Directory of the FlashInfer autotune cache, kept separate for the unfused variant. | In determinism mode it avoids mixing fused and unfused tunings; on v0.30 it lives in the container's /tmp because a persisted cache that differed between nodes deadlocked TP2 in the autotune collective (CHANGELOG 2026-09-25). · A persisted cache that differed between nodes has already hung TP2 (CHANGELOG 09-25, v0.30). | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 28 Kernels |
| `VLLM_FLASHINFER_MOE_FUSED_FINALIZE` | 0 in deterministic mode · 0 (injected when VLLM_MOE_DET_FINALIZE=1) | Disables FlashInfer's fused MoE finalize. · Env var that forces the unfused variant of the MoE finalize in FlashInfer. | Complements VLLM_MOE_DET_FINALIZE so that the CUTLASS path uses the unfused finalize. · Complements the determinism patch. | 28 Kernels, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `VLLM_GDN_DECODE_KERNEL` | {cuda, triton} (unused) | Chooses the Gated DeltaNet decode kernel. | The CUDA decode kernel fused_gdn_decode_post_conv_mtp supports up to 8 spec tokens. | 28 Kernels, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `VLLM_QSA_PROFILE` | name (gb10) | Env var read by _load_qsa_profile() in the patched ops/qsa.py to select a built-in profile. | Only exists when QSA_PROFILE≠stock; stock leaves the file byte-identical. | 28 Kernels |
| `VLLM_QSA_PROFILE / VLLM_QSA_PROFILE_JSON` | gb10 / JSON path (e.g., output of bench_qsa_kernels.py) | Env vars read by _load_qsa_profile() in the patched qsa.py to override (block_n, target_splits, partial_warps) and the MQA scorer tiles/warps. | Lets you plug in a profile measured on your own machine without editing code. | 28 Kernels |
| `VLLM_QSA_PROFILE_JSON` | /etc/vllm-qsa-profile.json | Path to a custom QSA launch table, mounted into the container. | Lets you apply the result of bench_qsa_kernels.py without editing code. | 28 Kernels |

#### Launch / topology

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--launch` | start.sh flag | Skips download and sync; only applies patches and launches. | It is the suggested path for a supervisor to run stop &amp;&amp; start --launch on a /health failure. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--nfs` | start.sh/stop.sh flag | Forces NFS mode in start.sh; in stop.sh it tears down the share and removes the volume. | Switches the weight distribution method without editing the .env. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--no-download` | start.sh flag | Skips the download (weights already on the head). | Typical use: ./start.sh --no-download. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--no-launch` | start.sh flag | Only downloads and distributes, without launching. | Useful for preparing the nodes ahead of a serving window. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--no-nfs` | start.sh flag | Forces rsync even with NFS_SHARE=true. | rsync cuts the cold start (the 458 s were NFS streaming, fable §4.6). | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `.env` | source .env after the environment: .env wins (exceptions: ABLIT and HF_TOKEN) | Configuration file read by start.sh after the environment variables, so its keys override what was passed on the command line. | KV_CACHE_DTYPE=fp8 ./start.sh is ignored if the .env defines the key — it cost a whole launch cycle; check the KV dtype: line in the launch summary. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `_CLI_ABLIT` | captures ABLIT from the environment before source .env | Internal variable that holds the ABLIT coming from the command line and reapplies it after loading the .env. | Makes ABLIT=1 ./start.sh work even with ABLIT=0 in the .env — the only exception (along with HF_TOKEN) to the rule that the .env wins. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `_CLI_HF_TOKEN` | captures HF_TOKEN from the environment before source .env | Internal variable that preserves the HF_TOKEN passed in the environment and reapplies it after source .env in start.sh, download.sh and check-weights.sh. | Prevents an empty token in the .env from wiping out the token passed on the command line for gated repositories. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DO_DOWNLOAD` | via flags (--no-download, --launch); DO_DOWNLOAD_DEFAULT=false with FP8_DENSE | Controls the download stage on the head. | Avoids downloading again when the weights are already in the cache. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DO_LAUNCH` | via flags (--no-launch disables it) | Controls the patch + container launch stage. | Lets you only prepare the weights without bringing up serving. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DO_SYNC` | via flags (--launch skips it) | Controls the stage that distributes the weights to the worker. | Skipping the sync saves minutes when the worker is already up to date. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `EXTRA_DOCKER_ARGS` | empty; BUG: dropped in the 2-node start.sh (works in start-tp1.sh L353) | Should append flags to docker run via the DOCKER_ARGS array. | The head and worker heredocs have hand-written flags and do not reference DOCKER_ARGS; NCCL env passed through here (as fable §3.5 suggests) has no effect — you have to edit the heredocs. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `EXTRA_VLLM_ARGS` | empty; appended last, verbatim | Escape hatch for extra vllm serve flags, re-parsed by the shell in the rendered script (JSON in single quotes). | Before the VLLM_ARGS array fix it was dropped; the final command lands in .last_head_launch.sh (0600), which may contain --api-key. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `IMAGE` | vllm/vllm-openai:qwen38-flash-next (day-0, sha256:d464f3b4…, ~20.6 GB, NVIDIA dev build) / vllm/vllm-openai:v0.30.0 | The engine's Docker image, guaranteed on both nodes; on day-0 the model code lives in vllm/models/qwen3_8_flash_next/nvidia/, on 0.30 in vllm/models/qwen4_exp/nvidia/ (Qwen4Exp* classes). | Every patch in the recipe replaces files in this image, and the chosen lane defines which overlays are needed (day-0 ~10, v0.30 only draft vocab and the optional FP8 KV). | 30 vLLM architecture, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `OVERRIDE_GPU_MEMORY_UTILIZATION` | start-v030.sh: 0.80 | Overrides GPU_MEMORY_UTILIZATION after the .env. | The v0.30 lane was validated at 0.80 (1 h soak with a minimum MemAvailable of 4.5/7.8 GiB). | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `OVERRIDE_IMAGE` | start-v030.sh: vllm/vllm-openai:v0.30.0 | Swaps the Docker image after the .env. | It is what turns start.sh into the stock vLLM 0.30 lane (native qwen4_exp code, only 1–2 overlays). | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `OVERRIDE_KV_CACHE_DTYPE` | start-v030.sh: auto (BF16); fp8 opt-in via OVERRIDE_KV_CACHE_DTYPE=fp8 · empty (BF16, v0.30 lane default) / fp8 | Overrides KV_CACHE_DTYPE after the .env. · On the vLLM 0.30 lane, enables the vllm#55557 backport (patch_qsa_fp8_kv_v030.py) and FP8 KV. | On the v0.30 lane the default is BF16 (1,340,476 tokens); fp8 with the backport gives 2.64M tokens but is ~5% slower. · 2.64M KV tokens vs 1,467,072 in BF16 (GMU 0.80); NLL 1.328/1.334, needles 3/3 at 200k; ~5% slower than BF16. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `OVERRIDE_MAX_MODEL_LEN` | start-fp8.sh: 262144 | Overrides MAX_MODEL_LEN after the .env. | The official FP8 lane runs at the native 262K context (~500k KV tokens), even if the .env asks for another value. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `OVERRIDE_MODEL_ID` | empty; start-fp8.sh exports Qwen/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-FP8 | Read from the environment after the .env; replaces MODEL_ID so that alternative lanes can reuse the same start.sh. | In the selection order it ranks behind only FP8_DENSE=true (FP8_DENSE &gt; OVERRIDE_MODEL_ID &gt; ABLIT=1 &gt; MODEL_ID); when it is set, ABLIT is ignored, which avoids serving the ablit with an incompatible checkpoint. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `OVERRIDE_SERVED_MODEL_NAME` | start-fp8.sh: qwen3.8-flash-next-fp8 | Overrides SERVED_MODEL_NAME after the .env. | Lets the official FP8 lane show up under a different name in /v1/models without editing the .env. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `OVERRIDE_YARN_ENABLE` | start-fp8.sh: false | Overrides YARN_ENABLE after the .env. | Guarantees YaRN is off on the FP8 lane (native context), since YaRN only costs precision without extending beyond 262144. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `SKIP_PLE_PATCH` | false (true in start-fp8.sh and start-v030.sh) | Skips the ple_layer.py patch. · Skips step 6 of start.sh (the PLE layer patch). | The official FP8 is fine-grained FP8 across the whole checkpoint and v0.30 loads the PLE natively, so the patch is unnecessary. · For native FP8 checkpoints, where the image's stock dispatch already works. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `V030` | true on the v0.30 lane | Signals to start.sh that the lane is vLLM 0.30, switching the overlays that get applied. | FP8_DENSE, QSA_PROFILE and determinism are not supported and raise an explicit error (start.sh:606–619). | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `vllm/vllm-openai:qwen38-flash-next` | NVIDIA day-0 build, vLLM v0.1.dev20073+g8e685d198; alternative: the vLLM 0.30 lane | Serving Docker image. It is never rebuilt; files are patched via bind-mount. | Reproducible and auditable; the _v030 patches exist because the 0.30 lane changes the paths (qwen4_exp). | 30 vLLM architecture, 41 Recipe engineering |

#### Memory / weight loading

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--gpu-memory-utilization` | 0.80 (previously 0.835) | Fraction of memory that vLLM reserves for weights, KV and activations. | 0.835 left less than 1 GiB of MemAvailable under load in unified memory; ~32 GiB remain for KV with 0.835 or ~28 GiB with 0.80. | 27 Unified memory, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `--safetensors-load-strategy` | lazy (always) | Loads the shards on demand, reading only what each rank needs. | 458 s load (target 392 + drafter 67) measured with lazy; with NFS the worker streams ~126 GiB over the CX7. | 24 Checkpoints, 27 Unified memory |
| `CONTAINER_MEM_GIB` | --memory ${CONTAINER_MEM_GIB}g --memory-swap ${CONTAINER_MEM_GIB}g | Memory cap for the TP1 container's cgroup, with no extra swap. | HANDOFF §0: GPU parameter allocations are not charged to the memcg (72 GiB of weights showed up as used on the host with the container at 10 GiB) — the cap limits the host side and the GMU limits the GPU side; §1 claimed the opposite. | 27 Unified memory |
| `CU_DEVICE_ATTRIBUTE_CAN_USE_STREAM_MEM_OPS` | 0 on GB10 | CUDA device attribute that indicates support for stream memory ops (cuStreamWaitValue32 / cuStreamWriteValue32). | On GB10 the wait returns success but the next kernel blocks the host, and inside a CUDA graph nothing waits: the GPU worker spins forever. This motivated the host-side handshake. | 27 Unified memory, 26 Inside the GPU |
| `EVICT_PAGE_CACHE` | true · true (default) | Calls posix_fadvise(POSIX_FADV_DONTNEED) on the checkpoint's .safetensors/.bin/.pt/.gguf files on both nodes (via ssh, or a throwaway container in NFS mode). · Enables step 4b-2 of start.sh, which runs evict_page_cache.py on both nodes before the load. | Issue #35: clean checkpoint pages (download/rsync/NFS) compete with the model; measured 4.15 → 0.00 GiB local and 3.00 → 0.00 GiB on NFS 4.2. Best-effort, no root. · 4.15 → 0.00 GiB resident per shard, no root; avoids CUDA OOM at load (issue #35). | 27 Unified memory |
| `GPU_MEMORY_UTILIZATION` | 0.80 (since 2026-09-26; previously 0.835; historically 0.85); TP1 derived from the budget | Fraction of the 121.69 GiB per node that vLLM may budget for weights, activations, graphs and KV (--gpu-memory-utilization). | At 0.835 the driver held 114.5 GiB/node and a 6-worker soak left MemAvailable at 0.3–0.9 GiB, GB10 hard-reset territory; at 0.80, 4.5/7.8 GiB remained over 1 h. KV shrinks by ~4 GiB/node and decode is unchanged; +0.01 GMU ≈ +0.83 GiB ≈ +95k fp8 tokens. | 27 Unified memory, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `HOST_SLACK_GIB` | 10.0 (start-tp1.sh) / 5 (tp1/.env.sample) | Slack added to the budget for the cgroup cap (cap = budget + slack). | Limits the container's host side without starving the process. | 27 Unified memory |
| `KV_TARGET_GIB` | 8.0 (start-tp1.sh) / 20 (tp1/.env.sample) | Target KV size (GiB) in the TP1 budget. | More KV = more UVM; 20 GiB ≈ 686k tokens with MemAvailable ~13 GiB. HANDOFF cites a 12 GiB default (discrepancy with the script). | 27 Unified memory, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `MEMWATCH_MIN_GIB` | 6 | MemAvailable floor for the memwatch.sh watchdog. | Below the floor the container is killed before the host hangs. | 27 Unified memory |
| `MTP_GIB` | 1.49 GiB | Memory cost of the MTP drafter added to the TP1 budget. | On TP1, MTP is disabled by default (0) because every GiB counts. | 27 Unified memory, 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `OS_RESERVE_GIB` | 16.0 | Minimum reserve for the OS: the cap always stays at least 16 GiB below the pool size. | The TP1 host has already hard-crashed 3×; exhausting the pool hangs the kernel instead of triggering OOM. | 27 Unified memory |
| `OVERHEAD_GIB` | 5.6 (measured 3.37 non-torch + 1.92 activation + 0.12 graphs = 5.41) | Non-weight overhead added to the TP1 budget: budget = (checkpoint − PLE) + OVERHEAD + MTP + max(kv_need, KV_TARGET); gmu = budget/MemTotal. | Derives the GMU from measurements instead of guessing a fraction. | 27 Unified memory |
| `PLE_GIB` | 26.82 | Size of the NVFP4 PLE table that stays off the GPU (mmap), deducted from the budget. | Without this deduction the derived GMU would include 26.8 GiB that does not live on the GPU. | 27 Unified memory, 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `PLE_OFFLOAD` | false (2 nodes) / true required (TP1) → VLLM_PLE_CPU_OFFLOAD=1 · false (default) / true → -e VLLM_PLE_CPU_OFFLOAD=1 | Moves the PLE table into a CPU process. · .env variable that enables offloading the PLE table to CPU. | Needs ~51 GB of free RAM (the log showed 44.92 GiB → OOM/thrash); in unified memory CPU RAM is the same pool and nothing is freed — it only reduces UVM pressure at load. Required on TP1 (98.6 GiB via UVM hung the host). · README: needs ~51 GB of free RAM and the box has ~42-45 GiB → keep false on dual-Spark. ⚠ In dual mode, start.sh only  | 27 Unified memory, 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `ROW_CHUNK_BYTES` | 16 MiB | Row chunk size when streaming the FP8-dense conversion. | Lets it run in a 1.2 GB cgroup alongside a live server. | 24 Checkpoints, 27 Unified memory |
| `VLLM_PLE_CPU_OFFLOAD` | 0 (default) / 1 | vLLM env var that builds the PLE table in a separate CPU process; the GPU workers receive only a placeholder. | In unified memory the offload does not reduce physical memory; it reduces UVM pressure at load and, with mmap, makes pages evictable. Requires --distributed-executor-backend mp. | 27 Unified memory, 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `VLLM_PLE_OFFLOAD_READY_TIMEOUT` | defined in vllm.envs (value not visible in the repo) | Startup timeout for the PLE offload process. | Bounds the wait for the CPU worker at boot. | 27 Unified memory |
| `VLLM_PLE_OFFLOAD_STEP_TIMEOUT` | 300 (TP1) · 300 (s) | Timeout for the handshake between the GPU worker and the PLE CPU worker. · Per-step handshake timeout between the model thread and the offload CPU worker. | Avoids waiting forever if the CPU worker fails to synchronize the H2D copy. · Avoids hanging silently forever if the worker does not respond. | 27 Unified memory |
| `VLLM_PLE_PACKED_TABLE_DIR` | TP1 only · empty / directory with {layer}.ngram_embedding.packed_u8 + .json | Directory of the packed PLE table, which is mapped via mmap. · Makes the offload CPU worker memory-map (mmap) a pre-packed PLE table instead of reading the shards. | A table in page cache is evictable, unlike anonymous RAM. · In unified memory: ~104 GiB → ~77 GiB of non-evictable footprint; the 26.8 GiB NVFP4 table sits in the page cache, evictable. | 27 Unified memory, 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `vm.min_free_kbytes` | 4194304 (measured, not recommended on dual) | Minimum free-memory reserve the Linux kernel maintains (runtime setting, resets on reboot). | TP1: 6 runs without a crash. Dual: the sysctl bundle cost 7–14% of decode (code S=8 417→360), MemAvailable read 0 (false alarm) and it still logged 1 NV_ERR_NO_MEMORY in 25 min. | 27 Unified memory |
| `vm.swappiness` | 30 (measured, not recommended on dual) | The kernel's preference for swapping vs dropping page cache. | Part of the memory-reserve bundle; the host has 16 GiB of swap. | 27 Unified memory |
| `vm.vfs_cache_pressure` | 200 (measured, not recommended on dual) | How aggressively the inode/dentry caches are reclaimed. | Part of the memory-reserve bundle measured on TP1. | 27 Unified memory |
| `vm.watermark_scale_factor` | 300 (measured, not recommended on dual) | Distance between the kernel's memory-reclaim watermarks. | Makes kswapd reclaim memory earlier; part of the bundle that helped on TP1 and cost 7–14% on dual. | 27 Unified memory |

#### MoE

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--all2all-backend` | allgather_reducescatter (together with EP) | MoE token dispatch/combine backend. | Inert with DP=1 (never invoked, fable §1); DeepEP low-latency would not help (fable §3.7). | 38 Parallelism |
| `--moe-backend` | {auto, flashinfer_cutlass, flashinfer_b12x, cutlass, marlin, emulation} (present, not used) | Chooses the MoE kernel implementation. | The default uses FlashInfer CUTLASS SM120 for NVFP4; B12X MoE is on the list of experiments that were not adopted. | 28 Kernels, 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `ENABLE_EXPERT_PARALLEL` | true (required for NVFP4) | Each GPU holds 256 of the 512 experts (--enable-expert-parallel). | With DP=1 and PCP=1, use_all2all_kernels is false: tokens are replicated across TP, each rank computes its own experts and the results are summed by the TP all-reduce — EP only changes which weights each GPU holds. Before the fix, false was ignored. | 38 Parallelism, 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `moe_intermediate_size / shared_expert_intermediate_size` | 640 / 640 | Intermediate width of the routed experts and of the shared expert (which has a sigmoid gate, *.mlp.shared_expert_gate). | The TP-sharded shared expert ends up with 320 rows per GPU, not divisible by 128 (hence the per-channel scale in FP8-dense). | 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `num_experts` | 512 (EP: 256 per GPU) | Routed experts per MoE layer. | With EP each GPU holds half (NVFP4 experts ~63 GiB in total); with DP=1 all2all is not even used. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts, 38 Parallelism |
| `num_experts_per_tok` | 10 | Router top-k: 10 of 512 experts per token. | 125B total parameters and ~6B active; the experts are ~2.1 GiB (23%) of the bytes per step. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `VLLM_USE_B12X_MOE` | 1 in the neighboring dspark image (not used here) | Turns on the b12x MoE kernels. | B12X MoE is among the kernel experiments that were listed and not adopted. | 28 Kernels |

#### Model architecture

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--hf-overrides` | {"text_config":{"ple_embedding_dtype":"float8_e4m3fn"[, "rope_parameters":{...}]}} · a single nested JSON: {'text_config': {'ple_embedding_dtype': ..., 'rope_parameters': ...}} | Overrides config.json fields without editing the cache, nested under text_config in a single JSON payload. · vLLM arg that overrides HF config fields (ModelConfig._apply_dict_overrides); the recipe generates a single override nested under text_config. | At the root level the rope_parameters override never reached text_config (YaRN no-op); README:435–450 still shows the buggy form. · The nesting is essential: at the top level the override does not reach text_config, and YaRN and the PLE dtype would be ignored. | 24 Checkpoints, 08 Position & RoPE |
| `_PLE_LAYER_PRIME` | 10007 | Prime used to derive the per-layer PLE seed (seed + 10007 × ple_dense_layer_id). | Guarantees distinct hash multipliers per layer. | 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `_SPLITMIX_GAMMA / _M1 / _M2` | 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15, 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9, 0x94D049BB133111EB | SplitMix64 bit-mixing constants that generate odd per-layer multipliers 2·(splitmix(v) mod half_bound)+1. | half_bound bounds the multiplier so that token×mult does not overflow int64. | 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `BOS/EOS 248044 · vision 248053/248054 · image 248056 · video 248057` | BOS=EOS=248044 | Ids of the tokenizer's special tokens (begin/end, vision, image and video). | Always kept in the draft vocabulary (the last 33 ids, 248044-248076). | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 17 Multimodal |
| `full_attention_interval` | 4 → 12 QSA layers + 36 GDN | Config field specifying that 1 out of every 4 layers uses full attention (here, sparse QSA) and the rest are Gated DeltaNet. | Only 12 (+1 MTP) layers have per-token KV: a tiny KV and near-linear prefill up to 600k. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids, 18 The three models |
| `hc_count` | multiple streams; low-rank projections of rank 320 · 4 | Number of HyperConnection residual streams (attn_hyper_connection, mlp_hyper_connection, hyper_connection_mixer). · Number of Gated Residual / HyperConnection branches (residual 4×2560). In the PLE it produces one key per branch (key_proj 2560 → 10240) and a shared value. | HyperConnections are BF16 and replicated on both GPUs (1.19 GiB read per step), a target for FP8-dense. · The PLE gate is one scalar per branch; the 97 HC modules (1.19 GiB bf16) are replicated on each GPU and account for 13% of the bytes per step. | 15 Hyper-connections, 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `hc_lowrank` | 320 | Rank of the Mix low-rank projections that generate element-wise gates in the Gated Residual. | Rank-320 projections are not divisible by 128, one of the reasons FP8-dense uses per-channel scales instead of block scales. | 15 Hyper-connections, 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |
| `head_dim` | 256 | Dimension of each attention head. · Per-head dimension of QSA attention. | Sets the KV bytes per token and the tile shape of the QSA kernels. · Enters the per-token KV calculation (13 layers: bf16 26,624 B, fp8 13,312 B) and softmax_scale = head_dim^-0.5. | 06 Attention, 10 Prefill, decode & KV |
| `heads_per_ngram` | 8 → ngram_heads = (3-1)×8 = 16 | Hash heads per n-gram order; with 2 orders this yields 16 ids looked up in the table per token. | 16 rows read per token; multiple heads with different primes reduce simultaneous collisions. | 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `hidden_size` | 2560 | Hidden width (and the width of each of the 4 residual branches). | The 248320×2560 lm_head takes 1.18 GiB in BF16. | 07 Transformer block, 05 Tokens & embeddings |
| `layer_types / full_attention_interval` | 3× linear_attention + 1× full_attention, repeated 12× (interval=4) | The model's hybrid pattern: 36 Gated DeltaNet layers and 12 QSA layers. | Only 12 layers (+1 MTP) have a growing KV; the rest have fixed-size state, so the KV is cheap and prefill is near-linear in context length. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids, 18 The three models |
| `linear_conv_kernel_dim` | 4 | Kernel size of the Gated DeltaNet's short causal conv. | Part of the constant per-request state (conv_state) of the linear layers. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `linear_num_key_heads / linear_num_value_heads / linear_key_head_dim / linear_value_head_dim` | 16 / 48 / 128 / 128 | Shape of the Gated DeltaNet (36 layers): 16 QK heads and 48 V heads of 128 dims. | Per-request state of 48 × 128 × 128 (3 MiB per layer in fp32, 1.5 MiB/GPU at TP2); in_proj_a/b = [48, 2560] breaks the MXFP8 kernel. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `make_ngram_vocab_size_divisible_by` | 128 | Rounds the total number of PLE table rows up to a multiple of 128. | Padding so the table can be split across TP ranks (VocabParallelEmbedding sharded by row range). | 16 N-gram & MTP, 38 Parallelism |
| `mamba_ssm_dtype` | float32 | Dtype of the GDN recurrent state declared in the checkpoint. | The recipe overrides it with MAMBA_SSM_CACHE_DTYPE=bfloat16. | 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `max_position_embeddings` | 262144 · 262,144 | Number of positions the model was trained on. · The model's native context. | Going beyond it requires VLLM_ALLOW_LONG_MAX_MODEL_LEN and YaRN; the high-frequency rotations never saw larger positions. · YaRN ×4 takes it to ~1M; the recipe serves with --max-model-len 262144. | 08 Position & RoPE |
| `model_type` | qwen4_exp_text (Qwen4ExpForConditionalGeneration) | Model type that vLLM's registry maps to the class; vLLM 0.30 uses vllm/models/qwen4_exp/..., while the day-0 image uses vllm/models/qwen3_8_flash_next/... | The paths of the patched files change between lanes, which is why there are separate _v030 patches. | 30 vLLM architecture, 18 The three models |
| `ngram_context` | 2 previous tokens per request | Per-request PLE state holding the last 2 tokens, kept across steps and chunks to form 2/3-grams; tokens before an EOS are not combined. | Chunked prefill requires carrying this context between chunks; PLE buffers are sized by max_num_batched_tokens and max_num_seqs. | 16 N-gram & MTP, 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `ngram_heads_vocab_sizes` | 16 heads (8 bigram + 8 trigram), primes 20,000,003 … 20,000,171; 320,001,536 rows in 128 shards in the checkpoint (code default 512), at layer index 1 | Sizes of the n-gram hash tables of Qwen3_8FlashNextNGramEmbedding (PLE). | Prime sizes spread out hash collisions; the table (~51 GB FP8) is parametric memory that is cheap to look up (gather) but huge. | 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `ngram_size` | 3 (config) | Maximum n-gram order of the PLE: the table is addressed by hashes of 2-grams and 3-grams from the local context. | It is also the dilation of the PLE short conv (dilation 3). | 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `ngram_vocab_size_base` | 20,000,000 | Each head h uses a table whose size is the (h+1)-th prime after base-1 (distinct primes per head). | Total ≈ 16 × 20M ≈ 320M rows × 160 = 51.2B parameters: ≈95.4 GiB in BF16, 47.7 GiB in FP8, 26.8 GiB in packed NVFP4. | 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `num_hidden_layers` | 48 (MTP instantiated at mtp.layers.48) | Number of decoder layers; vLLM creates the MTP layer at index num_hidden_layers + i. · Number of blocks in the main model. | The nvidia checkpoint writes mtp.layers.0.*, hence the need for .0. → .48. aliases in the quantization metadata. · The MTP layer is built at the next absolute index, mtp.layers.48, which requires the alias in the quantization config. | 16 N-gram & MTP, 24 Checkpoints |
| `num_key_value_heads` | 2 (24 query heads; 1 KV head per GPU at TP2) | Number of K/V heads shared by the 24 query heads (GQA). | Shrinks the KV 12×; it enters the formula 13 × 2 × 2 × 256 × 2 B = 26,624 B/token bf16. | 12 MLA & compact KV, 10 Prefill, decode & KV |
| `partial_rotary_factor` | 0.25 (64 of the 256 dims get RoPE) | Fraction of each head's dimensions that get RoPE. | Partial RoPE: only 25% of the dims rotate; the rest carry no positional information. | 08 Position & RoPE |
| `ple_conv_kernel_size` | 4 | Kernel size of the causal depthwise 1D conv (dilation 3) applied after the PLE gate. | conv_state_len = (4-1)×3 = 9 → a [10240, 9] state per request (matches LMSYS). | 16 N-gram & MTP, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `ple_embed_dim` | 2560 → head_dim = 2560/16 = 160 | Concatenated dimension of the N-gram embedding: 16 rows of 160 values form E_t ∈ R^2560. | Sets the table row width (160 values; 90 B per row in packed NVFP4). | 16 N-gram & MTP, 05 Tokens & embeddings |
| `ple_layer_ids` | [2] in the config; vLLM module at layers.1.ple | Which block the PLE sits in; the config uses ID 2 and vLLM exposes the module at layers.1.ple (zero-based index). | ⚠ One-based vs zero-based (LMSYS confirms: 'configured layer ID 2, corresponding to zero-based index 1'); this matters for quantization patterns and tensor names. | 16 N-gram & MTP, 24 Checkpoints |
| `rms_norm_eps` | 1e-6 | Epsilon of the grouped RMSNorm (groups of 2560, gain 1+w, Qwen3_8FlashNextPLEGroupedNorm) applied to the PLE key, query and conv. | Normalizes key and query before the gate sigmoid(sign(s)·sqrt(\|s\|)), which compares the key with the residual. | 16 N-gram & MTP, 02 Neural networks |
| `rope_parameters (rope_type yarn)` | YaRN ×4: 262,144 → ~1M; use only if you need more than 262k | rope_type override to YaRN, injected into the same --hf-overrides nested under text_config. | At the top level the override was a setattr on the parent config and never reached text_config: YaRN was a silent no-op. | 08 Position & RoPE, 24 Checkpoints |
| `rope_theta` | 1e7 | Base of the RoPE frequencies. | Part of rope_parameters, which YaRN overrides via the nested --hf-overrides. | 08 Position & RoPE |
| `seed` | 1234 (code default, absent from the config); base_seed = seed + 10007 × ple_dense_layer_id | Seed for the PLE hash multipliers. | Determines the hashing; it must be identical to the one used in training, otherwise the table is addressed at the wrong rows. | 16 N-gram & MTP |
| `split_ngram_parts` | 128 (config); code default 512 | How many row shards the checkpoint writes for the PLE (ngram_embedding.shard_{i}.weight). | The loader reassembles the shards and slices each one to the TP rank's vocabulary range. | 16 N-gram & MTP, 24 Checkpoints |
| `text_config.rope_parameters` | rope_type yarn, factor 4.0, original_max_position_embeddings 262144 (only with YARN_ENABLE) | RoPE parameters of the text model, overridden to enable YaRN. | Verified: with the override at the root, text_config.rope_parameters kept the default rope_type, and every earlier 1M run served 1M positions with unscaled RoPE. | 08 Position & RoPE |
| `tie_word_embeddings` | false | Indicates that the embedding and the lm_head are different matrices (248,320 × 2560 each). · lm_head separate from the input embedding; the MTP drafter also has its own lm_head. | The 1.18 GiB BF16 lm_head is read every step and the MTP drafter has its own, read 3× per step — 26% of the bytes per step. · 3 of the 4 lm_head reads per step (~2.4 of ~9.2 GiB/GPU) come from the drafter. | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `vocab_size` | 248,320 | Vocabulary size. | The drafter's lm_head (248320×2560×2 B = 1.18 GiB, 0.59/GPU at TP2) is read on every draft step, which motivates the reduced vocabulary. | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `YARN_ENABLE` | false (sample) / true; forced to false if MAX_MODEL_LEN ≤ 262144 | Enables YaRN RoPE scaling, emitted inside text_config.rope_parameters in --hf-overrides. | YaRN extends the context beyond the native one but costs accuracy; before Sep 2026 it was a silent no-op, so 1M is not validated. README:216–217 still shows true (outdated; .env.sample is authoritative). | 08 Position & RoPE, 43 Qwen recipe |
| `YARN_FACTOR` | 4.0 (262144 × 4 ≈ 1M) | YaRN scale factor, emitted with rope_type yarn and original_max_position_embeddings 262144. | Sets how much the RoPE frequencies are rescaled; it only takes effect with the fixed payload nested under text_config. | 08 Position & RoPE |

#### Multimodal

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--allowed-local-media-path` | not set (media only via URL or base64 data URL) | Would allow reading images/videos from the server's disk. | Without it, media arrives via URL or base64 data URL, without exposing the server's filesystem. | 17 Multimodal, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--mm-encoder-tp-mode` | data | vLLM arg that replicates the vision encoder on each GPU instead of slicing it by TP. | The vision MLP (intermediate 4304) does not slice under TP2 into multiples of 16. | 17 Multimodal, 38 Parallelism |
| `limite multimodal por prompt` | default 1 image + 1 video per prompt | Maximum number of media items accepted per request. | Sets the worst case for vision tokens per request and for encoder memory. | 17 Multimodal |
| `MM_ENCODER_TP_MODE` | data (default) / weights (crashes) · data (default) / weights crashes | data replicates the vision encoder on each GPU; weights would shard it by TP (--mm-encoder-tp-mode). · .env variable that becomes --mm-encoder-tp-mode. | The vision MLP intermediate is 4304; 4304/2 = 2152 is not divisible by the 16 required by the NVFP4 kernels, so weights crashes at load. · weights crashes because 4304/2 = 2152 is not a multiple of 16 (NVFP4 kernels). | 17 Multimodal, 38 Parallelism |
| `mrope_section / mrope_interleaved` | [11,11,10] / true | mRoPE (t, h, w) sections for temporal and spatial positions, interleaved. | Splits the rotated dimensions among time, height and width for images and video. | 08 Position & RoPE, 17 Multimodal |
| `orçamento do encoder de visão` | 16,384 tokens (video auto-resize / sparse sampling) | Maximum vision tokens per input; images are resized and videos are sampled to fit. | Verified OCR of 48 px text in a 3840×2160 frame (≈8.2k vision tokens); the MTP drafter does not support external multimodal embeddings, hence a lower acceptance rate with media. | 17 Multimodal |
| `vision_config (ViT 27 blocos, hidden 1152, intermediate 4304)` | 27 blocks / 1152 / 4304 | The model's ViT vision tower. | The 4304 intermediate is the origin of the MXFP8 [4304,1152] and NVFP4 problems at TP2, solved with --mm-encoder-tp-mode data and BF16 emulation of visual.*. | 17 Multimodal |

#### Operations / Docker

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--cap-add SYS_NICE` | fixed | Allows the container to adjust thread priority/affinity. | Part of the recipe's fixed set of inference-container flags; the inventory does not detail the specific use. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--cap-add SYS_PTRACE` | TP1 only | Allows attaching py-spy to the container's processes. | Diagnosing hangs (e.g., the PLE offload deadlock after graph capture). | 45 Benchmarking, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--device /dev/infiniband` | /dev/infiniband:/dev/infiniband | Passes the RDMA verbs devices to the container. | Without them NCCL cannot reach the HCA and falls back to TCP. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `--dry-run` | flag | Plans the FP8-dense conversion without writing shards. | Checks the tensor plan before spending I/O. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `--gpus all` | fixed in docker run | Exposes the GB10 GPU to the container. | Without it vLLM does not see the CUDA device. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--ipc host` | fixed | Shares the host's IPC namespace (shared memory). | vLLM processes exchange data through shared memory; Docker's default /dev/shm is too small. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `--memory=1200m --cpus=4 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=` | limits of the build container (CPU-only) | Limits of the container in which the FP8-dense converter and verifier run, with no GPU. | A ~2 min build with ~1 GB of RAM, feasible next to a live server. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--network host` | fixed | The container uses the host's network stack. | NCCL, NFS and the API work without NAT and see the CX7 interfaces. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--restart` | not set (dies on crash) | Container restart policy, deliberately absent. | Automatic restart is risky in 2-node TP: a rank restarted on its own hangs in NCCL init; prefer a supervisor that does stop &amp;&amp; start --launch. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--resume` | flag (build.sh passes --resume) | Keeps already-written shards and continues the conversion. | Resumes interrupted builds without redoing finished shards. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `--ulimit memlock=-1` | fixed | Removes the limit on locked (pinned) memory. | RDMA needs to register (lock) memory; with a limit in place, NCCL IB fails. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `--ulimit stack=67108864` | 64 MiB | Increases the per-thread stack size. | Part of the recipe's fixed set of inference-container flags; the inventory does not detail the specific use. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `HF_API_BASE` | https://huggingface.co (optional mirror) | Endpoint from which the repository's hash manifest is downloaded. | Allows verifying weights through a mirror on nodes with no route to HF. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `HF_HOME` | ~/.cache/huggingface (host); /root/.cache/huggingface in the container | Root of the HF cache on the head, mounted in the container at /root/.cache/huggingface. | The container runs as root: mounts must target /root/... or HF offline lookups fail (README:468). | 24 Checkpoints, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `HF_HUB_OFFLINE` | 1 | Prevents the hub from accessing the network inside the container. | Requires a complete cache and a refs/main without a trailing newline; makes the launch reproducible and independent of internet access. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `HF_TOKEN` | not set; required for the ABLIT gated repo | Hugging Face token for downloading gated repositories. | ABLIT=1 ./download.sh fails immediately without a token; HTTP 403 means the terms of use have not been accepted. | 24 Checkpoints, 19 Abliteration |
| `IMAGE / HF_HOME (fp8dense/build.sh)` | vllm/vllm-openai:qwen38-flash-next / ~/.cache/huggingface | Image in which the converter and the verifier run, and root of the HF cache. | The conversion runs inside the serving image itself, in CPU-only mode. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `NFS_CONTAINER` | vllm-fn-nfs | Name of the NFS server container on the head. | Do not stop vllm-fn-nfs during load or serving; stop.sh --nfs takes it down. | 27 Unified memory |
| `NFS_IMAGE` | vllm-fn-nfs:local | Locally built NFS server image (Alpine + nfs-utils). | Allows serving NFS without installing packages on the host. | 27 Unified memory |
| `NFS_IMAGE / NFS_CONTAINER / NFS_VOLUME` | vllm-fn-nfs:local, vllm-fn-nfs, vllm-fn-hf | Names of the NFS server's image and container, and of the NFS Docker volume on the worker. | They identify the Docker resources created by nfs-share.sh. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `NFS_OPTS` | ro,sync,no_subtree_check,no_root_squash,insecure,fsid=0 | NFS export options on the server (head). | Read-only export; fsid=0 makes the export the NFSv4 root (pseudoroot). | 27 Unified memory |
| `NFS_SERVER_IP` | auto from IFACE (live 10.0.22.1) · auto from IFACE | Address of the NFS export on the head. · IP of the NFS server (head) used by the worker. | Do not use the 10.0.0.1 alias (README:172). · Do not use the loopback alias 10.0.0.1. | 27 Unified memory, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `NFS_SHARE` | false = rsync (author's live setup: true) / --nfs / --no-nfs · false / true | The head exports the HF cache via NFSv4 (privileged vllm-fn-nfs container) and the worker mounts the vllm-fn-hf Docker volume read-only. · Shares the head's HF cache with the worker over NFS instead of copying the weights. | rsync: ~126 GiB on the worker, decoupled nodes and a shorter cold start. NFS: zero disk on the worker, but the head must stay up and every cold start streams ~126 GiB (the 458 s load was NFS streaming); good for swapping checkpoints. · Avoids copying ~126 GiB to the worker; in exchange the head must stay up and every cold start transfers  | 27 Unified memory, 24 Checkpoints |
| `NFS_VOLUME` | vllm-fn-hf | NFS Docker volume mounted read-only on the worker at /root/.cache/huggingface. | Gives the worker the HF cache without a local copy. | 27 Unified memory |
| `nfsvers=4.2,ro,nconnect=8,rsize=1048576,wsize=1048576,hard,timeo=600` | mount options on the worker · NFS volume options on the worker | Mount options of the NFS volume on the worker. · Mount options of the read-only NFS 4.2 volume on the worker. | nconnect=8 opens several TCP connections and 1 MiB rsize/wsize speed up shard streaming; hard avoids silent read errors. · nconnect=8 parallelizes TCP connections; 1 MiB rsize/wsize and hard for throughput and robustness. | 27 Unified memory |
| `refs/main = fp8dense-{rev8}` | written with echo -n (no newline) | Ref of the destination repo in the HF cache for the FP8-dense checkpoint. | The README warns that huggingface_hub ≥1.x fails offline if refs/main has a newline. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `REQUIRE_IDLE_GPU` | true | Aborts the launch if nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps shows a process on any node. | Without it, the co-tenancy symptom is a CUDA OOM 10 min later (the worker was running DeepSeek-V4 dspark, fable §4.3). | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `ro,sync,no_subtree_check,no_root_squash,insecure,fsid=0` | export options; rpc.nfsd 8 | Options of the read-only NFS export of the HF cache, served by 8 nfsd threads. | A read-only export protects the cache; fsid=0 makes the directory the v4 root. | 27 Unified memory |
| `SRC_REPO / DST_REPO` | RadixArk/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-NVFP4 / MiaAI-Lab/...-FP8dense | Source and destination of the FP8-dense converter. | ⚠ The SRC default is RadixArk, not the nvidia checkpoint served by default. | 24 Checkpoints, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `TP1_CONTAINER_NAME` | vllm-fn-tp1 | Container name for the TP1 lane. | Avoids a collision with the 2-node vllm-fn container. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE` | 1 | transformers offline mode (config/tokenizer from the cache). | Complements HF_HUB_OFFLINE so that no remote lookup happens at boot. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `vllm-fn` | container name (TP1: vllm-fn-tp1) | Fixed name of the serving container on both nodes. | All lanes use the same name and port: stop.sh is required before switching; logs with docker logs -f vllm-fn. | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `WORKER_HF_HOME` | ~/.cache/huggingface | Root of the HF cache on the worker. | The worker path mirrors the head's by rewriting the $HOME prefix, which allows different home directories on the two nodes. | 24 Checkpoints, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `WORKER_MODEL_PATH` | derived from the head's path | Path of the model snapshot on the worker, used by rsync and by verification. | Keeps head and worker pointing at the same snapshot even with different home prefixes. | 24 Checkpoints, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `WORKER_USER` | empty (author's live setup: zurih) | SSH user used to access the worker. | The launcher relies on passwordless SSH head→worker (prerequisite in README:24). | 43 Qwen recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `~/.cache/vllm` | -v $HOME/.cache/vllm:/root/.cache/vllm | Mounts the vLLM cache (FlashInfer autotune, modelinfos) into the container. | It is the only per-node state that persists across launches; the first request is slow because of autotune. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 28 Kernels |

#### Prefix caching

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--mamba-cache-mode` | align (forced by the model; all raises an error) | Mamba/GDN state cache mode: align stores state only at block boundaries. | Because the recurrent state cannot be reconstructed midway, prefix cache hits only happen at multiples of the block (1600 tokens). | 33 Prefix caching, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `disable_eagle_block_drop` | true (recipe); upstream default False | SpeculativeConfig key (backport of vllm#53388) that stops the prefix cache from dropping the last matched block when there is an EAGLE/MTP drafter. | With MTP 3, every turn recomputed a whole block (1,664 tokens) of prefill; decode, prefill and NLL unchanged. Native in vLLM 0.30. | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 33 Prefix caching |
| `mamba_cache_mode (align)` | align | Cache mode for the stateful layers (GDN, the PLE's short-conv) in which state is only stored at block boundaries. | In the hybrid cache, prefix caching only matches at aligned points. | 33 Prefix caching, 14 Linear attention & hybrids |
| `MTP_DISABLE_BLOCK_DROP` | 1 (sample) / 0 (fallback) → disable_eagle_block_drop:true · 1 in .env.sample (start.sh default = 0) | Does not drop the last matched prefix cache block when there is an EAGLE-like proposer (MTP). · Turns on disable_eagle_block_drop and mounts the patch_block_drop.py backport when the image does not know the key. | Backport of vllm#53388 that fixes #62 (2nd turn with zero hit): turn 1 TTFT of a 9.5k session 3.25 → 0.63 s, turn 2 1.12 → 0.59 s, post-tool 4k 2.55 → 1.98 s; decode/prefill/NLL unchanged. · Turn 1 TTFT: 3.25 → 0.63 s (9.5k session). | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 33 Prefix caching |
| `prefix caching (default do vLLM)` | on (default) | Reuse of KV blocks across requests that share a prefix. | 600k prompt: 336 s cold → 2.87–8.82 s warm (~40–110×); on a hybrid model it only hits at aligned boundaries. | 33 Prefix caching |
| `VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL` | 1664 (workaround for vllm#53504; measured, not adopted) | State retention interval for the prefix cache in hybrid models. | No change on top of block-drop; the 1st identical repeat of a short prompt still misses on the day-0 lane (7.9k: 2.70 s and then 0.58 s). | 33 Prefix caching |

| `FP8_MAX` | 448.0 | Maximum representable value of FP8 E4M3, used in the scale s = amax/448. | Defines the clamp of the per-channel quantization. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 04 Numbers in the computer |
| `FP8_PB_WO` | with group_size==128, treated as FP8_BLOCK_SCALES | New (or wrong) ModelOpt label for the MTP experts, normalized to FP8_BLOCK_SCALES. | nvidia's rev fc694b54, the Keys checkpoint and ABLIT use the new label; without normalizing it, the MTP MoE is not built. | 24 Checkpoints, 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |

Relative RMSE 0.0240/0.0262/0.0268, cosine ≥ 0.9996. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 20 Quantization fundamentals |
| `group_size` | 128 (MTP FP8 experts) | Scale block size declared in the checkpoint, read to build weight_block_size=[g,g]. | The patch refuses to guess: a wrong block silently applies misaligned scales. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 20 Quantization fundamentals |
| `group_size (FP8_BLOCK_SCALES)` | 128 → weight_block_size=[128,128] | Block size of the MTP experts' FP8 scales, read from the checkpoint (the patch refuses to guess). | A wrong block silently applies misaligned scales: it shows up as near-random acceptance, not as a crash. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 24 Checkpoints |
| `hf_quant_config.json` | legacy; may disagree with config.json (rev fc694b54) · legacy ModelOpt sidecar | Legacy ModelOpt file with quantization metadata. · Legacy quantization config file next to config.json. | In rev fc694b54 it stores FP8_PB_WO while config.json says FP8_BLOCK_SCALES; the recipe patches both and mounts them over the snapshot. · The draft model path reads the legacy file and the MoE dispatch reads config.json (issue #38), which is why both are patched. | 24 Checkpoints |

| `mm_mxfp8` | N ≥ 128, N%32==0, K ≥ 128, K%32==0 (measured on sm_121) | Shape constraint of FlashInferCutlassMxfp8LinearKernel; if the post-TP-split shape does not fit, the layer uses EmulationMxfp8LinearKernel. | in_proj_a/b [48,2560] fails (N below 128) and visual...linear_fc1 [4304,1152] fails (4304%32=16), fatal in the profile run ~7 min after launch. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 28 Kernels |

(126G/206) | HF repository to serve; nvidia = dense BF16 + NVFP4 experts + FP8 PLE, local-inference-lab = MXFP8 attention + NVFP4 PLE, RadixArk = BF16 attention + FP8 PLE. | Each checkpoint writes quantization metadata differently, requiring different patches; RadixArk is ~13–16% slower in decode (more bytes) with identical prefill. | 24 Checkpoints, 43 Qwen recipe |
| `MXFP8_BLOCK_SIZE` | 32 | MX microscaling block size (elements that share one E8M0 scale). | Shapes must be multiples of 32 for the native kernel. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |

| `PLE_EMBEDDING_DTYPE` | empty = auto (detect_ple_dtype.py); float8_e4m3fn / nvfp4 · empty (auto-detection by detect_ple_dtype.py) | Forces the dtype of the PLE/N-gram table injected into text_config.ple_embedding_dtype; empty lets the detector infer it from the checkpoint. · .env variable that forces the value of ple_embedding_dtype, skipping automatic detection. | nvidia declares the FP8 PLE only in quantization_config.config_groups; without the override, the 51 GB table does not load. · Only for a manual override when detection from the checkpoint's quantization_config does not work. | 16 N-gram & MTP, 24 Checkpoints |
| `ple_embedding_dtype` | nvfp4 / fp4 / w4a16_nvfp4 → NVFP4; float8_e4m3fn / fp8 → FP8; absent → old branches (BF16) | text_config field that picks the QuantizeMethodBase of the PLE table (N-gram embedding). The recipe injects it via a nested --hf-overrides. | Without it, on a MIXED_PRECISION checkpoint the PLE table becomes BF16 (≈102 GB) and the load dies (README 'The PLE Patch'). | 16 N-gram & MTP, 24 Checkpoints |
| `PLE_QUANT_OVERRIDE` | fp8 (cited in docs; does not exist in the current start.sh) · fp8 (cited in the README) — obsolete | Env var that the README and the fable say is injected by start.sh and read by the ple_layer.py shim. · Old variable for forcing PLE quantization. | Obsolete: grep does not find it outside the docs; the current mechanism is detect_ple_dtype.py + --hf-overrides text_config.ple_embedding_dtype. Do not rely on it when recreating the recipe. · ⚠ No current file reads this variable; the active dispatch is by text_config.ple_embedding_dtype. Do not trust the README on this point. | 16 N-gram & MTP, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `quant_algo` | FP8 / NVFP4 / W4A16_NVFP4 / MXFP8 / FP8_BLOCK_SCALES / FP8_PB_WO / FP8_PER_CHANNEL_PER_TOKEN | Quantization algorithm declared per layer, used by the ModelOpt dispatch to pick the method. | A value with no branch in the dispatch (e.g., FP8_BLOCK_SCALES for MoE) results in a silently unquantized layer or a late crash. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `QUANT_PATTERNS` | 19 patterns: GDN qkv/z/out, attention q/k/v/o, HC down/inject/up of the 2 GRs + mixer, shared expert gate/up/down, lm_head.weight | Which BF16 tensors become FP8 in the FP8-dense checkpoint. | The BF16 dense weights are ~6.9 of the ~9.2 GiB read per step per GPU; lm_head is 26% of the step because of the drafter's 3 extra reads. | 20 Quantization fundamentals, 24 Checkpoints |
| `quantization_config.config_groups` | groups with targets and num_bits (8 → FP8, 4 → NVFP4) | Section of config.json that declares quantization groups by module-name pattern. | It is the only place where nvidia declares the FP8 PLE; the MTP targets also need the .48 alias. | 24 Checkpoints, 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |
| `quantized_layers` | quant_algo per tensor/layer · map of layer prefix → quant_algo (ModelOpt MIXED_PRECISION) | Map from layer name to its quant_algo, matched by exact string. · Field of the ModelOpt quantization_config that states each layer's algorithm. | Exact matching is the origin of the mtp.layers.0 vs .48 bug; FP8-dense also uses per-tensor quantized_layers. · ModelOptMixedPrecisionConfig._resolve_quant_algo matches prefixes by exact string: mtp.layers.0.* does not match mtp.layers.48.*, and the MTP's MoE ends up unquantized. | 24 Checkpoints, 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |
| `Qwen/...-FP8 (oficial)` | FP8 experts, BF16 dense weights | Qwen's official FP8 checkpoint. | Does not help batch-1 decode: it keeps exactly the dense weights in BF16 and puts the experts in FP8 (more bytes than NVFP4). | 24 Checkpoints, 20 Quantization fundamentals |

| `rel 0.06 (limiar HIGH ERROR do verify_fp8_dense_checkpoint.py)` | above 0.06 → HIGH ERROR | Relative dequant error threshold above which the verifier flags HIGH ERROR. | Measured: 0.0240 / 0.0262 / 0.0268 (min/median/max), well below the threshold. | 25 Measuring quality, 24 Checkpoints |

| `visual.` | prefix always routed to BF16 emulation | In the MXFP8 dispatch, the entire vision tower (prefix visual.) goes to BF16 emulation. | Dequantizing everything globally would OOM; the vision MLP (4304) is not a multiple of 32. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 17 Multimodal |
| `weight_block_size` | [g,g] with g = group_size (128) | Fp8Config argument that defines the 2D block of the FP8 scales. | Must match the checkpoint; the patch reads it from group_size instead of assuming it. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |

#### Reasoning / tools

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--enable-auto-tool-choice` | always | Lets the model decide when to emit tool calls in the OpenAI format. | Required for agents; the tool-calling regression passes with MTP (CHANGELOG:490). | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--reasoning-parser` | qwen3 (always) | Splits the &lt;think&gt;…&lt;/think&gt; block into a reasoning field. | This build streams in delta[reasoning], not reasoning_content (HANDOFF §3; bench/longctx.py:9–11): clients and benches need to read the right field. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `--tool-call-parser` | qwen3_coder (always) | Converts Qwen's native function-call format into OpenAI tool_calls. | Without the right parser, calls come out as text and agents break. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `chat_template_kwargs.enable_thinking` | true (default) / false | false pre-fills an empty &lt;think&gt;&lt;/think&gt; and turns reasoning off. | With false, an invalid reasoning_effort is silently ignored; benchmarks use thinking off. | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `chat_template_kwargs.reasoning_effort` | low / medium / xhigh (default xhigh); high and max not accepted | Reasoning effort level read by the chat template. | high and max return HTTP 400 (Unexpected reasoning effort max…) before reaching the engine; the check only exists in the thinking branch. | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `chat_template_kwargs.thinking_budget` | not honored by this build | Thinking token budget. | Has no effect: the only way left to limit reasoning is max_tokens. | 09 Generation & sampling |

#### Sampling / generation

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--override-generation-config` | e.g.: {"temperature":0.7} (not used) | Overrides the sampling defaults of the checkpoint's generation_config. | The checkpoint's T=1.0 lowers speculative acceptance; lowering the default temperature would change real chat throughput. | 09 Generation & sampling |
| `generation_config` | temperature 1.0, top_p 0.95, top_k 20 | Sampling defaults stored in the checkpoint. | Benchmarks are greedy; at T=1.0 rejection sampling accepts fewer drafts, so real chat throughput is lower. | 09 Generation & sampling, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |
| `max_tokens` | 2–4k for image QA | Limit on generated tokens, consumed first by thinking. | Empty content + finish_reason length means thinking used everything up — increase max_tokens. | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `VLLM_MOE_DET_FINALIZE` | not set (opt-in); 1 = unfused finalize · empty / 1 (opt-in) | Replaces the fused finalize of the FlashInfer CUTLASS MoE with an unfused, fixed-order one. · use_fused_finalize = (env != '1') in the FlashInfer CUTLASS MoE: disables the fused finalize (top-k reduction of the expert outputs). | Floating-point reduction is not associative; the fused finalize varied between identical runs in TP2+EP. · With the sorted top-k: logprob spread 0.25/6.07 nats → 0/0; distinct greedy outputs 5/5 → 1/5; −4% in decode and prefill. | 09 Generation & sampling, 28 Kernels |
| `VLLM_QSA_DET_TOPK` | not set (opt-in); 1 sorts the top-k indices · empty / 1 (opt-in) | Sorts the top-k indices of the QSA indexer, sending -1 sentinels to the tail. · Sorts each top-k row of the QSA indexer (replaces −1 with INT32_MAX, sorts, restores −1) so the same selection is always visited in the same order. | With VLLM_MOE_DET_FINALIZE: prompt logprob spread median 0.25 / max 6.07 nats → 0/0 and distinct greedy outputs 5/5 → 1/5; cost ~−4% on prose decode and prefill. Not ported to v0.30 and incompatible with QSA_PROFILE. · The same summation order in split-K yields greedy bit-identity; cost ~−4% in decode and prefill. | 09 Generation & sampling, 25 Measuring quality |
| `VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER` | present, not used (sampling is already FlashInfer top-k/top-p) | Forces FlashInfer's top-k/top-p sampler. | The build already samples via FlashInfer top-k/top-p. | 09 Generation & sampling, 28 Kernels |

#### Scheduler / batching

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--async-scheduling` | --async-scheduling / --no-async-scheduling (auto-enabled with mtp + mp) | Schedules the next step while the current one runs on the GPU. | Hides the scheduler's CPU overhead; already enabled automatically with MTP and the mp executor. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `--enable-chunked-prefill` | always | Breaks long prefills into chunks interleaved with decode steps. | Required at 262K–1M so that active streams do not stall during a huge prefill. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `--long-prefill-token-threshold` | suggested 2048 (not used) | Classifies prefills above the threshold as long, to limit how many of them enter per step. | Suggested to stabilize ITL under concurrency. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `--max-num-batched-tokens` | 8192 | Token budget per step; the prompt is processed in pieces (chunked prefill). | The PLE needs ngram_context across pieces and sizes its buffers by this value. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `--max-num-partial-prefills` | suggested 2 (not used) | Maximum number of concurrent partial prefills in the batch. | Prevents several long prompts from competing for the same step and degrading active decodes. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `--max-num-seqs` | 8 | Maximum number of concurrent sequences in the scheduler. | The PLE buffers are sized by max_num_seqs; the benchmarks use S=1/2/4/8. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `MAX_NUM_BATCHED_TOKENS` | 8192 (TP1: 2048) | Engine token budget per step, i.e., the prefill chunk size (--max-num-batched-tokens). | Fable §5: reasonable, larger chunks do not reduce all-reduce bytes; explains bug #62 (a prompt that fits in a single 8192 chunk misses the cache on the 1st repeat). At TP1, 2048 reduces the activation memory (1.92 GiB). | 32 Scheduler & batching, 33 Prefix caching |
| `MAX_NUM_SEQS` | 8 (TP1: 4) | Maximum number of concurrent sequences in the batch (--max-num-seqs). | With 13.93× of KV at 262K, the concurrency limit is the scheduler, not the cache; it also defines the captured CUDA graph sizes (1…64 tokens = 8 × (1+3) × 2). ×16 goes beyond that and gives a mean TTFT of 6.5–9.7 s. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `perfil 1M + concorrência` | --kv-cache-memory=45502283776, MAX_NUM_SEQS 3 (≈3.0M tokens) | Profile that pins a larger KV pool for 3 requests of 1M. | Uses the headroom left unused by the GMU. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `perfil Deep-context sequencial` | MAX_MODEL_LEN 1000000, MAX_NUM_SEQS 2 | Serving profile for a few requests with a huge context. | 1M × 8 asks for 8M tokens and the pool holds ~2 resident 1M requests; beyond that, vLLM preempts and re-prefills. Depends on YaRN (not validated). | 32 Scheduler & batching, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `perfil Middle ground` | 512000, MAX_NUM_SEQS 4 | Intermediate profile between long context and concurrency. | Trades half of the 1M profile's context for twice as many sequences. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `perfil Throughput/agentic` | 262144, YaRN off, MAX_NUM_SEQS 8 (8 of 9.5 possible — bf16 figure) | Default profile: native context and 8 sequences. | Maximizes the aggregate and keeps per-step buffers small. | 32 Scheduler & batching |

#### Speculative decoding

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--balance-shards` | 1 by default (use = TP size) | Distributes the draft vocabulary padding evenly across the id ranges of each TP rank. | The step waits for the slowest rank; the 47k ends up ≈97% on rank 0 (45,734 ids). A balanced 65k (32,768/32,768) dropped to 0.16 GiB/rank but had 47.8% acceptance. | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 38 Parallelism |
| `--fill-to-size` | off | Fills the vocabulary with the lowest ids not seen in the corpus. | A proxy for the BPE merge order ('a proxy, not a measurement'): in byte-level BPE, low ids tend to be more frequent merges. | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 05 Tokens & embeddings |
| `--model / DRAFT_VOCAB_MODEL` | nvidia/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-NVFP4 | Tokenizer used to count ids in the corpus. | It must be the tokenizer of the served model (vocabulary of 248,320). | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 05 Tokens & embeddings |
| `--report-only` | flag | Only prints the coverage report, without writing the vocabulary. | Lets you choose N before generating the file. | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `--speculative-config` | {"method":"mtp","num_speculative_tokens":3,"use_local_argmax_reduction":true,"disable_eagle_block_drop":true,"index_share_for_mtp_iteration":true} · {'method':'mtp','num_speculativ | JSON that enables speculative decoding using the checkpoint's own MTP layer as the drafter. · vLLM JSON arg that enables MTP as the drafter and carries the recipe's extra keys. | Before the VLLM_ARGS array fix, MTP_NUM_SPECULATIVE_TOKENS=0 emitted num_speculative_tokens:0, which vLLM rejects (gt=0); now 0 omits the flag. · The image's SpeculativeConfig rejects unknown keys, so disable_eagle_block_drop requires the backport on the day-0 image. | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |
| `build_draft_vocab.py --size` | 32768 by default (the README suggests 65536) | Size of the draft vocabulary to generate. | Choose by the printed coverage, not by size: 65k with only 8.3k real ids dropped the acceptance to 47.8%. | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `corpus:N` | suffix: repeat the corpus N times | Gives weight to a small in-distribution corpus in the frequency count. | Prioritizes real traffic without requiring a large corpus. | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `index_share_for_mtp_iteration` | true (recipe) | IndexShare MTP: reuses the QSA indexer's top-k selection computed in the draft-extend across all draft steps of the iteration. | The query moved at most N positions, so the selection is still valid; it cuts N−1 indexer runs. Largest gain on code and at high concurrency (up to +26.6% at S=8). | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 13 Sparse attention |
| `MTP_DRAFT_VOCAB` | files/draft_vocab_en_code_47k.txt (47,149 ids; default since 2026-09-10) / empty = 248,320 (fallback) · files/draft_vocab_en_code_47k.txt (default since 2026-09-10) / empty = full  | Slices the drafter's lm_head down to a subset of frequent ids (reduced vocabulary). · Points to the drafter's reduced-vocabulary id file; start.sh mounts it at /etc/vllm-draft-vocab.txt and applies patch_mtp_draft_vocab.py. | The drafter's lm_head (248320×2560×2 B = 1.18 GiB, 0.59 GiB/GPU) is read 3× per step; with 47k: 0.59 → 0.22 GiB, +9.6% on average (09-11) and +8.4% (09-16) without changing the output. Tuned on English/code (7 language-extended 65k files since 288369d); incompatible with FP8_DENSE (both override nvidia/mtp.py). · +9.6% on average (2026-09 | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 05 Tokens & embeddings |
| `MTP_INDEX_SHARE` | true (sample) / false (fallback) → index_share_for_mtp_iteration:true · true in .env.sample (start.sh default = false) | Reuses the QSA indexer's top-k computed in the 1st draft step in the following draft steps (drafter only). · .env variable that enables index_share_for_mtp_iteration in the speculative config. | Code +6.1/+4.6/+21.3/+26.6% (S=1/2/4/8), prose within noise; HumanEval 156/164, NLL 1.328 vs 1.334. The indices lag by 1 position. · code +6.1/+4.6/+21.3/+26.6% (S=1/2/4/8), prose within noise; NLL 1.328 vs 1.334; HumanEval 156/164. | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 13 Sparse attention |
| `mtp_num_hidden_layers` | 1; mtp.layer_types=['full_attention'], mtp.hybrid=true, mtp_use_dedicated_embeddings=false | One MTP layer (full QSA attention + MoE with block FP8 experts), built as mtp.layers.48. | ≈4 B parameters (NVIDIA model card), with its own lm_head of 248,320 rows; the MTP drafter does not use PLE (LMSYS). | 16 N-gram & MTP, 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `MTP_NUM_SPECULATIVE_TOKENS` | 3 / 0 disables it (does not emit the flag), 1 is a safe comparison point, 2/4 for testing; TP1 0 · 3 (default) / 0 disables it | K draft tokens per step, proposed by the MTP layer and verified by the target model. · .env variable that becomes num_speculative_tokens in --speculative-config (method mtp). | Batch-1 greedy 24.5 → 52.1 tok/s (2.13×), acceptance 72.8% (89/74.5/60% per position). K greater than 1 reuses the same MTP layer (lower acceptance) and QSA rebuilds metadata per draft step; MTP 4 was not tested due to memory risk; if position 3 accepts less than ~35%, K=2 is faster. · 24.5 → 52.1 tok/s (2.13×); acceptance 89/74.5/60% per | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |
| `num_speculative_tokens` | 3 (the MTP layer is reapplied 3×) | Number of drafts per decode step; the single MTP layer is reapplied 3 times. | K=3 because the 3rd position still accepts ~60%; if it drops below ~35%, K=2 is better (fable §3.2). The PLE short-conv state reserves these extra positions for rollback. | 35 Speculation: fundamentals, 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `use_local_argmax_reduction` | true when MTP_DRAFT_VOCAB is set · true when there is a draft vocab | Makes the drafter use get_top_tokens: a local argmax per rank and an all-gather of (value, index) pairs. · Makes the speculator call get_top_tokens: local argmax per rank + all-gather of (value, id) pairs. | It is the only path that reaches the reduced lm_head and cuts the draft all-gather from O(vocab) to O(2·tp_size) per token. · It is the only path that reaches the reduced head; cuts the all-gather from O(vocab) to O(2·tp) per token. | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 38 Parallelism |
| `VLLM_MTP_DRAFT_VOCAB` | /etc/vllm-draft-vocab.txt | Path, inside the container, of the id file read by _attach_draft_vocab in the patched mtp.py. · Env var read by the patch after load_weights to cut the drafter's lm_head down to the id subset (one id per line). | The file is bind-mounted; relative paths in MTP_DRAFT_VOCAB are resolved against the repo root because the bind mount requires an absolute path. · Cuts the drafter's lm_head shard from 0.59 to 0.22 GiB per draft step (rank 0). | 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab, 41 Recipe engineering |

| `hotfix-dsv4-assistant-final-continuation.py` | opt-in DSPARK_ENABLE_ASSISTANT_FINAL_HOTFIX=1 · off (DSPARK_ENABLE_ASSISTANT_FINAL_HOTFIX=1) | Appends &lt;｜Assistant｜&gt;&lt;think&gt; when messages ends with an assistant message (or with latest_reminder after an assistant message). · Widens the condition for appending the generation header to last = assistant (#52) or latest_reminder preceded by assistant (#120), keepin | Causal A/B: 183 coherent tokens vs 400 tokens of &lt;\|DSML\|tool_calls&gt; markup; ON preserves 98 stock token ids (#52/#120). · Without it the prompt ends on a dead EOS (1–2 stop tokens, hallucinated markup). Causal A/B: open turn → 183 coherent tokens; closed with EOS → 400 tokens of raw DSML. A self-check restores the bytes if it fail | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `hotfix-dsv4-responses-store.py` | opt-in VLLM_ENABLE_RESPONSES_API_STORE=1 · only with VLLM_ENABLE_RESPONSES_API_STORE=1 | Exact-source backport with an LRU cap on terminal bundles (DSPARK_RESPONSES_STORE_MAX_ENTRIES), protecting continuations and active producers and removing orphaned messages on error. · A single LRU for response + rendered messages + background events; previous_response_id refresh | The native stores (response/message/event) grow without bound (issue #62). · The stock store never evicts (a leak); the state lives only in memory and is lost on any restart. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `hotfix-vllm-codex-agent-message.py` | opt-in DSPARK_ENABLE_CODEX_AGENT_MESSAGE_COMPAT=1 · off (DSPARK_ENABLE_CODEX_AGENT_MESSAGE_COMPAT=1) | Converts the exact shape of Codex's agent_message into an assistant message, discarding metadata. · Converts only an agent_message with the exact keys observed into message(role=assistant); applied after #138. | Codex's private item was rejected by the API. · Irreversibly discards id/author/recipient/metadata — that is why it is opt-in. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `hotfix-vllm-issue138-responses-history.py` | opt-in DSPARK_ENABLE_ISSUE138_RESPONSES_HISTORY_COMPAT=1 · off (DSPARK_ENABLE_ISSUE138_RESPONSES_HISTORY_COMPAT=1) | Accepts only the exact format (1 string output_text part), inserts type=message and reuses the stock coercion. · Accepts exactly {"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"output_text","text":"..."}]} without an item-level type, adding only "type":"message". | History replays with an assistant item lacking type returned 400; it has a verifier in strict mode. · Compatibility with clients that resend history in this format; a singleton is required because the conversion reads content[0]. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `hotfix-vllm-redact-api-key-log.sh` | required if a key is set; no bypass via DSPARK_SKIP_HOTFIX; --status · required if DSPARK_API_KEYS or VLLM_API_KEY is set | Replaces the key values with &lt;redacted:N value(s)&gt; in the non-default args line of both entrypoints. · Replaces api_key in log_non_default_args with &lt;redacted:N value(s)&gt;, preserving the count and without mutating the live list. | log_non_default_args() printed --api-key in plaintext; it closes only the log channel (argv/env remain visible). · The log printed the keys in plaintext; the count is how you verify that a single --api-key carried N keys. It does not close /proc/&lt;pid&gt;/cmdline or docker inspect. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 41 Recipe engineering |

#### Abliteration

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `hotfix-dsv4-runtime-ablation.py` | always runs; only acts with ABLATE=1 · always runs; with ABLATE=0 it only verifies | Injects a _refusal_dir buffer into DeepseekV4DecoderLayer and, in the DSV4_ABLATE_LAYERS layers, applies y ← y − λ·(y·v)·v post-attention in fp32 with a normalized v; without ABLATE it verifies the stock state and keeps the AOT stamp. · Injects a _refusal_dir buffer (fp32, 4096)  | The direction was captured on the 0731 FP8 and transferring it to Vision-Exp is experimental; abliteration drains the draft's acceptance (0.585 vs 0.718). · Every layer has the buffer so there is a single torch.compile/AOT signature; the stamp (version, λ, layers, sha) invalidates the cache when it changes. | 19 Abliteration, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `staging da direção de ablação` | only with ABLITERATED=1 | Copies the direction to head/worker(/worker2) with SHA-256 verification and an atomic mv. | Every rank needs the same vector; a truncated copy would silently change the model. | 19 Abliteration, 41 Recipe engineering |

Inconsistency: the doc says “not yet applied”, but the compose file applies it. · Without it, the compressor ran compress→RMSNorm→RoPE→quant→write for nothing on ≈127 of every 128 decode steps: ~2× in the kernel. Only outside FULL CUDA graphs, which cannot branch on CPU metadata. | 13 Sparse attention, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |

Guard #52492 never takes the shortcut during CUDA graph capture; otherwise the graph bakes in the shortcut and corrupts long prefixes under prefix cache. | 13 Sparse attention, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `hotfix-dsv4-sp-indexer-prefill.py` | opt-in DSPARK_ENABLE_SP_INDEXER=1; only for chunks ≥ DSPARK_SP_INDEXER_MIN_KEYS · off (DSPARK_ENABLE_SP_INDEXER=1) | Each rank scores only its page-aligned slice of compressed keys [lo_r,hi_r), does a local top-k, exchanges (score, id) and does a stable, exact top-k merge with the DCP kernel (stable_topk_from_gathered_candidates_cutedsl). · Splits each request's compressed keys into per-rank sl | The Lightning indexer is replicated (each rank scores all 64 heads against all keys); the real gain was 128K TTFT −4 to −6%, far below the 1.5–1.8× estimate. · The replicated indexer costs O(queries × keys) on each GPU (≈ 80 TFLOP per 1024-token chunk at 900K). Measured only −4 to −8% on 128K TTFT and ~1 GB less MemAvailable. | 13 Sparse attention, 38 Parallelism |

Needs a 128K+ quality | 08 Position & RoPE, 13 Sparse attention |

The scratch is reserved in the dummy run before lock_workspace(). | 28 Kernels, 13 Sparse attention |

Only affects TP ≥ 2. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 26 Inside the GPU |

Accepts 4 file identities; does not clean up orphaned /dev/shm/psm_*. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 30 vLLM architecture |

Do not teach it as the applied path. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |

Measured: per-stream decode +4–13 %, ≈ 200 tok/s at 16 streams, 5.0 M KV tokens, but TTFT +22–23 % at 128K–256K. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 38 Parallelism |
| `patches/tp3/apply_tp3_patch.py (+ patches/dsv4_tp_pad.py)` | only if TP_SIZE=3; no-op at TP≤2 | Pads the output groups 8→9 keeping 8 heads/group (heads 64→72), with zero/−inf in the pad ranges. | 64 heads and 8 o_groups are not divisible by 3: stock refuses (64 % 3 != 0) or silently keeps 8//3==2 groups. Expected log DSv4 TP pad: heads 64 -&gt; 72 on every rank; inconsistency: dsv4_tp_pad.py describes the opposite strategy (R1). | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
