| `ENABLE_VL_SIDECAR / GPU_MEMORY_UTILIZATION_VISION (sidecar Qwen3-VL, removido)` | removed (along with the ds4f-vision MCP and the MoonViT lane) | Former Qwen3-VL vision sidecar running next to the text-only model. | Replaced by Vision-Exp's native vision; in the sidecar era the main KV was 13.37 GiB + 1.54 GiB for the VL at util 0.80 (1.37M + 84k tokens). | 17 Multimodal |
| `hotfix-dsv4-vision-exp.py` | always on | Attaches a fail-closed hook to DeepseekV4ForCausalLM (text-only in 0.1.1) that builds the ViT + Aligner, maps the vision.*, aligner.*, image_*, bias_vl weights, makes the class SupportsMultiModal and registers the processor. | Vision-Exp brings a 32-layer ViT and the &lt;｜deepseek_image｜&gt; token that the container image did not support; requires_raw_input_tokens = True because the hash MoE needs the ids. | 17 Multimodal, 44 DeepSeek recipe |
| `hotfix-dsv4-vision-exp.py (+ patches/vision_exp/)` | always | An import hook at the end of nvidia/model.py builds the ViT (32 layers) + Aligner, maps the vision.*/aligner.*/image_*/bias_vl weights and registers the multimodal processor; remaps ffn.gate.bias_vl → e_score_correction_bias_vl in the draft; relaxes the rejection of already-subst | Anemll 0.1.1's DeepseekV4ForCausalLM is text-only; the patch provides native image_url (max 384 tokens/image, no video) and makes image rows route with bias_vl without the hash table (#175); otherwise all image tokens would collapse into a single expert. | 17 Multimodal, 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `hotfix-vllm-empty-encoder-output.py` | always · always on | Emits a distinct empty row per request on encoder-only steps, and the scheduler finishes the encoder-only request once it has consumed the prompt (upstream 7ca49fb). · Backport of vLLM 7ca49fbe: empty sampled_token_ids per request, an is_mm_encoder_only flag and a FINISHED_STOPPE | Encoder-only steps (EC producer) emitted a phantom token 0 and got stuck in a loop (#109). · The container image made up a token 0 when an encoder-only producer sampled nothing; pre-validates both files before writing. | 17 Multimodal, 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `regras de imagem do encoder (#167/#181)` | part of the Vision-Exp hotfix | Relaxes the rejection of the already-substituted placeholder and re-enforces: images only in user messages (system/assistant → 400), tool/function text is not scanned (#167), and &lt;image&gt;…&lt;/image&gt; quoted in prose is not an image (#181). | Lets image_url survive vLLM's parser without turning ordinary text into an image. | 17 Multimodal, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `remap ffn.gate.bias_vl no drafter (nvidia/dspark.py Anemll)` | part of the Vision-Exp hotfix | Remaps ffn.gate.bias_vl → e_score_correction_bias_vl in the drafter's loader. | Stock only handled names ending in .ffn.gate.bias; without this the drafter's vision weight did not load. | 24 Checkpoints, 17 Multimodal |
| `salt_mm_image_hash (#172)` | part of the Vision-Exp hotfix | Salts the multimodal hash with num_tokens and does not use the processor cache for images. | vLLM's hash covers only the image bytes, but the block has 122–125 tokens depending on start_pos % 4; without the salt the encoder cache would return embeddings of the wrong size. | 17 Multimodal |
| `vision_exp/image_processor.py` | part of the Vision-Exp hotfix | Resizes within a ≤ 384-token budget (minimum 147,456 pixels, max ratio 8), pads with gray 127, normalizes (x−0.5)/0.5 in bf16 and builds the block [pad] + IMAGE_START + rows + IMAGE_END. | Defines how many tokens each image takes and where the block starts in the prompt. | 17 Multimodal |
| `vision_exp/vision.py (ViT + Aligner)` | part of the Vision-Exp hotfix | 14×14 patches → Linear; pre-norm RMSNorm blocks with bidirectional attention and 2D RoPE + SwiGLU MLP; the Aligner does a 3×3 unfold (9 patches per token) and Linear → GELU → Linear to 4096. | The ×3 per-side downsample caps the tokens per image (≤ 384). | 17 Multimodal |

#### Operations / Docker

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `build-dspark-vllm-runtime.sh` | DSPARK_BASE_IMAGE (vllm-dspark-runtime:mia-raf-pr1), DSPARK_VLLM_IMAGE (…:dspark-nvfp4-stage-c), WORKER_BUILD=1, --tag-selftest · lane B: base unholy-fusion → mia-raf-pr1 → Stage A | Builds the Stage-C image (DSpark overlay + Stage A/B/C) on the head and, optionally, on the worker. · Builds the Stage-C image: applies the overlay on top of ghcr.io/bjk110/vllm-spark:unholy-fusion-prod-ready and then the three string-replace Dockerfiles. | Historical alternative to the prebuilt Anemll image; the basis of the 200K/16 lane. · It is the historical/optional lane (200K/16, Keys mask) and the readable reference implementation of DSpark. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `cadeia de boot do command: (docker-compose.dspark.yml)` | fixed order; each step with \|\| exit 1 | Sequence of ~21 boot steps: encoder + #21, #31, #55, #22, #79, #117, six v0.27 backports, redaction, store, compat, Vision-Exp, optional gates, TP=3 and finally exec vllm serve. | Order matters (patches that edit the same file recognize combined post-images) and must be the same on both ranks. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `contrato de exit codes do start (exit 3)` | 0 ok; 1 failure; 2 invalid config; 3 already running (stale head or worker) | start exits 3 if a rank is already running; a remote sentinel 42 distinguishes “rank exists” from an ssh failure (255). | After a reboot, unless-stopped restores the ranks; exiting 1 caused a retry storm in systemd — with SuccessExitStatus=3 the supervisor understands. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `docker-compose.dspark-nfs.override.yml` | opt-in with DSPARK_WORKER_HF_NFS=1 | Override that makes the worker mount the head's HF cache via an NFS volume (dspark-hf, ro) and keep the JIT caches local and writable (triton, tilelang, vllm-cache, flashinfer, b12x-cute-cache, nccl-fr). | Avoids a 2nd copy of 157 GiB; the volume is created with nfsvers=4.2,ro,nconnect=8,rsize=wsize=1048576,hard,timeo=600; weight loading goes over the same RoCE link. | 24 Checkpoints, 27 Unified memory |
| `docker-compose.stage-c.override.yml` | manual; start does not merge it on its own | Injects the Stage-C image's kill-switches (DSPARK_SLOT_CLAMP, VLLM_USE_B12X_WO_PROJECTION, VLLM_DSPARK_*, VLLM_DSV4_*, VLLM_TRITON_MLA_SPARSE, VLLM_SKIP_INIT_MEMORY_CHECK). | No-op (warning) on Anemll 0.1.1; it only makes sense with the Stage-C image and the Stage-C block of the .env. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `dspark-numeric-knobs.sh (dspark_validate_numeric_knobs)` | MAX_NUM_SEQS 1–4096; MTP_NUM_TOKENS 0–64; MAX_NUM_BATCHED_TOKENS 1–8388608; rejects &gt;10 digits | Validates and normalizes (10#) the numeric knobs before any shell arithmetic. | Avoids 64-bit overflow or octal interpretation that would leak into the compose's $(( )) (e.g., capture size). | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `files/nfs-share.sh (export NFS)` | opt-in DSPARK_WORKER_HF_NFS=1; export ro,sync,no_subtree_check,no_root_squash,insecure,fsid=0 | Brings up a privileged Alpine container with the kernel nfsd (host network), NFSv4 only (rpc.nfsd 8), exporting the HF cache read-only to the worker's IP and the interface's /24. | Avoids a 2nd copy of 157 GiB on the worker. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `mecanismo de patch fail-closed no entrypoint` | always; each step chained with \|\| exit 1 (issue #107) | Textual patching of vLLM's installed Python files with exact anchors, idempotency markers (# [issueXX-hotfix]), a stock→patched SHA-256 pin, compilation before publishing, atomic replacement, verification and byte-exact rollback. | Everything is applied before exec vllm serve; previously start applied .sh scripts and restarted compose in the middle of the load, knocking the head's TCPStore out from under the worker (#38). | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `normalização do .env (BOM/CRLF)` | always | Strips BOM and CRLF in a private 0600 copy of the .env before sourcing it; the worker receives an atomic 0600 copy. | Parsing robustness without forcing the operator to edit the file (it stays byte-identical). | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `padrão source-exact / fail-closed dos patchers` | all modern hotfixes patches/*.py | Each patcher pins the target file's SHA-256, builds the post-image in memory, runs compile(), publishes via an atomic os.replace with fsync, re-verifies and rolls back on failure. | Refusing different code avoids a silently wrong patch on another version; undoing it requires recreating the containers, not restarting them. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `preflight --check dos hotfixes` | sensitive opt-ins enabled + #117 always; worker first, then head | Runs docker compose run --rm --no-deps --entrypoint python3 … /opt/&lt;patch&gt;.py --check before bringing up any rank. | A patch that does not match the source blocks the boot before any rank comes up, without leaving the pair half-patched. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `prepare-dspark-model-cache.sh` | --official (writes ABLITERATED=0) / --abliterated / --yes; PREPARE_WORKER, THIS_NODE_HF_CACHE, IMAGE_PYTHON=/usr/bin/python3 | Downloads the pinned checkpoint with snapshot_download inside a container of the image itself (HF online forced, 120/30 s timeouts), writes refs/main and verifies that every shard in model.safetensors.index.json exists; repeats on the worker if NFS=0. | Allows serving offline (HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1) with the exact revision (~157 GiB). | 24 Checkpoints |
| `recriar containers vs docker restart` | changing a gate requires stop/start (recreate) | Any hotfix gate change requires recreating the containers. | docker restart reuses the already-patched writable layer, so the new gate would have no effect or the patcher would see an unexpected file. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `scripts/ci-validate.sh` | no GPU; does not measure tok/s · CPU-only CI (.github/workflows/validate.yml) | CPU validation: script syntax, py_compile of the patches, ~60 unit tests against fixtures of the pinned sources, and regression guards. · Runs the tests/ and scripts/test-*.py suites on CPU. | Forbids reintroducing the CPU-side budget (.cpu(, .tolist() and #26 v1; checks cap #27 default 1, capture rounding, a VLLM_HOST-aware healthcheck and TP forced to 2. · Proves pinned identities, transformations and compose wiring without needing the GPUs; tok/s requires the pair of Sparks. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `scripts/fixtures/* (arquivos upstream pinados)` | e.g., dspark-rope-swa, issue117, issue144-effort-align, dspark-block-k | Copies of the stock files from the pinned image, used by the patchers' CPU tests. | They make it possible to test each hotfix against the exact text of its target without bringing up the container. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `scripts/test-hotfix-atomic-transaction.py` | CPU test | Tests the whole-script transaction in the .sh files (staging, byte-exact rollback, permissions) and the frozen inventory of hunks by SHA-256. | Ensures that a patch that fails midway does not leave the image half-patched. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `scripts/test-python-hotfix-failclosed.py` | CPU test | Checks the order and the \|\| exit 1 of the Python patchers in the compose file. | A patch failure must abort the boot instead of serving a model without the fix. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `sincronização atômica do env` | always | The worker receives the env through a 0600 temporary file + mv -f. | A failed transfer could expose or truncate an env containing secrets. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `sincronização dos patchers ao worker` | always (dscp; tar of the whole patches/ directory in TP3) | Copies compose, .env, proposer, each patcher, patches/vision_exp/ and patches/tp3/ to the worker. | Both ranks need the same bytes and the same patch order. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `start-deepseek-v4-flash-dspark.sh` | --host / --port (CLI &gt; env) | Launcher: validates the config, resolves the RoCEv2 GIDs, syncs compose/env/patches to the worker, runs the --check preflight, brings up the worker (rank 1, headless) and then the head (rank 0), waits for /v1/models, and runs the smoke test and the boot-shape warmup. | Worker first because the head hosts the TCPStore; restarting the head midway through the load broke the handshake (#38). | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `start-tp3.sh` | --max-num-seqs N, --host, --port; exports DSPARK_TP3=1, TP_SIZE=3, NNODES=3 · launcher for TP_SIZE=3 | Launcher for the 3-Spark lane. · Launcher that brings up the 3-node variant with the padding patch. | 16 slots, ~3× KV (≈5M tokens), ≈200 tok/s aggregate at 16 streams; prefill 4–13% slower up to 64K and ≈22% slower at 128K–256K; first boot ~8 min. · First boot ≈ 8 min (engine init 193 s); a hash-based marker turns an old patch STALE and requires docker rm -f. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `stop-deepseek-v4-flash-dspark.sh` | --nfs (also stops dspark-nfs and removes the dspark-hf volume) | Stops the ranks (and optionally the NFS server). | Stop + start is how you recreate the containers after changing patch flags. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `WARN de bind aberto e permissões do .env` | always (never fails the start) | Warns if the API binds to 0.0.0.0 without a key or if the .env with secrets is readable by group/others. | Operational security without blocking the boot; the recipe recommends chmod 600 .env.dspark and restricting the port on the network. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `wrappers ssh/scp (BatchMode, ConnectTimeout)` | BatchMode=yes, ConnectTimeout=10 | All of the launcher's ssh/scp calls use non-interactive mode and a connection timeout. | ~100 ssh calls without a timeout made a “slow start” ambiguous. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |

Live acceptance: 145 cases, zero warnings. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |

| `draft_token_lengths (ModelRunnerOutput)` | overlay Stage-C | dict[str,int] field with the scheduled draft length per request. | The async scheduler uses it to size the next placeholder list before the real ids reach the CPU. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `hotfix-dsv4-adaptive-prefill-chunk.py` | opt-in DSPARK_ENABLE_ADAPTIVE_CHUNK=1; rejected · off (DSPARK_ENABLE_ADAPTIVE_CHUNK=1); rejected | threshold = 1024 if there is an active decode, otherwise max_num_batched_tokens. · With no active decode in self.running, the chunk cap becomes max_num_batched_tokens (8192). | It was meant to avoid 8 chunks for a cold 8K at c=1, but the larger workspace in unified memory dropped MemAvailable to 2.36/2.28 GB. · A cold c=1 paid a fixed per-chunk cost (86 all-reduces, ~1.5k launches) 8 times per 8K (estimated −10–25 % TTFT ≤ 32K), but MemAvailable fell to 2.36/2.28 GB. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 27 Unified memory |
| `hotfix-dsv4-issue27-partial-prefill-concurrency.py` | always · always on (r3) | Breaks out of admission when the in-flight prefills (counted exactly from self.running, r3 repair #211) reach DSPARK_MAX_INFLIGHT_PREFILLS. · In the waiting admission loop, counts partial prefills in self.running in parity with stock's _inflight_prefills and does a break when the | In vLLM 0.25.2, several admitted prefills ate the budget and decodes received num_new_tokens==0 (skipped, not preempted). With the fix + chunk 1024: x8 8K/16K/32K worst decode ~15 tok/s (was 2.07/0.47/0.36) and x8 spread from ~46× → ~1.05×. · v1 defined max_num_partial_prefills but did not read it; long prefills consumed the budget and de | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `hotfix-dsv4-issue43-decode-fairness-and-diag.py` | always · always on (no-op with the defaults) | While iterating RUNNING, if the item is a prefill chunk, reserves 1 decode step for each active decode behind it and caps the chunk; records zero-token skips and tokens per request (DSPARK_ISSUE43_SCHED_DIAG). · When scheduling a prefill chunk, reserves num_sampled_tokens_per_ste | Even with #27, the decode-rate spread was 0.107–0.238; with the current defaults (1024/8192/cap 1) it is a no-op, but it prevents mis-tuning from resurrecting #27 (worst ITL 2790 → 67 ms credited to #27). · After #27 (worst ITL 2790 → 67 ms), a per-request decode-rate dispersion of 0.107–0.238 remained; protects against mis-tuning. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `scheduler.py (overlay DSpark)` | overlay Stage-C | In async mode, resizes spec_token_ids = [-1] * draft_len with the confidence-based length; use_eagle() ⇒ num_lookahead_tokens = num_spec_tokens. | Allows variable per-request draft lengths without breaking async scheduling. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `tests/sim/test_issue43_scheduler_sim.py` | CPU test (pure-Python simulator) | Scheduler simulator showing that no active decode is skipped while a prefill is scheduled in the six cold cells (32K/62K × 2/4/8). | Makes it possible to prove fairness properties without a GPU; the application test checks py_compile, idempotence and ordering with #27. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 41 Recipe engineering |

#### Speculative decoding

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `_trim_rejected_target_context` | Stage-C overlay (legacy path) | Removes the rejected suffixes from the drafter context on the CPU. | Still requires uniform lengths; the GPU-mask path replaces it. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `captura de features DSpark (nvidia/model.py)` | Stage-C overlay | When passing through a target layer, runs hc_post, breaks the fused post/pre chain and writes hidden_states.mean(dim=1) into _dspark_hidden_buffer. | The drafter consumes the mean of the 4 mHC streams; breaking the fusion has a cost, hence the deferred-capture option. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 15 Hyper-connections |
| `confidence_threshold_prefix_length` | Stage-C overlay | Picks the longest prefix with survival ≥ threshold. | It is non-anticipating, and therefore compatible with rejection sampling (it does not bias the distribution). | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `config/speculative.py (overlay DSpark)` | Stage-C overlay | Adds dspark as a method, draft_sample_method, hf_config_override (deepseek_v4 → deepseek_v4_dspark with n_predict = dspark_block_size), compute_hash with aux hidden states and use_eagle() including dspark. | Shows how a new speculative method enters the config; in lane A, Anemll maps it to deepseek_mtp with n_predict = num_nextn_predict_layers, hence the forced k=6. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `DeepSeekV4DSparkAttention (overlay dspark.py)` | Stage-C overlay | Drafter attention with the same projections as the target and its own internal KV main_kv_cache[max_num_seqs, 128, 512] bf16; Q is RMS-normalized without a weight, non-causal attention over window + block with a sink, and fused_inv_rope_fp8_quant before wo_a. | The drafter does not register KV layers with vLLM (a different contract from MTP), which requires managing slots and rejections manually. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 12 MLA & compact KV |
| `DeepSeekV4DSparkConfidenceHead` | Stage-C overlay | fp32 Linear(hidden + 256 → 1) over [dense state, Markov embedding] that estimates the conditional acceptance per position. | Feeds the confidence-based schedulers that shorten the verified length. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |
| `DeepSeekV4DSparkLayer` | Stage-C overlay | Drafter stage: stage 0 has main_proj (hidden × len(target_ids) → hidden) + main_norm; each stage runs mHC pre → DSpark attention → mHC post → MoE (the same DeepseekV4MoE class as the target, 256 experts + 1 shared expert, learned routing: hashing only applies in layers 0–2) → mHC | The drafter reuses the target's architecture (mHC + MoE); in the step cost model it reads ≈ 0.9 GB per rank. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 15 Hyper-connections |
| `DeepSeekV4DSparkMarkovHead` | Stage-C overlay; rank 256 | markov_w1 (vocab → 256) and markov_w2 (256 → vocab) produce a logit bias that depends on the previous token, applied sequentially, position by position. | Provides dependency between block positions without rerunning the backbone; sharded, it costs 12 serialized collectives per step (129,280 × 256 bf16 = 66 MB per matrix). | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 03 Tensors & algebra |
| `DeepSeekV4DSparkModel.draft` | Stage-C overlay | Builds the block [anchor, noise, …], runs it through the layers in parallel, collapses it with hc_head and then, sequentially, adds the Markov bias and takes the argmax position by position. | The anchor in the first position follows DeepSpec's training; the parallel pass makes the drafter cheap and the Markov head recovers the left→right dependency. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `dspark_proposer.py (DSparkProposer)` | Stage-C overlay (identical copy in recipe/vllm/v1/spec_decode/) | DSpark proposer: no KV in vLLM (block_size = 1), persistent buffers, the drafter inside a PIECEWISE CUDAGraphWrapper, per-request slots, rectangular or ragged context, and the _last_draft_lengths decision. | Fixed buffers and padding with noise_token_id, zeros and slot 0 allow CUDA graph replay with variable batches. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `DSparkModelSpec.from_hf_config / infer_dspark_num_draft_layers` | Stage-C overlay | Validates the DSpark config fields and reconstructs the number of stages from contiguous mtp.N.*. | The HF config may say num_nextn_predict_layers=1 even with more stages in the weights. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 24 Checkpoints |
| `guard de lote não-uniforme (DSparkProposer)` | Stage-C overlay | If rows % batch_size != 0, logs once and returns zeros, turning speculation off for that step. | Avoids killing the WorkerProc; since it runs before the ragged branch, non-divisible batches skip speculation (interpretation of the inventory). | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `guarda de slot DSpark (clamp + DSPARK_STALE_SLOT_INDEX)` | Stage-C overlay; DSPARK_SLOT_CLAMP=1 | Does clamp(0, num_rows−1) on the device (safe during capture) and only logs outside capture. | A stale slot after condensation in long context triggered indexSelectSmallIndex and killed the worker; a bad draft is simply rejected by the target. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `hardware_aware_prefix_schedule` | Stage-C overlay | Admits prefix tokens in decreasing order of survival across all requests in the batch and keeps the point that maximizes E[accepted] × steps_per_second(batch_tokens). | Accounts for the verifier getting more expensive as the batch grows; uses the curve from VLLM_DSPARK_SPS_CURVE. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 45 Benchmarking |
| `hotfix-dsv4-replicate-markov-head.py` | opt-in DSPARK_ENABLE_REPLICATE_MARKOV=1; off · off (DSPARK_ENABLE_REPLICATE_MARKOV=1) | Replaces the Markov head's VocabParallelEmbedding + ParallelLMHead with nn.Embedding + ReplicatedLinear (66 MB each). · Replaces the sharded Markov head with local nn.Embedding + ReplicatedLinear. | Eliminates 12 serialized collectives per step from the 6-step draft loop, but it is correct with no gain: c=6 122 vs 148 and −0.44 GiB of KV on the head. · Eliminates 12 serialized collectives per step, but measured: no gain at c=1, c=6 aggregate 122 vs 148 and KV −0.44 GiB → OFF. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 38 Parallelism |
| `hotfix-vllm-dspark-block-k.py` | opt-in DSPARK_ENABLE_DSPARK_BLOCK_K=1 · off (DSPARK_ENABLE_DSPARK_BLOCK_K=1) | Adds self.method != "dspark" to the divisibility rule in config/speculative.py (stock 3f1abd1c… → 7fffe035…). · Appends self.method != "dspark" to the k % n_predict divisibility rule; the launcher relaxes MTP_NUM_TOKENS to ≥ 1. | In DSpark the stages are stacked in a single parallel pass and never reused per step; k=5 gives +2–10% single-stream with unchanged per-position acceptance. · The rule exists for layer reuse in classic MTP, not for stacked stages. k=5 vs k=6: 8K greedy 56.6 vs 55.3, 32K 56.6 vs 53.6, same acceptance — the gain comes from the cheaper step. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |
| `keys-concurrency.patch — Patch 1 (slot estável por requisição)` | built into the overlay | Maps req_id → slot (_req_id_to_slot, _free_slots, lowest free slot first) and does gather/scatter by slot_index; the runner passes req_ids. | With max_num_seqs&gt;1 the acceptance dropped to ~0 because batch condensation moved requests between rows and each one read another's history. Author: drowzeys (Keys) — unrelated to API keys. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `keys-concurrency.patch — Patch 2 (contexto ragged)` | only with VLLM_DSPARK_GPU_REJECTED_CONTEXT_MASK=1 | Detects segments of different lengths, computes the anchor per request and writes the ring buffer by query_start_loc. | Fixes HTTP 500 got 41 rows for batch_size=2 in mixed chunked-prefill steps; mixed steps are never captured, so Python loops are safe. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `keys-concurrency.patch — Patch 2b (detecção ragged sem rejeição)` | built into the overlay | Moves the ragged detection out of if num_rejected_tokens_gpu is not None, with rejected defaulting to zeros. | Prefill steps without rejection still returned 500 (166 rows for batch_size=3). Validation: GSM8K N=8 93.5% vs 95.0% sequential, 97.5% agreement, 0 errors. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `load_weights do drafter (mtp.* → model.layers.N)` | Stage-C overlay | Loads only mtp.&lt;stage&gt;.* as model.layers.&lt;num_hidden_layers + stage&gt;.*, renames scales, slices attn_sink per rank and views E8M0 scales as uint8. | Converting E8M0 numerically would zero out scales (2⁻⁷ → 0); the stacked mapping must not confuse markov_w1 with the FFN's w1. | 24 Checkpoints, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `make_dspark_warmup_draft_token_ids` | Stage-C overlay | On the first propose, returns a fixed block of noise_token_id instead of empty lists or −1. | There is no generated-token feature yet; a fixed tensor keeps shapes stable for the graph. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `map_dspark_stacked_param_name` | Stage-C overlay | Maps attn.wq_a/attn.wkv → attn.fused_wqa_wkv (shards 0/1) and shared_experts.w1/w3 → gate_up_proj, ignoring .experts.. | Stacked parameters fuse GEMMs; the segment-aware mapping avoids confusing names (tested by test_dspark_stacked_mapping.py). | 24 Checkpoints |
| `montagem do --speculative-config no entrypoint` | always | The speculative-config JSON is built inside the container, accepting only probabilistic\|greedy. | The raw .env value would lose its quotes or could inject JSON. | 41 Recipe engineering, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `patches/keys-concurrency.patch` | Stage-C lane (Keys, commit 7e4d94bb…) | Makes DSpark's main KV slots stable per request (not per row position in the batch) and uses ragged query_start_loc in mixed prefill/decode batches. | Prerequisite for a correct max_num_seqs&gt;1 in Stage-C, together with VLLM_DSPARK_GPU_REJECTED_CONTEXT_MASK=1. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `recipe/overlay/vllm/models/deepseek_v4/nvidia/dspark.py` | Stage-C overlay (not loaded in Anemll) | Implementation of the DSpark draft: replicates the residual into hc_mult streams (x.unsqueeze(1).repeat(1, self.config.hc_mult, 1)), uses HCHeadOp from vllm.model_executor.layers.mhc, a Markov head and a confidence head. | A reading reference for how a block drafter handles hyper-connections and the sequential Markov refinement. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 15 Hyper-connections |
| `speculative_acceptance_confidence / cumulative_survival` | Stage-C overlay | Acceptance probability Σ_x min(p(x), q(x)) = 1 − TV(p,q) and survival of a prefix as the product of the conditional acceptances. | It is the theory behind confidence-based length cutting. | 35 Speculation: fundamentals, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `store_main_kv / _store_main_kv_ragged` | Stage-C overlay | Writes the target features into the ring buffer by position (slot = pos % window), keeping the old value at rejected positions; the ragged version uses per-request query_start_loc. | The drafter's state lives outside the paged KV; without the mask, rejected drafts would pollute the window. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |

### Parameters (env vars, flags, configs, profiles)

#### API / serving

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--api-key` | empty (no auth) / 1 key (VLLM_API_KEY) / N keys (DSPARK_API_KEYS → a single --api-key k1 k2 …) · a single --api-key k1 k2 … (built from DSPARK_API_KEYS) | Bearer authentication on the /v1, /v2 and /inference routes. · Keys accepted by the OpenAI-compatible API. | The flag is list[str] (argparse nargs): repeating it would overwrite it, hence a single flag with N values. Routes outside these prefixes (/invocations, /generative_scoring, /tokenize, /metrics…) remain keyless → network-level control is required. · Repeating --api-key would overwrite it (nargs last-wins), so the compose builds a single f | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--host` | ${VLLM_HOST:-127.0.0.1} in the compose; .env sets 0.0.0.0; start's --host overrides | Bind address of the API's HTTP server. | 0.0.0.0 exposes the API on the LAN without authentication → the launcher emits a WARN; the healthcheck maps the wildcard to 127.0.0.1. Inconsistency: the compose fallback is 127.0.0.1, but the example .env opens 0.0.0.0. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--port` | ${VLLM_PORT:-8888}; start's --port overrides | HTTP port of the OpenAI-compatible API. | The launcher validates 1–65535 with decimal normalization (10#) so that leading zeros are not interpreted as octal. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--served-model-name` | ${SERVED_MODEL_NAME:-deepseek-v4-flash-vision-exp}; accepts space-separated aliases | Name(s) exposed in /v1/models and accepted in the model field of requests. | Probes, warmup and smoke use the 1st alias; old benchmark scripts still have deepseek-v4-flash-0731 as the default and need --model deepseek-v4-flash-vision-exp. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 44 DeepSeek recipe |
| `--tokenizer-mode` | deepseek_v4 | Uses the checkpoint's Python encoder (encoding_dsv4.py) instead of a Jinja chat template. | The checkpoint has no Jinja: the encoder is the chat template and is the target of hotfixes #21, #52, #144 and Vision-Exp. | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--tokenizer-mode deepseek_v4` | fixed | Uses vLLM's DeepSeek-V4 tokenizer wrapper, which calls the checkpoint's Python encoder. | The checkpoint ships no Jinja chat_template; the conversation, reasoning and tool format lives in encoding/encoding_dsv4.py. | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DEFAULT_CHAT_TEMPLATE_KWARGS` | built at boot (contents not detailed in the inventory) | Env var built at boot and passed to vllm serve as the default chat template/encoder kwargs. | Lets you pin encoder defaults on the server for clients that do not send chat_template_kwargs (interpretation; the inventory does not show the contents). | 34 API, tools & JSON, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DSPARK_API_KEYS` | empty / "k1 k2 …" (quotes required; rejects CR/LF/VT/FF, backslash, a token starting with -) · empty; space-separated list | Several revocable keys, passed as a single --api-key with N values. · List of keys turned into a single --api-key k1 k2 …. | It must be set only in the .env (a divergent value in the environment → exit 2); the keys are visible in ps/docker inspect and rotation requires a stop/start. · Repeating the flag would overwrite it (last-wins); the parser rejects CR/LF, backslash and tokens starting with -, and it is mutually exclusive with VLLM_API_KEY. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_ASSISTANT_FINAL_HOTFIX` | 0 / 1 · 0; 1 applies #52/#120 | Adds the generation header &lt;｜Assistant｜&gt;&lt;think&gt; when messages ends in an assistant turn (or latest_reminder after assistant). · Appends the generation header after a final assistant turn (#52) and after a latest_reminder that follows an assistant turn (#120). | Stock renders EOS and the model generates from a dead state (empty turns / hallucinated DSML) (#52/#120). · Fixes the empty-turn loop of agent harnesses that resend the conversation ending on the assistant turn. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 05 Tokens & embeddings |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_CODEX_AGENT_MESSAGE_COMPAT` | 0 / 1 | Converts Codex's agent_message item (one input_text part) into an assistant message. · Converts Codex's agent_message into message(role=assistant). | Compatibility with the Codex client; drops id/author/recipient. · Irreversibly drops id/author/recipient/metadata — that is why it is opt-in. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_ISSUE138_RESPONSES_HISTORY_COMPAT` | 0 / 1 | Accepts an assistant history item without type that has an output_text part. · Accepts an assistant output_text item without an item-level type in the Responses API history. | Full-history replays from some clients returned HTTP 400. · Compatibility with clients that resend history in this format. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DSPARK_ENCODING_FILE` | empty = auto-discovery of encoding/encoding_dsv4.py in the snapshot (prefers DSPARK_REVISION; fallback 0731 / /models/...) | The checkpoint's Python encoder to install inside vLLM. | Without it: a WARN, and it skips the copy, the effort mapping and fix #21; the file must exist on both the head and the worker nodes. | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DSPARK_RESPONSES_STORE_MAX_ENTRIES` | 256 (positive integer; the verifier accepts 2..16 in test mode) · 256 (integer &gt; 0) | LRU cap on terminal bundles (response/message/event) in the store. · Maximum number of terminal responses retained in the Responses API's LRU store. | It is not a byte limit; active work can exceed it temporarily. · Limits the count, not the bytes; in-progress or pinned entries can exceed it temporarily. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `pi-models.dspark.example.json` | thinkingLevelMap low/high/max; input text+image; contextWindow 1048576; maxTokens 32768; supportsThinkingTokenBudget false; chatTemplateKwargs thinking.enabled/effort (omitWhenOff) | Example configuration of the pi client for this server. | Declaring the budget capability as true with the boot flag at 0 makes every request fail (“not yet supported by the V2 model runner”); pi maps the levels minimal 1024 / low 2048 / medium 8192 / high 16384. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `VLLM_API_KEY` | empty / a single key | vLLM's native single key. · A single key (native alias of --api-key). | Mutually exclusive with DSPARK_API_KEYS (the entrypoint and scripts exit with 2 if both are set). · Routes outside /v1, /v2, /inference are left without a key (including POST /invocations); requires the log redaction. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `VLLM_ENABLE_RESPONSES_API_STORE` | 0 / 1 · 0; 1 enables the store and applies the limit backport | Enables the native Responses API store (store=true + previous_response_id) and applies the bounded-store backport. · Enables the Responses API response store and the patch that bounds it with an LRU. | The native store grows without bound; the state is lost on any restart. · The stock store never evicts (memory leak). | 34 API, tools & JSON |

#### Abliteration

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ABLATE` | internal; 1 only via ABLITERATED=1 (ABLATE=1 alone → exit 2) · 0; 1 exports DSV4_ABLATE_FILE | Turns on the ablation hook; the entrypoint exports DSV4_ABLATE_FILE=/cache/huggingface/dspark-ablation/direction_r1.pt (any value other than 0/1 → exit 2). · With 1, exports DSV4_ABLATE_FILE and the patcher injects the refusal-direction projection; with 0 the patcher only checks  | Prevents turning on ablation without going through the terms-of-use flow and the direction check. · Set by the launcher when ABLITERATED=1; ablation changes the AOT cache stamp. | 19 Abliteration |
| `ABLITERATED` | 0 (official) / 1 (official + runtime refusal-direction projection) · 0; 1 makes the launcher export ABLATE=1 | Product flag for directional ablation. · Launcher flag that turns on runtime ablation of the refusal direction. | 1 implies ABLATE=1 and requires RESPONSIBLE_USE.md + direction_r1.pt (18 KiB) from a gated repo — it does not download a modified 157 GiB checkpoint. · Avoids editing the weights: the projection is injected into the forward pass by the runtime ablation hotfix. | 19 Abliteration |
| `DSPARK_ABLATE_DIRECTION_REPO / _FILE / _SHA256` | drowzeys/keys-DeepSeekV4-Flash-GA-0731-Dspark-Abliterated-Anchored-Tensors / ablit/refusal_direction_r1.pt / 6e4d8a8f… | Source of the gated direction download. | Local fallback files/direction_r1.pt (torch pickle with a float tensor broad, a 4096-dim unit vector). | 19 Abliteration, 24 Checkpoints |
| `DSPARK_ABLATE_SOURCE_FILE` | $HF_CACHE/dspark-ablation/direction_r1.pt; SHA-256 6e4d8a8f… | Source file of the refusal direction. | Copied to each node with SHA-256 verification. | 19 Abliteration |
| `DSV4_ABLATE_FILE` | /cache/huggingface/dspark-ablation/direction_r1.pt when ABLATE=1 | File with the 4096-dim refusal direction (broad or directions[0]). | It is validated (finite, norm &gt; 0, 4096 dims); the direction was captured on the 0731 FP8 and transferring it to Vision-Exp is experimental. | 19 Abliteration |
| `DSV4_ABLATE_LAMBDA` | 3.5 (finite, ≥0) · 3.5 (finite, ≥ 0) | Strength λ of the projection y ← y − λ·(y·v)·v. · Strength λ of the projection y ← y − λ(y·v)v applied after attention. | λ ≥ 4 risks degenerating long CoT; it is part of the AOT cache compatibility stamp. · λ ≥ 4 risks degenerating long CoT (docs/ENVS.md:120). | 19 Abliteration |
| `DSV4_ABLATE_LAYERS` | 10-42 (ordered range within 0–42) | Decoder layers where the projection is applied. · Range of decoder layers that receive the ablation projection. | Excludes the DSpark draft layers (43–45). · Part of the AOT cache stamp: changing the range invalidates torch_compile_cache. | 19 Abliteration, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |

#### Attention / kernels

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--attention-config` | {"use_fp4_indexer_cache":true}, only with DSPARK_ENABLE_MXFP4_INDEXER_CACHE=1 · absent; {"use_fp4_indexer_cache":true} if DSPARK_ENABLE_MXFP4_INDEXER_CACHE=1 | Turns on the Lightning indexer's K cache in MXFP4. · Passes options to the attention backend; here it turns on the lightning indexer's K cache in MXFP4. | The indexer logits read half the bytes per key; the allocation stays at 132 B/row in the pinned image. · Halves the bytes read per scored key; the risk is top-k selection drift at deep context, which is why it sits behind a quality gate. | 23 Quantized KV cache, 13 Sparse attention |
| `_C128A_TOPK_ALIGNMENT` | 128 (8192 entries at 1M) | Maximum number of C128 entries = ceil(ceil(max_model_len/128)/128)*128. | At 1M context that is 8192 compressed entries per C128A layer. | 13 Sparse attention |
| `_DECODE_MAX_TOKENS` | 64 | Up to 64 rows, FlashInfer uses the dedicated decode kernel; above that it goes to the prefill orchestrator. | It is the threshold that #141 respects when slicing large calls. | 28 Kernels, 13 Sparse attention |
| `_DECODE_SPLIT_TILE` | 64 | Size of the decode split-K partitions: cdiv(topk,64) + cdiv(extra_topk,64). | Splits the key axis to parallelize decode and combines the partitions via LSE. | 28 Kernels |
| `_SPARSE_PREFILL_TOPK_ALIGNMENT` | 128 | Alignment of the sparse prefill top-k. | FlashMLA prefill requires topk % B_TOPK == 0 (64 for h_q=64, 128 for h_q=128). | 28 Kernels, 13 Sparse attention |
| `attn_sink` | 1 learned logit per head | Extra logit added to the softmax denominator, allowing the model to attend to nothing. | The DSpark and FlashInfer kernels receive the sink; TP=3 padding uses −inf (exp(−inf)=0), and so do padded heads on SM120. | 06 Attention, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `cabeças q padded (SM120)` | 16 / 32 / 64 / 128 | The SM120 kernel only accepts these query-head counts; the padded attn_sink is set to −inf. | With TP, the local heads must be rounded up to one of these shapes. | 28 Kernels, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_C128A_PREFILL_CACHE` | 0 / 1 | Reuses the C128A index conversion across layers of the same forward pass. · Reuses the C128A index conversion across layers within the same forward pass. | The changelog warns that no end-to-end gain is implied. · Reduces conversions, but no E2E gain is claimed. | 13 Sparse attention |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_ROPE_SWA_FIX` | 0 / 1 · 0; 1 applies plain RoPE to the SWA layers | 1: sparse-SWA layers (compress_ratio ≤ 1: layers 0–1 + the drafter's 3) use plain RoPE (factor 1.0, θ=10000) instead of YaRN (port of vllm#54815). · Turns on the port of vllm#54815: layers without a compressor use RoPE with factor 1 instead of YaRN ×16. | In the DSV4 reference, YaRN applies only to the layers with a compressor; a 128K+ quality gate is pending. · Requires a 128K+ quality A/B before becoming the default, because the checkpoint may have adapted to the embedding as served. | 08 Position & RoPE, 13 Sparse attention |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_SP_INDEXER` | 0 / 1 (ON in the 2026-09-02 A/B) · 0; 1 turns on the sequence-parallel indexer in prefill | Turns on the SP indexer hotfix (item 6), which partitions the Lightning indexer's scoring across ranks. · Splits the prefill lightning indexer's scoring across ranks (sequence parallel) with an exact top-k merge. | 128K TTFT −4 to −6% (76.0/74.4 s vs 79.0/81.2 s); 8K/32K unchanged; RULER-lite 8/8. · Measured −4 to −8% on 128K TTFT (far below the projected 1.5–1.8×) and costs ~1 GB of MemAvailable. | 13 Sparse attention, 38 Parallelism |
| `DSPARK_SP_INDEXER_MIN_KEYS` | 8192 compressed keys (= 32K tokens at ratio 4); 0 disables it even with the patch applied · 8192 (0 disables it even with the patch) | Minimum chunk size (in compressed keys) for using the partitioned indexer. · Minimum number of compressed keys in the chunk for taking the indexer's sequence-parallel path. | The measured gain only shows up at long context (128K); 8K/32K stay unchanged. · 8192 keys = 32K tokens of context at ratio 4; below that the chunk uses the stock replicated path. | 13 Sparse attention, 38 Parallelism |
| `head_dim` | 512 = 448 NoPE + 64 RoPE (64 query heads) | Head dimension; the KV latent has the same size (kv_lora_rank = v_head_dim = head_dim). | K = V is a single 512-dim vector per token, which makes the KV an MQA over a latent. | 12 MLA & compact KV |
| `index_head_dim` | 128 | Indexer head dimension; the indexer K cache takes 132 B per row (FP8 + scale). | The indexer cache adds 21 × 132/4 ≈ 0.69 KB per token, replicated on every rank. | 13 Sparse attention, 23 Quantized KV cache |
| `index_n_heads` | 64 | Number of lightning indexer heads (only in C4A layers). | The indexer is replicated across ranks; 64 heads × all keys is the O(queries × keys) cost that motivated the SP indexer. | 13 Sparse attention |
| `index_topk` | 512 | How many C4 entries the lightning indexer selects per query; sets the width of topk_indices_buffer. | Below 512 × 4 = 2048 tokens the selection is trivial (shortcut #49486). | 13 Sparse attention |
| `kv_lora_rank` | = head_dim = 512 | Rank of the MLA KV latent. | Equal to head_dim: the cache stores the whole latent (448 FP8 + 64 bf16 + scales = 584 B). | 12 MLA & compact KV, 23 Quantized KV cache |
| `o_lora_rank` | 1024 | Rank of the grouped output projection. | Part of the wo_a contract that constrains TP=3 padding. | 12 MLA & compact KV, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `q_lora_rank` | 1024 | Rank of the low-rank query projection (wq_a → q_norm → wq_b). | Reduces the parameters and bandwidth of the Q projections. | 12 MLA & compact KV |
| `VLLM_ATTENTION_BACKEND` | not set; B12X_MLA_SPARSE selects the V3.2 backend — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Forces an attention backend; B12X_MLA_SPARSE is for the V3.2 family (656 B/token). | It is not the DSV4 backend, which uses DSV4_SPARSE_MLA_SM120. | 30 vLLM architecture, 28 Kernels |
| `VLLM_DSV4_B12X_COMPRESSED_MLA` | 0 · 0 — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Compressed-MLA experiment with b12x. · Runs sparse-MLA decode via b12x's compressed_mla_decode_forward (backend=sm120_unified). | Off; exists only in Stage-C. · Experimental alternative kernel to FlashInfer SM120. | 12 MLA & compact KV, 28 Kernels |
| `VLLM_SPARSE_INDEXER_MAX_LOGITS_MB` | 256 (stock 512); 512 tested · 256 in the compose file (stock 512) | Size of the sparse indexer's logits workspace. · Cap on the indexer's fp32 [M,N] logits tensor during prefill. | 256 = more launches per long chunk and less memory; 512 tied (−1.5% at 32K, −1.8% at 128K, within noise). · 512 was a tie (−1.5%/−1.8% on 32K/128K TTFT, within the ±2% noise), so 256 saves workspace at no measured cost. | 13 Sparse attention, 27 Unified memory |

#### Benchmark / quality

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--before / --after` | optional host-side modes | Modes of the backport scripts that capture a KV snapshot and a prompt histogram via /metrics before and after applying. | Turns each backport into a documented A/B experiment. | 45 Benchmarking, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `AB_MEM_GATE_KB` | 3000000 | MemAvailable gate the A/B requires before measuring. | Fails closed if it cannot read the value; on UMA, a knob that eats host RAM is rejected. | 45 Benchmarking, 27 Unified memory |
| `API_URL` | http://127.0.0.1:8888 | Endpoint queried by the host-side modes (/metrics). | Collects a KV snapshot and a prompt histogram. | 45 Benchmarking |
| `B12X_TRACE_MHC / VLLM_TRACE_B12X_MHC` | off; limit of 240 calls (_LIMIT) — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Logs each b12x mHC call up to the limit. | Trace for debugging mHC kernel selection. | 28 Kernels |
| `CONTAINER` | deepseek-v4-flash-vllm-dspark-1 | Container name used by the backports' host-side modes. | Makes it possible to take KV and metric snapshots before/after the patch. | 45 Benchmarking |
| `DSPARK_I144_TOKENIZER_JSON` | not set | Points to a real tokenizer.json to run the #144 shared-blocks proof with the true BPE. | Without it the test uses fixtures; with it, it measures the real BPE merge at the junction (costs 1 token). | 33 Prefix caching, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `loop_detector.py` | word 8-gram novelty per window | Tells a loop apart from a long reasoning tail by measuring 8-gram novelty per window. | A loop collapses and does not recover; a long tail keeps saying new things — it tells you when raising max_tokens helps. | 25 Measuring quality, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `RESULTS_DIR` | ../results | Directory where the host-side modes write their results. | Keeps the before/after measurements recorded alongside the repo. | 45 Benchmarking |
| `scripts/ab-boot.sh / scripts/ab-measure.sh` | ab-boot.sh LABEL KEY VAL [--long] | Edits the .env, does stop/start, applies boot gates on both ranks and a memory gate of ≥3 GB, and measures with ab-measure.sh + 5 extra c=1 trials. | c=1 noise ≈ ±10 tok/s → ≥8 trials; TTFT ±2%; reject any knob that costs ≥0.5 GB of MemAvailable or increases swap. | 45 Benchmarking |
| `scripts/bench-miaai.py` | --prompt 256 --concurrency 1 --repeat 5; thinking=false, min=max=128 tokens, ignore_eos, temp 0.6/top_p 0.95 | Measures per-stream decode with unique cold prefixes and fixed-size output. | Aug 14 matrix: c=4/6 × 32K+ sit at the ~8 tok/s floor (one decode token per serialized prefill step); the default still points to the 0731 model. | 45 Benchmarking |
| `scripts/benchmark-0731.py` | --prompt-lengths 256,2048,8192,32768,131072; --concurrency 1,2,4,6; --thinking-token-budget; --max-tokens 4096; --output | Prompt × concurrency sweep with natural completions (~512 tokens) and unique prefixes. | Produced the 0731 table: 256×c1 75.4 tok/s decode; 256×c6 191.2 aggregate; 131K×c1 TTFT 78.75 s. | 45 Benchmarking |
| `scripts/context-garble-sweep.py` | --lengths 2048,8192,32768,131072; --runs 2 | Degradation (garbling) sweep with growing context. | Gate “CLEAN up to ~900K”; the English filler measures ~1.56 tok/word (340,787 words → 533,110 tokens). | 25 Measuring quality |
| `scripts/ruler-lite.py` | --lengths 8192,32768,131072,262144; --tasks sniah,mkniah,vartrack,cwe; --request-timeout (900 s only covers ~787K prefill tokens) | Reduced RULER: single/multi-key needle, variable tracking and common words, with lengths verified via /tokenize. | Long-context quality gate (8/8 required to accept changes such as the SP indexer). | 25 Measuring quality, 45 Benchmarking |
| `scripts/run-audit.sh` | --base-url, --model deepseek-v4-flash-vision-exp, --lengths, --tool-lengths, --garble-lengths, --request-timeout | Full audit: throughput, acceptance, RULER-lite, tool battery and garble. | Expected values (08-16): C1 73–76 tok/s, C6 168–180 aggregate, acceptance ~68–75%, RULER 8/8, tool battery 7/7 — treat them as a ceiling (“best seen”). | 45 Benchmarking, 25 Measuring quality |
| `scripts/spec-acceptance.py` | --trials 5 --prompt 256 | Reads the vllm:spec_decode_num_* metrics from /metrics and computes per-position acceptance. | Pathological prompts (a repeated word) knock the curve down (pos0 0.72 → 0.11) and simulate a false ~45 tok/s regression. | 35 Speculation: fundamentals, 45 Benchmarking |
| `scripts/validate_tp3.sh` | host:port; 5 groups (identity, factual recall, reasoning, ~1.5k-token needle, degeneration) | Sanity battery for the TP3 lane. | A wrong TP shard produces fluent, wrong text with no error; expect 7/7 and the log DSv4 TP pad: heads 64 -&gt; 72 on every rank. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 25 Measuring quality |
| `scripts/verify-issue138-responses-history-live.py` | --expect-legacy rejected\|accepted | Checks live whether the legacy history format is accepted or rejected. | Confirms the effect of the #138 opt-in. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `scripts/verify-responses-api-live.py` | --base-url, --model, --output, --store-capacity 2..16, --skip-multiturn, --skip-disconnect | Strict live verifier for the Responses API (including the store and previous_response_id). | Checks the stateful contract before exposing the endpoint to clients. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `smoke-deepseek-v4-flash-dspark.sh` | CONCURRENCY=6, CHAT_URL; temperature 0 | Fires 6 short concurrent requests (reply OK and the number i). | Quick check that all 6 slots respond after boot. | 45 Benchmarking, 44 DeepSeek recipe |
| `tool-battery.py` | simple/complex/parallel/multi-turn calls and truncation | Tool-calling battery at short and deep context. | Expected 7/7 and deep-context 8/8; benchmarks that read only message.tool_calls are sensitive to DSML syntax errors; the default still points to the 0731. | 25 Measuring quality, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `VLLM_DSPARK_ITER_TIMING` | 0 — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Times the model runner's entire iteration. | Synchronizes CUDA — diagnostics only. | 45 Benchmarking |
| `VLLM_DSPARK_ITER_TIMING_LOG_EVERY` | 20 — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Interval (in steps) of the VLLM_DSPARK_ITER_TIMING logs. | Controls the profiling log volume. | 45 Benchmarking |
| `VLLM_DSPARK_STAGE_TIMING` | 0 — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Times the proposer's stages (context_prepare, prefill_main, graph_prepare, draft, postprocess, total). | Synchronizes CUDA — do not use in benchmarks. | 45 Benchmarking, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `VLLM_DSPARK_STAGE_TIMING_LOG_EVERY` | 20 — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Interval (in steps) of the VLLM_DSPARK_STAGE_TIMING logs. | Controls the profiling log volume. | 45 Benchmarking |
| `VLLM_DSPARK_TARGET_TIMING` | 0 — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Times the stages of the target's forward pass (mHC, attn, ffn, dspark_capture, hc_head). | Synchronizes CUDA — diagnostics only. | 45 Benchmarking, 15 Hyper-connections |

#### Distributed / network

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--distributed-executor-backend` | mp | Multiprocess executor (no Ray) for the workers. | Local inter-process communication uses the shared-memory MessageQueue, the target of hotfixes #79 (spin-wait) and #117 (recheck). | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `--distributed-executor-backend mp` | fixed | Multiprocessing executor (no Ray) for the workers. | Combines with --nnodes/--node-rank/--master-addr/--headless to run one container per node. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `--headless` | only if HEADLESS is non-empty (worker: NODE_RANK=1 HEADLESS=1) · worker only | The worker runs without bringing up the HTTP API. · Brings up the worker with no API server or scheduler, only running the model. | Only the head (rank 0) serves :8888; the worker's healthcheck exits 0 if $HEADLESS is set. · Separates the head/worker roles: the worker only receives the scheduler's plan and takes part in the collectives. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `--master-addr` | ${MASTER_ADDR} — required in the .env (e.g., 10.0.0.1) | TCPStore/rendezvous endpoint hosted by rank 0. | Must be the head's RoCE fabric IP; the worker waits for it — which is why the worker comes up first and the head cannot restart mid-load (#38). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `--master-port` | ${MASTER_PORT:-25000} | TCPStore rendezvous port on the head. | Same value on both nodes; default 25000. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `--nnodes` | ${NNODES:-2} (3 on TP3) | Number of nodes in the distributed group. · Number of nodes in the multi-node vLLM deployment. | The 2-node path always forces NNODES=2; start-tp3.sh exports 3. · One rank per node (TP=2) over NCCL/RoCE via ConnectX-7; the head runs API + scheduler + rank 0 and the worker runs headless. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `--node-rank` | ${NODE_RANK}: 0 head, 1 worker, 2 worker2 · 0 (head) / 1 (worker) | The node's rank in the distributed group. · Identifies the node's position in the multi-node job. | Injected by the launcher per node (env -u NODE_RANK -u HEADLESS), not by the shared .env, so the same file serves both nodes. · The head (rank 0) runs the API and scheduler and ZMQ-broadcasts the plan at every step (RTT ≈ 0.33 ms). | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `--pipeline-parallel-size` | 1 · 1 (no PP) | Turns off pipeline parallel: the whole model sits on each rank, partitioned only by TP. · Keeps pipeline parallel off: the whole model is split only by TP. | It is the recipe's documented choice: PP=1, EP is not used and DCP is not enabled for DSV4 in this image. · The _mtp_hidden_buffer buffer only exists on the last PP rank; with 2 nodes the recipe uses TP=2 and no pipeline stages. | 38 Parallelism, 44 DeepSeek recipe |
| `--tensor-parallel-size` | ${TP_SIZE:-2}; start forces 2 (or 3 via start-tp3.sh) · ${TP_SIZE:-2} (2); 3 via ./start-tp3.sh | Number of GPUs in the tensor parallel group (one GPU per node). · Splits attention heads, wo_a groups, the experts' intermediate dimension and the vocabulary across ranks (one rank per node). | The launcher ignores TP_SIZE from the .env on the 2-node path so that a copied snippet does not bring up nnodes=3 with a single worker. · With TP=2 each rank gets n_local_heads = 32 and n_local_groups = 4. TP=3 requires the padding patch because 64 heads and 8 o_groups are not divisible by 3. | 38 Parallelism, 44 DeepSeek recipe |
| `alias de loopback 10.0.0.1/32` | 10.0.0.1 routed over the CX7 (reference cluster) | Address used as MASTER_ADDR/VLLM_HOST_IP on the author's cluster. | [inferred from the inventory] a cluster convention, not mandatory; do not use this alias as NFS_SERVER_IP. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `busy_loop_s` | 1 → 0.002 (hotfix #79) | SpinCondition's spin window before sleeping on the notification socket. | In decode, messages always arrive in &lt; 1 s, so 1 s of spinning kept 3–4 P-cores at max clock; with 2 ms there is a short spin window and then the process sleeps on the socket. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 27 Unified memory |
| `DSPARK_TP3` | exported as 1 by start-tp3.sh together with TP_SIZE=3 / NNODES=3 | Selects the 3-node path in the launcher. | The 2-node path always forces TP=2/NNODES=2. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `ethtool -G rx 8192 tx 8192 (e 2ª PF em sub-rede distinta)` | rx/tx rings 8192; second PF on another subnet | Enlarges the NIC rings and puts the second PCIe function on a separate subnet. | Part of the measured network preparation; the separate subnet avoids ARP flux between the two PFs. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME` | enp1s0f1np1 (default = NCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME) | Gloo interface (CPU process groups). | On TP3 it goes to the LAN; never lo (Gloo would bind to 127.0.0.1). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `MTU 9000` | 9000 on both controller interfaces (RoCE active_mtu 4096); default 1500 limits the RoCE MTU to 1024 | Jumbo frames on the interfaces of both PFs of each QSFP port. | Pair 1 (2026-08-21): 1311 → 1343 tok/s (MTU 9000) → 1410 tok/s (two HCAs) = +7.6% end-to-end; no material change in decode. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `MTU 9000 (RoCE 4096) e anéis da NIC 8192` | MTU 9000; RoCE 4096; rings 8192; no duplicate IPs on the same subnet across PFs | Host network tuning for NCCL over RoCE on the ConnectX-7. | MTU, the PCIe Gen5 ×4 PFs (~126 Gb/s each) and pause frames affect prefill; without DMA-BUF the collectives go through a host buffer. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_CROSS_NIC` | 1 | Allows NCCL rings to cross different NICs. | Useful with multiple HCAs (the two PFs of each port). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_CUMEM_ENABLE` | 0 | Disables allocation of NCCL buffers via cuMem/VMM. | [inferred from the inventory] avoids cuMem/VMM issues on unified memory. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 27 Unified memory |
| `NCCL_DEBUG` | WARN | NCCL log level. | INFO would be needed to see the list of chosen NET/IB devices. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_GIN_ENABLE` | unset (= enabled); 0 = communicator init via CPU | Turns NCCL's GPU-initiated networking on/off. | Measured on 2026-09-05 at ~97% memory pressure: comm-init ~2 min → ~13 s with 0, bandwidth unchanged; it only speeds up the bootstrap. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_ADDR_FAMILY` | AF_INET | Makes NCCL use IPv4 GIDs. | Consistent with the launcher matching GIDs by the interface's IPv4. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_DISABLE` | 0 | Keeps the IB/RoCE transport enabled. | Complements NCCL_NET=IB; with 1 the collectives would fall back to sockets. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_GID_AUTO` | 1 (default) / 0 = manual pinning | The launcher validates each selected HCA/port in sysfs and leaves NCCL_IB_GID_INDEX unset so NCCL picks it. | The RoCEv2 GID index differs per node/HCA and changes after a reboot; a global pin could hang init (ibv_modify_qp 61) with dual-HCA. Fails closed if any member lacks a RoCE v2 GID that matches its own IPv4. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_GID_INDEX` | empty (auto); e.g. 3 / 6 with AUTO=0; WORKER_ and WORKER2_ variants | Manual pin of the GID index used by NCCL. | With AUTO=1 it is reported and ignored; the entrypoint unsets an empty value because NCCL would read empty as index 0 (link-local fe80). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_GID_MATCH_IP / WORKER_NCCL_IB_GID_MATCH_IP` | empty (uses the ifname's IPv4); e.g. 10.0.22.1 | IP used to match the ffff:aabb:ccdd suffix of the IPv4-mapped GID. | Only needed if the RoCE address is not the interface's primary IPv4. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_HCA` | e.g. rocep1s0f1; recommended dual-HCA =rocep1s0f0,roceP2p1s0f0 (^ excludes, = exact match) — required | Selects the RDMA adapters (HCAs) used by NCCL. | The GB10's QSFP port shows up as 2 virtual NICs (2 PCIe Gen5 x4 controllers, ~100G each); with a single HCA you run half a port. The launcher's parser mirrors NCCL's parseStringList (32 entries, names truncated at 63 bytes). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_MERGE_NICS` | unset (NCCL default 1 = merges dual-port NICs); 0 disables; TP3 forces 0 | Controls merging of compatible ports into a single logical NIC. | In TP3 each port goes to a different neighbor, so merging is wrong; unset means truly absent (the entrypoint unsets empty values) so as not to mask /etc/nccl.conf. Dual-HCA without merge (2026-08-06) gave +3% and cost KV buffer. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `NCCL_IB_ROCE_VERSION_NUM` | 2 | Selects RoCE v2 (routable UDP/IP). | RoCE v2 is the mode used over the ConnectX-7 with /24 IPs per link. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IB_SUBNET_AWARE_ROUTING / NCCL_IB_SUBNET_PREFIX_LEN` | unset; TP3: 1 / 24 | Pairs GIDs by subnet when building the connections. | The default /16 would treat 10.0.22.x and 10.0.23.x as the same subnet in the 3-node ring. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_IGNORE_CPU_AFFINITY` | 1 | Makes NCCL ignore the process's CPU affinity. | The inventory records no justification or measurement; pinned together with the other GB10 NCCL_* settings. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_NET` | IB | Uses the IB network plugin (verbs, includes RoCE). | Forces RDMA instead of TCP sockets. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_NET_GDR_LEVEL / NCCL_NET_GDR_READ / NCCL_DMABUF_ENABLE` | unset (passthrough); e.g. SYS / 1 / 0 | NCCL's GPUDirect RDMA and DMA-BUF controls. | On the GB10 stack (driver 580.173.02) CU_DEVICE_ATTRIBUTE_DMA_BUF_SUPPORTED=0 and the transport shows up as via NET/IB/x without /GDRDMA → the collectives go through a host buffer. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 27 Unified memory |
| `NCCL_NVLS_ENABLE` | 0 | Disables NVLink SHARP. | There is no NVSwitch on the Spark. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME` | e.g. enp1s0f1np1 — required | Interface used for NCCL's TCP bootstrap. | It is also the NFS export interface (NFS_IFACE). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `SHM_READER_RECHECK_INTERVAL_MS` | 5000 | Upper bound on how long an SHM reader waits before rereading the flag. | Limits the damage of a lost notification to 5 s instead of forever. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `TP3_BOOTSTRAP_IFNAME` | enP7s7 (RJ45 LAN 192.168.1.0/24) | LAN interface used for Gloo, NCCL-socket and TP TCP in the 3-node lane. | The CX pairs on /24 subnets cannot reach all ranks; never use lo. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `TP3_NCCL_IB_HCA` | rocep1s0f0,rocep1s0f1 | Uses both CX ports in TP3. | With a single HCA there was a timeout at QP RTR (spark1 trying 10.0.22.1→10.0.23.3). | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `TP3_PATCH_DIR` | ./patches/tp3 → /opt/dsv4-tp3 | Mounts apply_tp3_patch.py into the container. | The patch only runs if TP_SIZE=3. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `TP_SIZE` | 2; 3 applies patches/tp3/apply_tp3_patch.py | Sets the tensor parallel degree; with 3 the compose applies the TP=3 padding patch. | Should be used via ./start-tp3.sh; TP=3 gives 16 slots and 5.0 M KV tokens, but worse TTFT at long context. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 38 Parallelism |
| `TP_SOCKET_IFNAME` | enp1s0f1np1 (default = NCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME) | Interface for the TP group's TCP sockets. | In TP3 it goes to TP3_BOOTSTRAP_IFNAME (LAN). | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `VLLM_DSV4_TP_PAD` | "1" | Turns on head/group padding for non-divisible TP. | Without padding, 64 heads and 8 groups are not divisible by 3. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `VLLM_HOST_IP / WORKER_VLLM_HOST_IP` | RoCE IP of each node (defaults MASTER_ADDR / WORKER_HOST) | IP that distributed vLLM/ZMQ uses on each node. | Prevents the worker from binding to the head's mesh IP. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `watchdog NCCL (600 s) e flight recorder` | watchdog 600 s; flight recorder dumps enabled in the compose | Collective timeouts and NCCL event recording for debugging hangs. | A JIT in the middle of serving can make one rank wait past the watchdog and bring down the other; the dumps help find who hung. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `WORKER2_HOST` | e.g. 10.0.0.3 (required in TP3) | Third node (rank 2) of the TP3 lane. | Needs passwordless SSH and the image already pulled (~19 GB). | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `WORKER2_NCCL_IB_HCA / WORKER2_NCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME / WORKER2_TP_SOCKET_IFNAME / WORKER2_GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME` | e.g. rocep1s0f1 / enp1s0f1np1 | spark3's port facing the head. | Do not copy the WORKER_NCCL_* values: in the ring each node faces its neighbors through different ports. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `WORKER2_VLLM_HOST_IP / WORKER2_DIR / WORKER2_HF_CACHE` | defaults = WORKER2_HOST / WORKER_* | IP and directories of the 3rd node. | Same per-node logic as worker 1, with its own defaults. | 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `WORKER_NCCL_IB_HCA / WORKER_NCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME / WORKER_TP_SOCKET_IFNAME / WORKER_GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME` | defaults = the head's values; e.g. rocep1s0f0 / enp1s0f0np0 | Per-node HCA and interface overrides for the worker. | With a direct QSFP cable, the port facing the other node may have a different name; start injects the values into the remote compose (rank-aware). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |

#### Inference engine

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--max-cudagraph-capture-size` | ((MAX_NUM_SEQS*(MTP_NUM_TOKENS+1)+7)/8*8) → 48 at 6×7; 112 at 16×7; 40 with block-k (6×6) · ((seqs*(k+1)+7)/8)*8 = 48 at 6×6; 36 → 40 with k=5 | Largest batch (in tokens) captured in a CUDA graph; with spec decode the capture list is rounded to multiples of uniform_decode_query_len (k+1). · Largest batch size (in tokens) captured in a CUDA graph. | The raw value 42 was truncated to 40 and became [7,14,21,28,35], leaving the c=6 step (42 tokens) in eager mode; rounding up to 48 keeps [7,…,42] (c=6 aggregate 139 vs 122). · Without rounding up to a multiple of 8, 42 was truncated to 40 and the c=6 shape (42 tokens) ran eager: −12% on the c=6 aggregate (122 vs 139 tok/s). | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `DSPARK_BOOT_SHAPE_WARMUP` | 1 (default) / 0 | Runs scripts/boot-shape-warmup.sh after the smoke test. | Materializes Triton shape buckets before real traffic (#117); a failure = WARN, it never brings the boot down. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `DSPARK_WARMUP_BEARER / DSPARK_WARMUP_MAX_CONCURRENCY / DSPARK_WARMUP_TRITON_CACHE_DIR` | 1st key; MAX_NUM_SEQS; host path of the Triton cache | Authentication, scope and postcondition of the warmup, passed by the launcher. | Clusters with a key do not get 401s in the sweep; the postcondition checks the Triton cache. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `DSPARK_WARMUP_REQ_TIMEOUT` | 240 s | Per-request curl --max-time for the warmup. | The 1st boot pays for real compilations; the current script schedules 47 requests at N=6 (the .env comment still says 35). | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `formas de warmup (kernel_warmup.py)` | slot mapping 1..16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512; route-pack q_len, 12/16/20/32, 411/415/416/512/513/1024; autotune seq_lens 512 and 2048; prefill 8192 + second chunk | Shapes pre-JITed at boot. | They cover the real shapes (the chat template takes a 512 prompt to ~415 tokens) to avoid JIT and IMA on the first request. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `VLLM_DSV4_DSPARK_DEFER_TARGET_CAPTURE / VLLM_DSV4_DSPARK_DEFER_TARGET_CAPTURE_EXACT` | 0 | Defers the target's CUDA graph capture. | Off in the Stage-C override. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `VLLM_ENABLE_DEEPSEEK_V4_SPARSE_MLA_WARMUP` | 1 — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Pre-JIT of the sparse-MLA preparation and attention kernels at boot. | Closes the IMA window caused by cold JIT on SM12x. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` | 1800 (stock 300) · 1800 in the compose (stock 300) | Deadline of the sample_tokens RPC between the EngineCore and the workers. · Deadline of the sample_tokens RPC between the engine and the workers. | CuTeDSL/TileLang JIT mid-service exceeded 300 s and killed the EngineCore (#65/#87); it does not extend the 600 s NCCL watchdog. · CuTeDSL/TileLang JIT mid-service can exceed 300 s (issues #65/#87); it does not fix the #136 hang. | 30 vLLM architecture, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `VLLM_PLUGINS` | empty; gb10_hybrid_nvfp4 with the plugin · gb10_hybrid_nvfp4 when the patch is on | List of vLLM plugins to load at boot. · Selects which vllm.general_plugins plugins are loaded. | The mechanism by which an external quantization method is registered without changing the image. · It is the mechanism that registers the modelopt_gb10_hybrid quantization without editing vLLM. | 30 vLLM architecture |

| `VLLM_USE_BREAKABLE_CUDAGRAPH` | 0 (keep it; never leave it unset) · 0 (compose and overlay envs.py) | Opt-out from the “breakable CUDA graphs” that Anemll turns on automatically for DS4 when the variable is unset. · Keeps regular CUDA graphs instead of Anemll's breakable graphs. | Regular vs breakable: C1 95.9 vs 74.55 tok/s (+28.6%), C2 151.8 vs 134.2 (+13.1%); prefill 14K within noise. · If it is not set, Anemll turns on breakable graphs automatically; at 1M ctx the regular ones gave c=1 74.6 → 95.9 tok/s (+28.6%) and c=2 aggregate +13.1%. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup, 45 Benchmarking |

| `--block-size` | 256 | Tokens per paged-KV block of the MLA group; also the hash granularity of the prefix cache. · KV page size in tokens; also the hash block size of the prefix cache. | SWA groups use blocks of 64/4/8 tokens with the same page size in bytes (common page of 64 rows × 584 B = 37,376 B); prefixes are shared in 256-token blocks (issue #144). · For C4, 256/4 = 64 rows × 584 B = 37,376 B, equal to the SWA page (64 tokens × 584 B): the hybrid allocator requires pages of the same byte size across groups. The 256 | 31 PagedAttention & KV, 33 Prefix caching |
| `--kv-cache-dtype` | nvfp4_ds_mla (fixed) · nvfp4_ds_mla (default); fp8_ds_mla is byte-identical on DSV4 | Format of DeepSeek-V4's MLA KV cache. · Selects the DS-MLA KV layout (FP8 latent + bf16 RoPE + scales) used by the sparse-MLA backend. | Despite the name, it is byte-identical to fp8_ds_mla: 584 B/row = 448 B FP8 e4m3 NoPE + 128 B BF16 RoPE + 8 B (7 UE8M0 scales + 1 pad); the “nvfp4” only changes the kernel dispatch (hence bug #22). It is not the abandoned “true” 416 B layout. · On DSV4 it is byte-identical to fp8_ds_mla (584 B/token); the name only changes the kernel disp | 23 Quantized KV cache, 12 MLA & compact KV |
| `bloco de quantização do KV (64 dims)` | 64 | One UE8M0 scale every 64 NoPE dims (7 blocks per row). | Per-block granularity limits the damage from outliers while keeping only 7 bytes of scale. | 23 Quantized KV cache, 20 Quantization fundamentals |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_MXFP4_INDEXER_CACHE` | 0; requires DSPARK_ENABLE_DEEPGEMM_SM121_ALIAS=1 (start aborts) · 0; 1 relaxes the SM100 gate and passes --attention-config | Enables the MXFP4 indexer K-cache hotfix and --attention-config. · Enables the indexer K cache in MXFP4 on sm_12x. | Half the bytes read per scored key; allocation still 132 B/row. · Half the bytes read per key; risk of top-k selection drift at depth; requires DSPARK_ENABLE_DEEPGEMM_SM121_ALIAS=1. | 23 Quantized KV cache, 13 Sparse attention |
| `DSPARK_SKIP_ISSUE22_HOTFIX` | 0 (do not change) · 0; 1 skips the nvfp4_ds_mla → FP8 kernel route | 1 skips the nvfp4 → FP8 kernel dispatch fix. · With 1, hotfix #22 is not applied and nvfp4_ds_mla falls into the slow BF16 path. | It is on the “don't” list: without the fix, decode at 600K+ drops from 17.3 to 1.0 tok/s. · Without #22, decode drops to ~1 tok/s at 600K+ (vs ~17 tok/s); README.md:291 says not to do this in this recipe. | 23 Quantized KV cache, 44 DeepSeek recipe |
| `layout KV DSV4 (584 B/token)` | 448 B FP8 e4m3 NoPE + 128 B bf16 RoPE + 8 B scales (7 UE8M0 + 1 pad) | Layout of one DSV4 KV row; in the page, first all the data (576 B × tokens), then all the scales (8 B × tokens). | It is the envelope that fp8_ds_mla and nvfp4_ds_mla share; keeping it enables hybrid MLA/SWA grouping. | 23 Quantized KV cache, 12 MLA & compact KV |
