Do not set GPU_MEMORY_UTILIZATION by hand (start overrides it); if capture hits OOM, lower it to ~0.78. · Each +0.01 ≈ +1.2 GiB of KV ≈ +165 K tokens on TP=2, but in the GB10's unified memory this competes with the host (MemAvailable). | 27 Unified memory, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `_dspark_hidden_buffer` | [max_num_batched_tokens, len(target_ids) × 4096] | Buffer where the target writes, for the drafter, the average of the mHC streams of each target layer. | It grows with MNBT and with the number of target layers. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 15 Hyper-connections |
| `_mtp_hidden_buffer` | 8192 × 16384 × 2 B = 256 MiB/rank | Persistent hidden-states buffer for MTP/Eagle, sized by MNBT × hc_dim. | It is useless for DSpark (which uses _dspark_hidden_buffer); #50312 tries not to allocate it. | 15 Hyper-connections, 27 Unified memory |
| `earlyoom desligado / multi-user.target (host enxuto)` | disable earlyoom; no Xorg/gnome-shell; keep polkit/wireplumber in check | Leaves the host free of services that consume RAM or hold a GPU context. | With UMA, all host RAM comes out of the pool: at util 0.835 only ~3–5 GB remain for the OS. | 27 Unified memory |
| `GPU_MEMORY_UTILIZATION` | 0.835 | Env var that feeds --gpu-memory-utilization. | Each +0.01 ≈ +1.2 GiB of KV at TP=2, but the host loses the same amount of memory under UMA; MemAvailable ≥ ~3 GB is a boot gate. | 27 Unified memory, 44 DeepSeek recipe |
| `GPU_MEMORY_UTILIZATION_TEXT` | 0.835; alt. 0.87 (big-prompt), 0.80 (conservative), 0.78 (if capture OOMs), 0.83/0.85 (A/Bs) · 0.835 (docs/ENVS.md cites ≈ 0.80) | The .env knob from which start derives GPU_MEMORY_UTILIZATION. · Default memory utilization for the text lane in the compose file. | Raise it only after freeing host RAM (each +0.01 ≈ +165K tokens). Inconsistency: ENVS.md still says ≈0.80. · docs/ENVS.md (Recommended defaults) says ≈ 0.80, but the compose file uses 0.835 — the compose file is the source of truth. | 27 Unified memory, 31 PagedAttention & KV |
| `PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF` | expandable_segments:True | PyTorch CUDA allocator with expandable segments. | Reduces fragmentation; LMCache mode has to disable it (KV connectors reject this option). | 27 Unified memory |
| `topk_indices_buffer` | [max_num_batched_tokens, index_topk=512] | Top-k index buffer shared by all C4 layers. | The SP indexer writes the exact merge directly into it. | 13 Sparse attention |
| `VLLM_MEMORY_PROFILER_ESTIMATE_CUDAGRAPHS` | 0 | Skips estimating CUDA graph memory in the boot profiler. | [inventory inference] avoids undersizing the KV because of a conservative estimate. | 31 PagedAttention & KV, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |

#### MoE

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--moe-backend` | flashinfer_b12x | Selects the b12x MoE backend, which runs the MXFP4 experts as W4A16 on SM12x. | Maps to Mxfp4MoeBackend.B12X_MXFP4. The checkpoint format (fp4_e8m0_k32) forces W4A16, leaving the MoE prefill inefficient (an estimated +30–60% prefill with MXFP4×MXFP8 or re-quantization). | 28 Kernels, 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |
| `--moe-backend flashinfer_b12x` | fixed | Selects FlashInfer's B12X (CuTeDSL) MoE backend; the log shows Using 'B12X_MXFP4' Mxfp4 MoE backend and W4A16FusedMoeKernel. | Since the experts are fp4_e8m0_k32 (MXFP4) and b12x only has an NVFP4 tensor-core path for ModelOpt sources, it runs W4A16 (dequant → BF16 MMA): fine for memory-bound decode, inefficient for prefill. | 28 Kernels, 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |
| `bias_vl / e_score_correction_bias_vl` | Vision-Exp checkpoint tensor | Separate router score-correction bias for image tokens (aux-loss-free balancing). | The draft needs the ffn.gate.bias_vl → e_score_correction_bias_vl remap; without this routing, image tokens collapsed onto a single expert. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts, 17 Multimodal |
| `experts: 256 roteados, top-6, +1 compartilhado` | 256 / top-6 / 1 shared | Each token goes to 6 of 256 routed experts, plus 1 always-active shared expert. | The bytes read in decode depend on how many distinct experts the batch touches (≤42 per layer at c=1 with k=6) → ≈12 GB/rank/step, the largest item in the cost model. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts, 10 Prefill, decode & KV |
| `hash-routed MoE (camadas 0–2, tid2eid)` | layers 0–2 | In layers 0–2, routing is by token id via the tid2eid table, with no router GEMM. | Image tokens have no meaningful id → the vision hotfix makes image rows use bias_vl and skip the hash table (#175). | 11 Mixture-of-Experts, 17 Multimodal |
| `moe_intermediate_size` | 2048 · 2048 (2112 at TP=3) | Intermediate width of each expert. · Intermediate dimension of each expert (256 routed top-6 + 1 shared). | In MXFP4 each expert weighs ~6.7 MB per rank in the decode cost model. · At TP=3 it becomes lcm(3,64)=192 → 2112 (704/rank, a multiple of 64 for NVFP4). | 11 Mixture-of-Experts, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `norm_topk_prob` | true (config.json cfg:25) | Renormalizes the weights of the chosen top-k experts. | Same reason: the routing contract. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `num_hash_layers` | 3 (layers 0–2) | Layers that route through a tid2eid[vocab, top_k] table (hash MoE), with no router GEMM. | It needs the raw ids: that is why the vision model declares requires_raw_input_tokens = True, and image tokens need their own route (#175). | 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `routed_scaling_factor` | 1.5 (config.json cfg:55) | Scale applied to the weights of the routed experts. | Part of the router contract that the MoE kernel must honor. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `scoring_func` | sqrtsoftplus (default in the code) | Router score function. | Differs from the softmax/sigmoid of other MoEs; the kernels use RoutingMethodType.DeepseekV4. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `topk_method` | noaux_tc (with e_score_correction_bias) | Top-k routing with a load-balancing correction bias and no auxiliary loss. | The loader maps ffn.gate.bias → e_score_correction_bias; vision adds e_score_correction_bias_vl. | 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `VLLM_USE_B12X_MOE` | 1 | Turns on the B12X MoE path. · Turns on the b12x MoE. | Complements --moe-backend flashinfer_b12x (W4A16 over MXFP4 experts). · It is the backend that runs DSV4's MXFP4 experts on SM12x. | 28 Kernels, 11 Mixture-of-Experts |

#### Model architecture

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `compress_ratios` | layers 0–1 SWA only; 2–42 alternate C4A (21 layers) / C128A (20); MTP 43–45 SWA only · 0 (SWA only, layers 0–1) / 4 (C4A, 21 layers) / 128 (C128A, 20 layers) | Per-layer KV compression ratio along the sequence. · Per-layer KV compression ratio; 0 is raised to 1 and drafter layers beyond num_hidden_layers use 1. | This is what makes 1M viable: ≈3.07 KB/token in the C4 layers + 0.09 KB in the C128 layers + 0.69 KB for the indexer, plus ≈3.4 MB/seq of SWA windows, replicated on both ranks. · Sets the physical cost per token: C4 584/4 B and C128 584/128 B per layer, which is what enables 1M context. | 13 Sparse attention, 23 Quantized KV cache |
| `compress_rope_theta` | 160000 (layers with a compressor) | RoPE frequency base for the C4A/C128A layers. | Only these layers should receive YaRN ×16 (RoPE SWA fix). | 08 Position & RoPE |
| `hc_eps` | 1e-06 (config.json cfg:10) | Numerical epsilon of the hyper-connections. | Numerical stability of the mHC normalizations. | 15 Hyper-connections |
| `hc_mult` | draft residual streams (hyper-connections) · 4 (hc_dim = 16384) | Number of copies of the residual stream; the draft replicates the residual and uses HCHeadOp. · Number of hyper-connection residual streams. | Not parameterized by the recipe, but needed to read the DSpark code. · Hidden buffers become 4× larger (_mtp_hidden_buffer 8192 × 16384 × 2 B = 256 MiB) and the DSpark capture takes mean(dim=1) over the streams. | 15 Hyper-connections |
| `hc_sinkhorn_iters` | 20 (config.json cfg:12) | Iterations of the Sinkhorn normalization that constrains the mHC stream-mixing matrix. | Keeps the mixing across streams well-conditioned (manifold-constrained). | 15 Hyper-connections |
| `hidden 4096 · 64 cabeças × head_dim 512 · 43 camadas` | 4096 / 64×512 / 43 (indices 0–42) | Main decoder dimensions of DeepSeek-V4-Flash. | 64 heads are not divisible by 3 (TP3 pad); each decode step does 43 all-reduces of wo_b + 43 for the MoE. | 18 The three models, 07 Transformer block |
| `hidden_size` | 4096 | Width of the residual stream (each of the 4 mHC streams). | The b12x mHC requires 4096; the vision Aligner projects to 4096. | 07 Transformer block, 15 Hyper-connections |
| `index_n_heads / index_head_dim / index_topk` | 64 × 128, top-k 512 | Shape of the Lightning indexer: 64 index heads of 128 dims that pick the top-512 compressed positions. | It is replicated under TP and is O(queries×keys): it dominates prefill at ≥128K (≈80 TFLOP per 1024-token chunk at 900K, duplicated on both GPUs). | 13 Sparse attention |
| `num_nextn_predict_layers` | 3 (Vision-Exp); 1 (0731) | Number of extra MTP layers in the checkpoint (indices 43–45), used as the draft by DSpark. · Number of MTP/DSpark layers in the checkpoint. | Origin of the k divisibility rule (“MTP module reuse”). · In lane A it becomes n_predict, and the divisibility rule forces k=6 on Vision-Exp. | 16 N-gram & MTP, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `o_groups` | 8 · 8 (8 heads per group) | Output projection groups (BMM with r = 8 heads × 512 = 4096, then wo_b RowParallel → all-reduce). · Output projection groups: each group runs an FP8 BMM einsum(bhr,hdr-&gt;bhd) with r = 8 × 512 = 4096. | The r=4096 contract forces the TP=3 pad to add groups (8→9), not heads within a group. · 8 is not divisible by 3; the R2 padding raises it to 9 groups while keeping 8 heads per group. | 12 MLA & compact KV, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `original_max_position_embeddings` | 65536 (YaRN factor 16 → 1M) | Reference training context for YaRN. | The RoPE SWA fix uses factor=1.0 and original_max_position_embeddings = max_position_embeddings to get an identity scale. | 08 Position & RoPE |
| `q_lora_rank / o_lora_rank` | 1024 / 1024 | Ranks of the MLA's low-rank query and output projections. | Low-rank factorization reduces the parameters and bandwidth of the attention projections. | 12 MLA & compact KV, 03 Tensors & algebra |
| `RoPE dim 64 / NoPE 448 (head_dim 512)` | 64 of 512 dimensions with RoPE | Only 64 of the 512 dimensions per head are rotated; the other 448 are NoPE. | Explains the KV row: 448 B NoPE FP8 + 128 B RoPE BF16. | 08 Position & RoPE, 12 MLA & compact KV |
| `rope_scaling` | {type: yarn, factor: 16, original_max_position_embeddings: 65536} (flat); newer ones may come nested {main, compress} | The checkpoint's YaRN configuration. | In the flat format the builder applied YaRN to every layer, including SWA — which the RoPE SWA fix corrects. | 08 Position & RoPE |
| `rope_scaling {type: yarn, factor: 16}` | YaRN ×16 from 64K to 1M | Context extension via YaRN. | vLLM also applied YaRN to the SWA layers (the reference applies it only to the compressor layers) → DSPARK_ENABLE_ROPE_SWA_FIX. | 08 Position & RoPE |
| `rope_theta` | 10000 (SWA layers) | RoPE frequency base for the layers without a compressor. | Partial RoPE over 64 dims, is_neox_style=False (interleaved pairs). | 08 Position & RoPE |
| `sliding_window` | 128 · 128 (all layers) | Sliding-window attention window (layers 0–1, SWA groups and the drafter's 3 layers). · SWA window of each layer; layers 0–1 are SWA only. | Windows free old blocks by design — which broke the hybrid prefix cache (#26) and the draft state on prefix hits. · SWA costs a constant ≈ 3.4 MB per sequence (46 layers × 128 × 584 B) and the drafter also keeps 128 positions. | 13 Sparse attention, 33 Prefix caching |
| `swiglu_limit` | 10.0 (config.json cfg:58) | Clamp on the SwiGLU activation (SiluAndMulWithClamp). | b12x receives the same value as gemm1_clamp_limit; a mismatch would change the experts' numerics. | 07 Transformer block, 11 Mixture-of-Experts |
| `vocab_size` | 129280 (129408 padded at TP=3) | Vocabulary size. | 129,280 % 3 ≠ 0, so TP=3 uses padding_size = lcm(64, tp); the Markov head replicates 129,280 × 256. | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 40 TP=3 & TP=4 |
| `vocabulário 129.280 (lm_head BF16)` | 129,280 × 4096 BF16 = 1.06 GB | Size of the vocabulary and of the output head. | The lm_head (target + draft base) costs ≈1.06 GB read per decode step. | 05 Tokens & embeddings, 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |

#### Multimodal

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--limit-mm-per-prompt` | {"image":8} (converted from image=8) | Maximum number of multimodal items (images) per request. · Limits the number of images per prompt. | Anemll's argparse runs json.loads; {"image":8} in the .env would lose its quotes when sourced, and plain image=8 raised ArgumentTypeError → the entrypoint converts image=N into JSON. Before 2026-09-06 the processor capped it at 16. · image=N is converted into JSON because the Anemll image's argparse only accepts JSON; there is no video, a | 17 Multimodal |
| `COMPRESS_PAD_TO` | 4 | Aligns the start of the image block to 4-token boundaries. | Matches C4 compression (the inventory's interpretation: avoids compressed blocks that mix text and image). | 17 Multimodal, 13 Sparse attention |
| `IMAGE_TOKEN_ID` | 129264 | Id of the &lt;｜deepseek_image｜&gt; placeholder. | Every image row has the same id, so they cannot use the hash MoE (#175). | 17 Multimodal, 05 Tokens & embeddings |
| `image_url (regras de papéis)` | image_url (JPEG/PNG/GIF/WebP) only in user messages | Official restriction: images only in user messages; image/image_url/raw token in system/assistant/tool/function → 400. | A tool 400 with an image stays in the history and poisons the session (#178); a GIF becomes a static frame (no video). | 17 Multimodal, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `LIMIT_MM_PER_PROMPT` | image=8 (→ {"image":8}); image=32 tested (33 images → 400) | .env knob for the per-request image limit, in the image=N form. · Compose env converted into --limit-mm-per-prompt. | The image=N form exists because JSON loses its quotes when sourced by bash; the entrypoint converts it into JSON before vllm serve. · The image=N format is translated into JSON because Anemll's argparse only accepts JSON. | 17 Multimodal, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `vision tower (ViT 32 camadas + Aligner)` | patch 14, downsample 3, ≤384 tokens/image, vision_dim 1024, 16 heads, vision_inter_dim 2816, 2D RoPE θ=10000; placeholder &lt;｜deepseek_image｜&gt; id 129264 | A ViT that encodes 14×14 patches and an Aligner that merges 3×3 blocks (F.unfold) and projects to 4096 via a GELU MLP. | The result replaces placeholder tokens in the prompt (up to 384 per image, “N” layout with newlines and alignment padding to 4). | 17 Multimodal |
| `vision_*` | code defaults: patch 14, downsample 3, max_n_token 384, min_pixels 147456, max_wh_ratio 8, n_layers 0, dim 1024, heads 16, inter_dim 2816, rope_theta 10000 | Vision tower fields read by the Vision-Exp hotfix. | The checkpoint's real values are not in the repo (only a 32-layer ViT in the docstring); n_layers 0 turns vision off. | 17 Multimodal |

#### Operations / Docker

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--status` | verification mode of the patchers | Checks whether the hotfix is applied; for the redaction patch it does a behavioral check by running the helper. | Checking behavior, not just the presence of text, avoids a false “patch applied” positive. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DEEP_GEMM_IMPLS_DIR` | .../vllm/third_party/deep_gemm/include/deep_gemm/impls | Where the alias hotfix writes the sm121_* headers. | It is the directory that DeepGEMM's JIT uses to include the per-cc implementations. | 28 Kernels |
| `devices: /dev/infiniband` | /dev/infiniband:/dev/infiniband | Exposes the verbs devices to the container. | Enables NCCL IB/RoCE from inside the container. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `DSPARK_*_HOTFIX (caminhos dos patchers)` | each patches/*.py mounted :ro at /opt/…; patches/ at /opt/dspark-patches; patches/tp3 at /opt/dsv4-tp3; e.g. DSPARK_ISSUE27_HOTFIX | Read-only volumes that make the patchers available in the container; each path can be overridden by a variable. | Allows swapping a patcher without rebuilding the image, keeping patching inside the container before the exec. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_* (convenção dos opt-ins)` | 0 default; exactly 1 applies (fail-closed); any other value = stock | Common convention of the opt-in hotfixes (ISSUE31_GPU, ASSISTANT_FINAL, ISSUE138, CODEX, ISSUE141, SP_INDEXER, DEEPGEMM_SM121_ALIAS, ADAPTIVE_CHUNK, REPLICATE_MARKOV, ISSUE136, ISSUE191, DSPARK_BLOCK_K, ROPE_SWA_FIX, DSPARK_SWA_PREFIX, DSML_RECOVERY, MXFP4_INDEXER_CACHE, C128A_PR | Changing any patch flag requires recreating both containers (./stop + ./start): docker compose restart preserves the already-patched bytes in the writable layer. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DSPARK_RESTART_POLICY` | unless-stopped (default) / no | Compose restart policy for each rank. | After a reboot dockerd restores the ranks and start exits with code 3 (“already running”); independent restarts of a single rank can split the TP pair. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DSPARK_SKIP_HOTFIX` | 0 · 0; 1 skips the six v0.27 backports | 1 skips the 6 v0.27 backports (#50312, #49486+#52492, #48407, #48957, #50298, #44993); #22 remains applied. · With 1, skips the six vLLM 0.27 backports (#50312, #49486+#52492, #48407, #48957, #50298, #44993). | Allows isolating the effect of the backport train in an A/B. · The backports bring kernel gains (1.88×, ~2×) and the grammar fix; skipping them is only useful for A/B (scripts/bench-baseline-no-patches.sh). Changing a gate requires recreating the containers. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DSPARK_SKIP_ISSUE117_RECHECK_HOTFIX` | 0 · 0; 1 skips the vLLM #45224 backport | 1 rolls back the #45224 backport (SHM ring buffer). · With 1, does not apply the bounded recheck of the SHM ring buffer. | Diagnostics only; the backport restores the missed-notify path. · Without it, a reader can stay parked forever after missing a notification (lost wakeup). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `DSPARK_SKIP_SPIN_WAIT_HOTFIX` | 0 · 0; 1 keeps busy_loop_s=1 | 1 keeps busy_loop_s=1s in shm_broadcast. · With 1, keeps the 1 s spin window of the shared-memory IPC. | Grace P-cores would go back to spinning in the decode's IPC gaps. · With 1 s, 3–4 P-cores spin at max clock during decode (issue #79), stealing heat/power headroom from the GPU on the same SoC. | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 27 Unified memory |
| `DSPARK_STOP_GRACE` | 10s | Compose stop_grace_period. | vLLM ignores SIGTERM during load; 180 s made compose stop hang. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DSPARK_TMP_HOST` | ${HOME}/.cache/dspark-tmp → /tmp | The container's /tmp lives in a host directory. | The flight recorder's fr_dump_pipe_&lt;rank&gt;.pipe FIFO becomes visible on the host (root-owned). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `DSPARK_VLLM_IMAGE` | ghcr.io/anemll/dspark-vllm-gx10:0.1.1, pinned by digest sha256:a8394849… | Runtime image used by the vLLM service on both nodes. | The digest prevents a silent retag; every hotfix is source-exact against this image's vLLM, so the same digest must be on both nodes. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DSPARK_WORKER_HF_NFS` | 0 (default: 2nd copy on the worker) / 1 (worker mounts the head via NFSv4) | Strategy for distributing the weights to the worker. | 1 avoids the 2nd download of ~157 GiB; reuses an existing exporter (e.g., Qwen's vllm-fn-nfs) or brings up dspark-nfs. | 24 Checkpoints, 27 Unified memory |
| `entrypoint: []` | [] | Overrides the image's entrypoint with nothing. | The compose's bash command then controls the whole hotfix chain before exec vllm serve. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `gpus: all` | all | Gives the container access to the node's GPU. | Each container uses the single GB10 GPU of its node (one TP rank per node). | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `healthcheck` | curl /health on VLLM_HOST (wildcard→127.0.0.1), skipped if HEADLESS; interval 30s, timeout 10s, retries 3, start_period 480s | Health probe of the head. | The 480 s start_period covers the cold load (~6 min measured); issue #185 fixed the probe for binding to a LAN IP. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `HF_CACHE` | ${HOME}/.cache/huggingface → /cache/huggingface | Hub cache on the host, mounted at /cache/huggingface in the container. | It also holds the JIT caches (Triton, TileLang, b12x, vLLM, FlashInfer) and the NCCL-FR dumps, so they survive a container recreate. | 24 Checkpoints, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `HF_DOWNLOAD_WORKERS` | 1 (prepare); e.g. 4 | Parallel shard downloads. | 1 is safe for the disk but slow. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET` | 1 | Disables huggingface_hub's Xet transfer backend. | [inventory inference] download stability. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `HF_HUB_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT / HF_HUB_ETAG_TIMEOUT` | 120 / 30 (hf_hub default 10/10) | Hub download and ETag timeouts. | 10 s aborted multi-GB shards. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `HF_HUB_OFFLINE / TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE` | 1 / 1 when serving; prepare forces 0 | Prevents access to the Hub while serving. | Avoids a silent ~155 GB download if the revision is not in the cache (validate-dspark-config warns about it). | 24 Checkpoints |
| `HF_TOKEN / HUGGING_FACE_HUB_TOKEN` | shell &gt; .env &gt; ~/.cache/huggingface/token | Hub authentication (used only by prepare). | Required for the gated abliteration repo; since the .env is copied to the worker, prefer keeping the token in the shell. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `ipc: host` | host | Shares the host's IPC namespace with the container. | vLLM uses shared memory between processes (shm_broadcast). | 39 NCCL & RDMA, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `network_mode: host` | host | The container uses the host's network stack. | Required for RoCE, TCPStore and ZMQ directly on the physical interfaces. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `NFS_SERVER_IP` | unset = IPv4 of NCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME (e.g., 10.0.22.1) | IP of the NFS exporter on the head. | Do not use the loopback alias 10.0.0.1. | 24 Checkpoints |
| `NFS_VOLUME / NFS_CONTAINER / NFS_IMAGE / NFS_IFACE` | dspark-hf / dspark-nfs / dspark-nfs:local / NCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME | Names of the NFS volume, server container, image and interface. | ./stop … --nfs removes only dspark-nfs (never another recipe's vllm-fn-nfs). | 24 Checkpoints |
| `PATH / CUDA_HOME / LD_LIBRARY_PATH` | /usr/local/cuda/bin, /usr/local/cuda, /usr/local/cuda/lib64 | CUDA toolchain exported in the entrypoint. | Required for the JITs (DeepGEMM's nvcc, CuTeDSL, TileLang). | 28 Kernels |
| `PROJECT_NAME` | deepseek-v4-flash | Compose project name (container deepseek-v4-flash-vllm-dspark-1). | Identifies the ranks' containers; an already-existing rank makes start exit with code 3. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `pull_policy` | if_not_present | Does not pull the image if it already exists locally. | start refuses to launch if the image is missing on any node (~19 GB). | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `shm_size` | 64gb | Size of the container's /dev/shm. | Room for the broadcast/IPC buffers between vLLM processes. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `status-/logs-deepseek-v4-flash-dspark.sh` | TAIL=160, API_URL; exit 1 if any probe fails | Status of both ranks and tail of the logs. | Inconsistency: status falls back to the Stage-C image when DSPARK_VLLM_IMAGE is not set. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `TILELANG_CACHE_DIR` | /cache/huggingface/tilelang-cache | Persists TileLang's JIT cache (mHC kernels) on a volume. | Same reason: never compile in the middle of serving. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `TORCH_FR_BUFFER_SIZE` | 2000 | Size of the ProcessGroupNCCL flight recorder's ring buffer. | Prerequisite for dump-on-timeout: keeps each rank's most recent collectives. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `TORCH_FR_DUMP_TEMP_FILE` | /cache/huggingface/nccl-fr/comm_lib_trace_rank_ | Prefix of the flight recorder dump files; the entrypoint creates the directory with a non-fatal mkdir -p. | Static name per rank, truncated on every dump → archive both ranks together. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `TORCH_NCCL_DEBUG_INFO_PIPE_FILE` | /tmp/fr_dump_pipe_ | FIFO to trigger an on-demand dump (write anything to /tmp/fr_dump_pipe_&lt;rank&gt;.pipe). | It is asynchronous → wait for the line “Finished writing Flight Recorder debug info”. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `TORCH_NCCL_DUMP_ON_TIMEOUT / TORCH_NCCL_ENABLE_MONITORING` | 1 / 1 | Dumps the flight recorder when the NCCL watchdog (600 s) fires. | Leaves per-rank evidence when the pair dies (#117). | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `ulimits.memlock` | -1 (unlimited) | Removes the container's locked-memory limit. | RDMA memory registration (verbs) requires memlock. | 39 NCCL & RDMA |
| `ulimits.stack` | 67108864 (64 MiB) | Increases the thread stack size. | [inventory inference] for compilation/JIT threads. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `validate-dspark-config.sh` | renders and filters the compose command; warns if the pinned revision is not in the cache | Prints the rendered vLLM command line so you can check the flags. | Inconsistency: it shows the MAX_NUM_SEQS*(MTP+1) capture without rounding up to a multiple of 8 and echoes Stage-C knobs. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `VLLM_FLASHINFER_AUTOTUNE_CACHE_DIR` | None → VLLM_CACHE_ROOT/flashinfer_autotune_cache/&lt;ws&gt;/&lt;hash&gt;/autotune_configs.json | Directory for FlashInfer's autotune cache. | Persisting the tactics avoids re-autotuning on every boot. | 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `VLLM_GB10_PATCH_DIR` | ./vllm_patch_gb10 → /opt/vllm-gb10-hybrid-nvfp4 | Mounts the optional GB10 plugin directory into the container. | Only used with ENABLE_VLLM_GB10_PATCH=1. | 30 vLLM architecture |
| `VLLM_ROOT` | /usr/local/lib/python3.12/dist-packages/vllm | Target vLLM root for the shell patchers. | The patchers edit the installation inside the image; the fixed path matches the pinned image. | 44 DeepSeek recipe, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `WORKER2_NFS_SERVER_IP` | e.g., 10.0.23.1 | IP of the head on the spark1↔spark3 link. | worker2 is on a different /24; start adds that /24 to the export (nfs_grant_subnet). | 40 TP=3 & TP=4, 24 Checkpoints |
| `WORKER_HF_CACHE` | empty = HF_CACHE; with NFS=1 it is the worker's JIT overlay directory | Worker cache. | With NFS=1 and empty, it resolves to the worker's own $HOME/.cache/huggingface. | 24 Checkpoints |

#### Prefix caching

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--enable-prefix-caching` | always · on | Reuses KV blocks whose chained prefix hash is identical. · Reuses already-computed KV blocks, identified by a chained hash. | Big win for agents with long system prompts and tool schemas; interacts with SWA (#26/#36), with the draft state (DSpark SWA prefix) and with the effort directive (#144). · Interacts with SWA (#26 v2 + VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL), with the DSpark drafter's window (SWA prefix fix) and with the position of the effort directive (#1 | 33 Prefix caching, 44 DeepSeek recipe |
| `--enable-prompt-tokens-details` | always | Returns usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens in responses. | Lets the client check prefix cache hits (e.g., results/smoke-text-only.json shows cached_tokens: 0). | 33 Prefix caching, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--kv-transfer-config` | {"kv_connector":"LMCacheMPConnector","kv_role":"kv_both","kv_connector_extra_config":{"lmcache.mp.server_urls":"tcp://…:6667,…"}} | Configures a KV transfer connector in vLLM. | With LMCache, KV chunks go to RAM (L1) and disk (L2) on each node. | 33 Prefix caching, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_DSPARK_SWA_PREFIX` | 0 / 1 · 0; 1 recomputes the last 128 tokens on hits | Turns on the prefix cache hit limit that forces the target to recompute the draft's last window (128). · Turns on the fix that limits the prefix cache hit so the drafter's window is recomputed. | Without it, repeated identical prompts degenerate (upstream json60 12.1 → 86.5 tok/s with the fix). · Fixes truncated responses on repeated prompts (json60 upstream 12.1 → 86.5 tok/s). | 33 Prefix caching, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_ISSUE144_EFFORT_ALIGN` | 0 / 1 · 0; 1 moves the effort directive after the system block | Moves the effort directive from the start (after BOS) to the end of the system region. · Turns on hotfix #144, which repositions the effort directive after the system block. | Directives of 93 tokens (max/xhigh), 80 (high) and 0 (low/off/medium) made the hashes diverge as early as block 0; with the patch, 0 → 18/18 blocks shared on a 4646-token prompt (quality gate pending). · Stock shares 0 prefix cache blocks across effort levels; aligned shares 18/18. | 33 Prefix caching |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_LMCACHE` | exactly 1 to enable (experimental, outside the default Compose) | Enables LMCache mode (KV offload/persistence across restarts). | ~107K tokens: re-prefill ~65 s vs reload ~1.8–1.9 s (n=2); but a dead server silently hangs the engine — which is why it stays off the default path. | 33 Prefix caching |
| `LMCACHE_DISK_DIR / LMCACHE_L1_GB / LMCACHE_PORT / LMCACHE_OOM_SCORE_ADJ / LMCACHE_FORCE_REPLACE` | $HOME/lmcache-disk / 12 / 6667 / 0 / 0 | On-disk L2, CPU L1 (GB), ZMQ port, OOM killer bias and guard override for the LMCache server. | On UMA, the 12 GB of L1 come out of the same pool as the GPU's. | 33 Prefix caching, 27 Unified memory |
| `PYTHONHASHSEED` | 0 (LMCache mode) | Pins the seed of Python's hash(). | LMCache keys use hash(); without a fixed seed, different processes do not find the same chunks again. | 33 Prefix caching |
| `VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL` | 4096 (multiple of 256) | Sparsifies the prefix cache checkpoints of the SWA groups: one retained “tail” per 4096-token segment + a replay boundary. · Spacing of the retained SWA tail checkpoints for the prefix cache. | SWA groups free old blocks by design and zeroed the common hit; with #26 v2, long hits come from this retention (x8 22.8K/44.7K/88.4K warm 8/8). · It is what gives warm hits on long prefixes in #26 v2: a 21K prefix can cache ~20K, which is correct. | 33 Prefix caching, 31 PagedAttention & KV |

#### Quantization

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--quantization` | modelopt_gb10_hybrid (only with ENABLE_VLLM_GB10_PATCH=1) | Selects the quantization method registered by the GB10 plugin (hybrid Marlin/CUTLASS NVFP4 linear). | Only applies to ModelOpt NVFP4 W4A4 checkpoints; it is not DSV4's format (MXFP4 in the experts, FP8 elsewhere). | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `--quantization modelopt_gb10_hybrid` | only if ENABLE_VLLM_GB10_PATCH=1 (default off) | Enables the experimental plugin that dispatches Marlin W4A16 for M below the threshold and CUTLASS W4A4 for M above it. | Requires a ModelOpt NVFP4 W4A4 checkpoint; does not apply to the standard DSV4, whose experts are MXFP4 [inventory inference]. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 28 Kernels |
| `ENABLE_VLLM_GB10_PATCH` | 0 (default) · 0; 1 installs vllm_patch_gb10 and passes --quantization modelopt_gb10_hybrid | Turns on the GB10 hybrid NVFP4 plugin (pip install -e + --quantization modelopt_gb10_hybrid). · Installs the GB10 hybrid NVFP4 linear plugin. | Experimental and off by default; does not apply to the standard DSV4 checkpoint. · Only useful for ModelOpt NVFP4 W4A4 checkpoints — which is not the case for DSV4. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `expert_dtype` | fp4 | Type of the expert weights; selects the scale-name mapping (weight_scale vs weight_scale_inv). | The experts are MXFP4 (e2m1 + E8M0 per 32), which forces W4A16 in b12x. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 24 Checkpoints |
| `fp4_e8m0_k32 (dtype dos experts, MXFP4)` | FP4 E2M1 + E8M0 scale per block of 32 | Format of the routed experts' weights. | Defines the backend: b12x runs W4A16 for this source; there is no FP4 tensor-core path as there is for ModelOpt NVFP4. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling, 28 Kernels |
| `FP8_MAX` | 448.0 | Maximum representable value in FP8 e4m3. | Defines the 2^ceil(log2(amax/448)) scale of the KV quantization. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |
| `GB10_HYBRID_NVFP4_M_THRESHOLD` | 128 · 128 (suggestion: 64, 128, 256, 512) | The plugin's M threshold: Marlin W4A16 below it, CUTLASS W4A4 at or above it. · M below the threshold uses Marlin W4A16; M greater than or equal to it uses CUTLASS W4A4. | Separates the decode regime (small M, memory-bound) from the prefill regime (large M), which can take advantage of FP4 tensor cores. · Decode (small M) is bandwidth-bound and prefers W4A16; prefill is compute-bound and prefers FP4 tensor cores. | 28 Kernels, 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |
| `lineares FP8 em blocos 128×128` | fp8, 128×128 blocks | The model's remaining linears, in FP8 with one scale per 128×128 block. | The FP8 attention weights (some replicated) cost ≈2.4 GB/rank/step in the cost model. | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |
| `quantization_config.scale_fmt` | UE8M0 | Format of the FP8 scales, one per 128×128 block, of the linear layers. | UE8M0 is a power of 2 in 1 byte; the loader cannot convert it numerically (2⁻⁷ would become 0). | 21 FP8, FP4 & microscaling |

#### Reasoning / tools

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--default-chat-template-kwargs` | derived from DEFAULT_THINKING: off→{"thinking":false}; low/high/max→{"thinking":true,"reasoning_effort":"low\|high\|max"} | Default value of chat_template_kwargs when the client sends none. | Request kwargs always win; lets the server choose the default reasoning mode (max ≈ 3× the latency of low). | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--enable-auto-tool-choice` | fixed · on | Enables tool_choice: "auto". · Lets the model decide on its own to emit tool calls. | Required for agents that let the model decide when to call tools. · Prerequisite for the DSML parser to act; with a named/required tool_choice, the fail-closed contract of #191 kicks in. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--reasoning-config` | {"reasoning_parser":"deepseek_v4","reasoning_start_str":"&lt;think&gt;","reasoning_end_str":"&lt;/think&gt;"} | Declares the reasoning start and end markers. · Declares the parser and the reasoning start and end markers. | Used by the thinking budget and by stop suppression during reasoning. · It is used by suppress-stops, by budget #31 (requires &lt;think&gt;/&lt;/think&gt; as single tokens) and by the thinking-off fallback of #191. | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--reasoning-parser` | deepseek_v4 | Separates reasoning_content (between &lt;think&gt; and &lt;/think&gt;) from the final content. | The reasoning/answer boundary is where the grammar must advance (#44993) and where the client's stops start to apply (suppress-stops). | 34 API, tools & JSON, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `--reasoning-parser deepseek_v4` | fixed | Splits the &lt;think&gt;…&lt;/think&gt; span into reasoning_content. | Basis for suppressing stops inside the reasoning and for the thinking budget. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `--tool-call-parser` | deepseek_v4 | Parser that converts the DSML markup (&lt;｜DSML｜tool_calls&gt;, invoke, parameter) into OpenAI tool_calls. | It is the target of hotfixes #55 (truncation), #191 (fail-closed) and DSML recovery. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--tool-call-parser deepseek_v4` | fixed | Parser that converts the DSML markup (&lt;｜DSML｜tool_calls&gt; / &lt;｜DSML｜invoke&gt;) into structured tool_calls. | If a single structural token comes out wrong (temperature also applies to syntax tokens), the call turns into text; hence the DSML recovery and #191 opt-ins. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `chat_template_kwargs` | per request: {"thinking":bool,"reasoning_effort":"low\|high\|max"} | Template kwargs sent by the client to turn reasoning on and choose the effort. | They override the server default; clients should use this path instead of a generic top-level reasoning_effort (GLM-NEW-REPORT §3.4). | 34 API, tools & JSON, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `chat_template_kwargs.reasoning_effort` | low / medium / high / max / xhigh; only high and max/xhigh insert a directive | Effort level passed to the encoder, which inserts a directive right after the BOS (BOS+directive = 93 tokens at max/xhigh, 80 at high, 0 at low/off/medium). | The inline fix in the compose makes high stay high and any other value become low. Only the field inside chat_template_kwargs reaches the encoder (the top-level reasoning_effort is ignored), and different efforts shared 0 prefix-cache blocks (#144). | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DEFAULT_THINKING` | low (default); off / high / max | The server's default reasoning mode, converted into --default-chat-template-kwargs. | low opens &lt;think&gt; with no extra directive; max injects the checkpoint's directive and generates ~50,000 chars (~12.5k tokens) of reasoning on a moderate prompt, ~3× the latency of low. | 09 Generation & sampling, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_DSML_RECOVERY` | 0 / 1 · 0; 1 applies the port of vllm#52645 | Enables recovery of DSML &lt;invoke&gt; with a missing or corrupted tool_calls wrapper. · Enables recovery of DSML &lt;invoke&gt; emitted without the tool_calls wrapper. | Validates against the declared tools and only commits at &lt;/invoke&gt;; correct DSML stays byte-identical. Gate: parity vs the 42.3% C1 tool-call baseline. · Prevents malformed tool calls from leaking as text or reasoning. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_ISSUE136_XGRAMMAR_HOTFIX` | 0 / 1 · 0; 1 applies backports #52805 + #53046 | Enables the vLLM #52805 (termination) + #53046 (post-reasoning draft validation) backports in XGrammar. · Enables the XGrammar termination and post-reasoning validation backports (#136 + #210). | Speculative tokens after termination desynchronized the matcher; requires vLLM 0.25.2.dev0+g752a3a504.d20260714 and xgrammar 0.2.3. · Fixes the matcher that received tokens after terminating (FSM desync) and could hang until the 1800 s timeout. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_ISSUE191_TOOLCALL_FAILCLOSED` | 0 / 1 · 0; 1 enables the fail-closed contract | Checks, after generation, the named/required tool_choice contracts (non-streaming). · Enables the fail-closed contract for named/required tool_choice. | Violations regenerate up to RETRIES times and then become HTTP 500, instead of a wrong 200. · Turns a wrong HTTP 200 (zero tool calls or arguments outside the schema) into a regeneration and, once the attempts are exhausted, into HTTP 500. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_ISSUE31_GPU_HOTFIX` | 0 (default) / 1 · 0; 1 enables thinking_token_budget | Installs the thinking budget in GPU-resident Triton kernels. · Enables thinking_token_budget in Model Runner V2 with GPU kernels (hotfix #31 v2). | Off by default because traffic without the field may hit a “decode cliff” (#66); with the flag, the smoke test uses thinking_token_budget:1 to compile the kernels at boot. · Off by default because traffic without the field may suffer a decode cliff (issue #66); without the hotfix, the field returns HTTP 400. | 09 Generation & sampling, 44 DeepSeek recipe |
| `DSPARK_ISSUE191_TOOLCALL_MODE` | failclosed / log · failclosed (default); log | #191 mode: block violations or just log them. · log only logs a WARNING for violations instead of regenerating. | log is for measurement (145-case gate). · Lets you measure the violation rate before enabling the contract. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DSPARK_ISSUE191_TOOLCALL_RETRIES` | 2 (0–5) | Number of regenerations allowed by #191. · Number of regenerations before returning HTTP 500 when the tool call violates the contract. | Limits the latency cost of the contract layer. · Trades silently wrong answers for retries and, at the limit, an explicit error. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DSPARK_ISSUE191_TOOLCALL_THINKOFF_FALLBACK` | 1 / 0 · 1 (0 disables) | Makes the last attempt with thinking off (&lt;think&gt;→&lt;/think&gt;, reasoning_ended=True). · On the last attempt, replaces the final &lt;think&gt; of the prompt with &lt;/think&gt; and parses without reasoning. | Fixes the residual failure in which 300–500 reasoning tokens overflowed max_tokens=512. · The measured cause of the residual failure was 300–500 tokens of reasoning exhausting max_tokens=512 before the DSML call. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `DSPARK_SKIP_SUPPRESS_STOPS_HOTFIX` | 0 / 1 · 0; 1 does not apply the in-reasoning stops patch | 1 does not apply the patch that suppresses stops during reasoning. · With 1, does not apply the patch that keeps stop strings dormant until &lt;/think&gt;. | Only useful for A/B; without the patch, stops can fire inside &lt;think&gt;. · Without the patch, a client stop fires inside the reasoning and the response comes back with content: null. | 34 API, tools & JSON, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `DSPARK_SUPPRESS_STOPS_IN_REASONING` | 1 (inherits VLLM_SUPPRESS_STOPS_IN_REASONING); 0 restores stock matching | Turns the in-reasoning stop guard on or off at runtime (with the patch applied). | Lets you return to stock behavior without rebuilding the patched image. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DSPARK_SUPPRESS_STOPS_IN_REASONING (alias VLLM_SUPPRESS_STOPS_IN_REASONING)` | 1 / 0 | Keeps the client's stop strings dormant until &lt;/think&gt;. | Without it, stop:["Question:"] killed the answer inside the reasoning (content null). | 34 API, tools & JSON, 09 Generation & sampling |
| `thinking_token_budget` | request field; only accepted with DSPARK_ENABLE_ISSUE31_GPU_HOTFIX=1; 0 disables reasoning; N&gt;0 caps it · no server-side default; HTTP 400 without hotfix #31 | Limits only the reasoning part and forces a &lt;/think&gt; at the boundary. · Request field that limits how many reasoning tokens the model can generate before &lt;/think&gt; is forced. | Unlike max_tokens, it leaves budget for the answer; the stock V2 runner returns HTTP 400; without the field, no default is injected. · In Model Runner V2 it only works with hotfix #31 (GPU kernels without D2H); the gate stays off because of the decode-cliff risk (issue #66). | 09 Generation & sampling |
| `tool_choice / parallel_tool_calls / strict` | request fields (auto/none/named/required) | Control whether a tool must be called and which one, whether multiple calls are allowed and whether the arguments must follow the schema. | Under spec + async, the structural tag bitmask can fail open (vLLM family #49694/#54437); #191 checks the contract after generation. | 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `VLLM_SUPPRESS_STOPS_IN_REASONING` | source of the default of DSPARK_SUPPRESS_STOPS_IN_REASONING (1) | VLLM_* name from which the DSPARK_SUPPRESS_STOPS_IN_REASONING gate inherits its default. | Keeps compatibility with the name used by the original patch (Patch 5 from tonyd2wild/Capicua25x). | 34 API, tools & JSON |

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--async-scheduling` | present with DSPARK_ASYNC_SCHEDULING=1 (default); removed on both ranks with 0 · on (DSPARK_ASYNC_SCHEDULING=1) | The scheduler prepares step N+1 while the GPU executes step N. · The scheduler schedules step N+1 while step N is still running on the GPU. | Hides CPU/ZMQ overhead (0.33 ms RTT to the worker); without async it costs ~4% single-stream. It interacts with grammar and spec decode (placeholders for not-yet-verified tokens); the args must match across ranks. · Hides CPU overhead but creates -1 draft placeholders; relevant to #136/#191/#44993 and to the resizing via draft_token_lengt | 32 Scheduler & batching, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `--enable-chunked-prefill` | always · on | Splits long prefills into chunks that are mixed with decodes in the same step. · Processes prefills in pieces mixed with decodes in the same step. | It is the basis of fairness control (#27/#43), together with --long-prefill-token-threshold and the cap on in-flight prefills. · Produces mixed prefill+decode batches, which become ragged batches in DSpark (Patch 2/2b). Disabling it would require max_num_batched_tokens ≥ max_model_len, which is infeasible at 1M. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `--long-prefill-token-threshold` | ${LONG_PREFILL_TOKEN_THRESHOLD:-1024}; 0 = no cap; 2048 tested and rejected · 1024 (0 disables; 2048 tested) | Maximum size of each chunk of a long prefill. · Limits the number of tokens in each chunk of a long prefill. | With 1024, a chunk stays well below the 8192 budget and decodes always get budget (issue #27). 2048 cost ~1.5 GB of host RAM on the head node (MemAvailable 2.55 → 0.99 GB) because larger workspaces live in unified memory, outside the --gpu-memory-utilization budget. · Prevents a prefill from consuming the whole step and killing decode (to | 32 Scheduler & batching, 27 Unified memory |
| `--max-num-batched-tokens` | ${MAX_NUM_BATCHED_TOKENS:-8192}; alt. 16384 (big-prompt), 12288 (with N=32) | Scheduler token budget per step (decodes + prefill chunks). · Scheduler token budget per step (prefill + decode). | With k=6 each decode consumes k+1 positions; with high admission, the draft slots eat up the budget (warning max_num_scheduled_tokens is set to …). · Sizes persistent buffers: topk_indices_buffer, _mtp_hidden_buffer (256 MiB/rank) and _dspark_hidden_buffer; with #50298 the workspace also includes max_num_batched_tokens × combined_topk × 4 | 32 Scheduler & batching, 27 Unified memory |
| `--max-num-partial-prefills` | rejected by Anemll 0.1.1 | Stock vLLM flag to limit concurrent partial prefills. | vLLM 0.25.2 defines max_num_partial_prefills but the v1 admission loop never reads it, and the Anemll image rejects the flag → the recipe uses DSPARK_MAX_INFLIGHT_PREFILLS + hotfix #27. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 30 vLLM architecture |
| `--max-num-seqs` | ${MAX_NUM_SEQS:-6}; alt. 4 (big-prompt), 16 (Stage-C 200K / TP3), 32 (data, not a recommendation) · 6 (1M/6 profile); 16 in Stage-C 200K/16 and at TP=3 | Maximum number of simultaneously active sequences in continuous batching. · Maximum number of simultaneously active sequences in the batch. | It also defines the CUDA graph capture sizes and the “verify rows” = seqs × (k+1); the stochastic hangs #141 seem to grow with the verification rows. · Defines the rows of the DSpark drafter's ring buffer and the CUDA graph capture size (seqs × (k+1)). More slots raise the aggregate but split the KV pool across streams. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 29 CUDA graphs & warmup |
| `DSPARK_ASYNC_SCHEDULING` | 1 (default) / 0 (only for the #191 A/B) · 1 (default); 0 removes --async-scheduling; any other value → error | Recipe switch that includes or removes --async-scheduling in the rendered command line. · Decides whether the compose passes --async-scheduling to vLLM. | 0 is for testing whether the grammar bitmask flips because of real drafts (issue #191); it costs ~4% single-stream. · Exists for the single-variable A/B of #191 (isolating the trigger in the engine); disabling it costs decode throughput. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 34 API, tools & JSON |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_ADAPTIVE_CHUNK` | 0 / 1 (rejected) · 0 (rejected) | chunk=1024 only when there are active decodes; otherwise uses the whole budget. · Uses the 1024 chunk cap only when there is an active decode; with no decode, the chunk goes up to max_num_batched_tokens. | Rejected: drops the head node's MemAvailable to 2.36/2.28 GB (vs 3.8–5.2 GB). · Rejected: the head node's MemAvailable fell to 2.28–2.36 GB because the larger workspace lives on the host, outside the gpu_memory_utilization budget. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 27 Unified memory |
| `DSPARK_ISSUE43_SCHED_DIAG` | 0 / 1 · 0; 1 logs one line per step | 1 prints one compact line per scheduler step (zero-token skips and tokens per request). · With 1, logs [issue43-step N] run= prefill_toks= decode_toks= decode_skips= and [issue27-adm] at every step. | Starvation/fairness diagnostics without changing the scheduling. · Scheduler diagnostics at ~0 cost when disabled. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 45 Benchmarking |
| `DSPARK_MAX_INFLIGHT_PREFILLS` | 1 (default); 2 (qualified opt-in #217); 3 (limited); &gt;3 → 3; invalid → stock max_num_partial_prefills with 1 warning · 1 (1–3; &gt;3 clamped to 3; invalid → max_num_partial_pref | Maximum number of concurrent partial prefills, read by hotfix #27 when the Scheduler is constructed. · Maximum number of in-flight partial prefills when admitting new requests (hotfix #27). | The default swung 2 → 1 (#154) → 2 → 1 (#211). At 1: gate26 1.60–1.76×, 0 preemptions; at 2 (post-r3, #217): 4×8K gate26 1.50–1.57× and TTFT spread 2.1–2.4× vs 4.1×, but 4×32K gets worse (3.0–3.3× vs 2.1×). · 1 serializes (fairness spread 1.60–1.76×); 2 gives a spread of 1.50–1.57× and a TTFT spread of 2.1–2.4× vs 4.1× (issue #217), with  | 32 Scheduler & batching, 44 DeepSeek recipe |
| `LONG_PREFILL_TOKEN_THRESHOLD` | 1024 | Launcher env var converted into --long-prefill-token-threshold. | Chunk cap that protects decode; 2048 cost ~1.5 GB of host RAM. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `MAX_NUM_BATCHED_TOKENS` | 8192; alt. 16384, 12288 | .env knob that feeds --max-num-batched-tokens. | Raising the value did not eliminate the 8150/8168 budget warning. | 32 Scheduler & batching |
| `MAX_NUM_SEQS` | 6; alt. 4, 16, 32, 13 (one operator in production, no proof of safety) | .env knob that feeds --max-num-seqs. | The .env itself warns that no tested value is guaranteed safe: N=32/c=32 (224 verification rows at k=6) died in bursts; nothing above 64 rows is established as clean; the default gives 48 rows. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `perfil 1M/6` | max-model-len 1048576 × max-num-seqs 6 (default) | Default serving profile: 1M of context per request and 6 slots. | Keys mask microbench (lane B, 2026-06-29): ~182 tok/s aggregate (6/6), solo ~67; on lane A the KV pool is 2.22× at 1M at TP=2. | 32 Scheduler & batching, 44 DeepSeek recipe |

#### Speculative decoding

| Name | Value | What it does | Why it matters | Study in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--speculative-config` | {"method":"dspark","num_speculative_tokens":${MTP_NUM_TOKENS:-6},"draft_sample_method":"${DRAFT_SAMPLE_METHOD}"} · {"method":"dspark","num_speculative_tokens":6,"draft_sample_metho | Enables DSpark speculative decoding with k speculative tokens. · Enables speculative decoding with the DSpark block drafter embedded in the checkpoint. | k must be ≥ dspark_block_size (5) and divisible by num_nextn_predict_layers (3) → 6, 9…; the JSON is built inside the container because .env values are sourced by bash and would lose their quotes. · k=6 is forced by the divisibility rule with n_predict=3 (Vision-Exp), even though the trained block is 5 (see hotfix block-k). In the TP=3 pr | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |
| `DRAFT_SAMPLE_METHOD` | probabilistic (default) / greedy · probabilistic (default); greedy | Defines how the draft samples and how verification accepts tokens. · Compose env var, validated at boot, that becomes the draft_sample_method field of SPECULATIVE_CONFIG. | Probabilistic preserves the target distribution exactly via rejection sampling; in the 2026-09-02 A/B greedy tied (56.5 vs 56.6 tok/s c=1) → probabilistic was kept. Any other value → exit 2 before the JSON is built. · Enables the greedy vs probabilistic A/B without editing JSON; the measured result was a tie and the default stayed probabi | 35 Speculation: fundamentals, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `draft_sample_method` | probabilistic (default); greedy | Defines whether the draft is sampled (probabilistic, using the full logits in the ratio test) or chosen by argmax (greedy, one-hot q). | Probabilistic preserves the target distribution exactly via rejection sampling, but costs memory for the full logits; greedy tied in the A/B and the default stayed probabilistic. | 35 Speculation: fundamentals, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `dspark_block_size` | 5 (checkpoint) | Size of the trained draft block: anchor token + block_size−1 noise positions processed in one parallel pass. · Width of the parallel draft block (anchor + 4 noise positions). | It is the lower bound for k: k&lt;5 truncates blocks. · In the overlay it becomes n_predict; it is the trained block, so k=5 is the natural choice (block-k). | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_DSPARK_BLOCK_K` | 0 / 1 · 0; 1 exempts dspark from the k % n_predict rule | Enables the hotfix that removes the divisibility rule for method=dspark. · Enables the block-k hotfix, which allows k=5 (the trained block) on Vision-Exp. | Allows k=5 (the trained block) instead of k=6 (one extra noise query): +2–10% single-stream; the 6×6 capture becomes 40. · Measured 8K greedy decode 56.6 vs 55.3 tok/s with the same per-position acceptance. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `DSPARK_ENABLE_REPLICATE_MARKOV` | 0 (default) / 1 · 0 (OFF after the A/B) | Enables the hotfix that replicates the Markov head instead of sharding it across TP. · Replicates markov_w1/markov_w2 (66 MB each) instead of sharding them, eliminating 12 collectives per step. | Removes 12 serialized collectives per step, but the A/B showed unchanged acceptance, no gain at c=1 and c=6 aggregate 122 vs 148 → off. · Correct (same acceptance), but the c=6 aggregate dropped 148 → 122 tok/s and the KV lost 0.44 GiB → OFF. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 38 Parallelism |
| `dspark_markov_rank` | 256 | Rank of the Markov head (low-rank bigram): markov_w1 (vocab→256) and markov_w2 (256→vocab). · Rank of the Markov head and dimension of the Markov embedding that feeds the confidence head. | Sequentially refines each block position; each matrix is ~66 MB (129,280 × 256 × 2 B) and w2 applied 6× costs ≈0.2 GB/step in the cost model. · With a 129,280 vocabulary each matrix is 66 MB in bf16 — the cost that motivated the replication test. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 03 Tensors & algebra |
| `dspark_noise_token_id` | 128799 (config.json cfg:68) | Token that fills the positions after the anchor in the parallel draft pass. · Noise token that fills positions 1..k−1 of the block, the padding and the warmup step. | With k=6 there is one extra noise query beyond the trained block — the cost that block-k eliminates. · The drafter is trained to predict these positions from the noise, in parallel. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `dspark_num_draft_layers` | overlay: len(dspark_target_layer_ids) (≥ 1); Vision-Exp = 3 stages | Number of drafter stages (mtp.0..N-1 in the checkpoint). | infer_dspark_num_draft_layers reconstructs the number from the weight names because the HF config may say num_nextn_predict_layers=1. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `DSPARK_PROPOSER_FILE` | recipe/vllm/v1/spec_decode/dspark_proposer.py | DSpark proposer that start requires and copies to the worker. | Inconsistency: the Anemll compose does not mount it (the comment says “Optional Stage-C overlay”), so it is copied but not used in the default lane. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 41 Recipe engineering |
| `DSPARK_SLOT_CLAMP` | 1 · "1"; 0 = only detects and logs DSPARK_STALE_SLOT_INDEX — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Draft KV slot clamp for long context. · Clamps an out-of-range slot_index before the gather from the drafter's ring buffer. | Stage-C knob that validate-dspark-config.sh still echoes (inconsistency). · A stale slot triggered a device assert and killed the worker; since it is only a draft, a wrong slot produces a bad draft that the target rejects, without corrupting the output. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `dspark_target_layer_ids` | values are not in the repo | Target layers whose outputs feed the drafter; main_proj receives hidden × len(ids). | They must be strictly increasing in {-1} ∪ [0, L−1]; they enter the spec config's compute_hash because they change the target graph. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 36 MTP, EAGLE & reduced vocab |
| `MTP_NUM_TOKENS` | 6 (Vision-Exp); history 3 → 5 (0731, 2026-07-29) → 6 (2026-08-31); 5 with block-k · 6 (Vision-Exp); 5 recommended with block-k (validation relaxed to ≥ 1) | k = DSpark's num_speculative_tokens. · Launcher env var that sets DSpark's num_speculative_tokens. | Rule: k ≥ dspark_block_size (5) (k&lt;5 truncates blocks: silently in 0.25.2, rejected in ≥0.26) and k % num_nextn_predict_layers == 0 (“MTP module reuse”). The official cards use k=7 + greedy, which fails the divisibility rule; acceptance of 0.28/0.21 at positions 5–6 → k&gt;6 does not pay off. · With the block-k hotfix the launcher acce | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |
| `num_speculative_tokens` | 6 (Vision-Exp default); 5 with block-k; 0731 used 5 | Number k of draft tokens proposed and verified per step; each request becomes 1+k rows in the verify. | Per-position acceptance drops fast (Vision-Exp k=6: 0.89/0.73/0.49/0.34/0.23/0.15). k=5 keeps the same acceptance and makes the step cheaper (8K greedy decode 56.6 vs 55.3 tok/s; 32K 56.6 vs 53.6). | 35 Speculation: fundamentals, 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `parallel_drafting` | True for dflash/dspark (lane A) | Anemll flag that marks drafters that propose the whole block in parallel. | Distinguishes DFlash/DSpark from classic MTP, which reruns one layer per position. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |

| `VLLM_DSPARK_CONFIDENCE_SCHEDULER` | off (overlay default auto) · auto (off / threshold / hardware) — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Confidence scheduler: picks the draft length from the confidence and the hardware curve. · Strategy for choosing the verified length per request. | Stage-C knob; a no-op in Anemll 0.1.1. · auto = threshold if the threshold is &gt; 0, otherwise off; hardware uses the engine's cost curve. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `VLLM_DSPARK_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD` | 0.0 · "0.0" (off) — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Draft confidence threshold (Stage-C). · Cumulative survival threshold used to cut the verified draft prefix. | A no-op in Anemll 0.1.1. · 0 verifies all k tokens; &gt;0 shortens the verify when the confidence head predicts rejection. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 35 Speculation: fundamentals |

| `VLLM_DSPARK_GPU_REJECTED_CONTEXT_MASK` | 1 · 0 (Stage-C: 1) — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Rejected-context mask computed on the GPU (Keys' ragged path). · Masks the rejected rows on the GPU when writing the drafter's ring buffer, without syncing rejection counts to the CPU; it is the ragged path (Patch 2/2b). | Required for a correct 200K/16 in Stage-C. · Required for a correct max_num_seqs&gt;1 with staggered arrivals (PATCHES.md:107-109). | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 26 Inside the GPU |
| `VLLM_DSPARK_HARDWARE_SCHEDULER_EARLY_STOP` | 1 · "1"; 0 = exhaustive sweep — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Early stop for the draft's hardware-aware scheduler. · Stops the greedy sweep at the first drop in estimated tok/s. | Stage-C knob; a no-op in Anemll 0.1.1. · 0 does an exhaustive sweep, useful for curves with cliffs. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark |
| `VLLM_DSPARK_LOCAL_ARGMAX` | 1 · 0 (Stage-C: 1) — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Local (vocab-parallel) argmax in the draft. · Does a local vocab-parallel argmax and only exchanges (value, index) pairs between ranks, avoiding an all-gather of the draft logits. | Avoids gathering the whole vocabulary across ranks for each draft position. · Experimental: may add per-position synchronization. | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 38 Parallelism |

| `VLLM_DSPARK_REPLICATE_MARKOV_W1` | 1 · 0 (Stage-C: 1) — lane B only (Stage-C overlay) | Replicates the Markov head's markov_w1 (Stage-C). · Turns markov_w1 into a replicated nn.Embedding (no all-reduce of the embedding). | Stage-C equivalent of the idea of replicating the head to cut collectives. · Lane A equivalent: hotfix-dsv4-replicate-markov-head.py (measured worse and left OFF). | 37 DFlash2 & DSpark, 38 Parallelism |
